
===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/regression-overview.tsx =====
import type { BaselineRegressionRun, BaselineStepProgress, BaselineFieldMetrics, BaselineRegressionItem } from "@/lib/types"
import { SelectionFact, dimensionSelectionName } from "./regression-results"
import { modelExecutionSummary } from "./real-model-execution"
import { Card } from "@/components/ui/card"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { BaselinePerformanceSummary } from "./baseline-performance-summary"
import { baselineRunMetricsView } from "./baseline-run-metrics"
import { percent } from "./regression-page-shared"
import { workflowSelectionFacts } from "./workflow-selection-facts"

const STEP_DISPLAY_NAMES: Record<string, string> = {
  upstream: "上游预处理",
  prompt_a: "调用 A",
  prompt_b: "调用 B",
  downstream: "结果写回",
}

function stepDisplayName(key: string): string {
  if (STEP_DISPLAY_NAMES[key]) return STEP_DISPLAY_NAMES[key]
  const base = key.replace(/_\d+$/, "")
  const named: Record<string, string> = {
    eligibility_redline: "红线准入",
    track_routing: "赛道路由",
    media_adjustment: "媒介调整",
    quality_cap_exception: "质量封顶豁免",
    dimension_scoring: "维度评分",
    anchor_calibration: "锚点校准",
    score_level_matcher: "分数等级撮合",
  }
  return named[base] ?? key
}

export function RunSelectionSummary({ run }: { run: BaselineRegressionRun }) {
  const workflowFacts = run.selection.workflow
    ? workflowSelectionFacts(run.selection.workflow)
    : null
  return (
      <div>
        <p className="text-sm font-bold">本轮配置与版本</p>
      <section className="mt-3 grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-2">
        {workflowFacts ? (
          <>
            <SelectionFact label="本轮完整工作流" value={workflowFacts.workflow} />
            <SelectionFact label="提示词节点与版本" value={workflowFacts.prompts} />
            <SelectionFact label="规则模块与版本" value={workflowFacts.rules} />
          </>
        ) : (
          <>
            <SelectionFact
              label="本轮调用 A"
              value={run.selection.prompt_a
                ? `${run.selection.prompt_a.version} · ${run.selection.prompt_a.name}`
                : "历史 run 未记录"}
            />
            <SelectionFact
              label="本轮调用 B"
              value={run.selection.prompt_b
                ? `${run.selection.prompt_b.version} · ${run.selection.prompt_b.name}`
                : run.selection.prompt_a
                  ? "单提示词模式（B 位不调用）"
                  : "历史 run 未记录"}
            />
            <SelectionFact
              label="本轮维度版本"
              value={dimensionSelectionName(run.selection.dimension)}
            />
          </>
        )}
        <SelectionFact
          label="结果判定方式"
          value={run.selection.execution_mode === "structured" ? "标准评分合同" : "自由实验 · 无结构也可完成"}
        />
        <SelectionFact
          label="模型执行"
          value={run.selection.model_execution_mode === "real"
            ? `${modelExecutionSummary("real").label} · 最多 ${run.selection.real_execution_budget?.max_requests ?? "—"} 次`
            : modelExecutionSummary("simulated").label}
        />
      </section>
      </div>
  )
}

export function RunStepProgress({ run, stepProgress }: { run: BaselineRegressionRun; stepProgress?: BaselineStepProgress | null }) {
  return (
    <>
      {run.status === "running" && (
        <div className="mt-4 border border-alias-edge-035 bg-alias-surface-044 px-4 py-3 rounded-md">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 text-xs font-semibold">
            <span>
              回归运行中，页面每 3 秒自动刷新
              {stepProgress?.active_steps.length ? (
                <span className="ml-2 font-normal text-[var(--muted)]">
                  当前步骤：{stepProgress.active_steps.map((key) => stepDisplayName(key)).join("、")}
                </span>
              ) : null}
            </span>
            <span className="font-data">
              {stepProgress
                ? `步骤 ${stepProgress.done_steps}/${stepProgress.total_steps} · 整条完成 ${run.completed}/${run.total}`
                : `${run.completed}/${run.total}`}
            </span>
          </div>
          {/* 主进度按步骤完成率：11 步流水线按「整条走完」计数时，前 80% 时间进度
              都趴在 0，最后十分钟突然冲满，运营会误判为卡死（run136 实况）。 */}
          <div className="mt-2 h-1.5 overflow-hidden bg-white">
            <div
              className="h-full w-full origin-left bg-brand transition-transform duration-[var(--visual-ease-out-ms)] ease-[var(--visual-ease-out)]"
              style={{ transform: `scaleX(${(stepProgress && stepProgress.total_steps
                ? Math.round(stepProgress.done_steps / stepProgress.total_steps * 100)
                : run.total ? Math.round(run.completed / run.total * 100) : 0) / 100})` }}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  )
}

export function RegressionDashboard({ run, items, fieldMetrics, onOpenMetrics }: {
  run: BaselineRegressionRun
  items: BaselineRegressionItem[]
  fieldMetrics?: BaselineFieldMetrics
  onOpenMetrics: () => void
}) {
  const metrics = run.metrics
  const metricsView = baselineRunMetricsView(metrics)
  const hasImageMetrics = "image_grade_metrics" in metrics && !!metrics.image_grade_metrics
  const primaryAccuracy = metricsView.kind === "structured_fields"
    ? metricsView.fieldAggregate?.accuracy
    : fieldMetrics?.aggregates.macro.accuracy ?? metricsView.levelMetrics?.exact_accuracy
  const primaryRecall = metricsView.kind === "structured_fields"
    ? metricsView.fieldAggregate?.recall
    : fieldMetrics?.aggregates.macro.recall
  const availableDeviationIds = items
    .filter(item => item.status === "completed" && item.deviation && item.optimization_case_id === null)
    .map(item => item.id)
  return (
    <Card data-testid="baseline-dashboard">
      <BaselinePerformanceSummary metrics={metrics} actions={<Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={onOpenMetrics}>查看字段证据</Button>} />
      <section className="mt-6 grid items-stretch gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
        {!hasImageMetrics && run.category_key !== "knowledge_graph" && <Metric label={metricsView.kind === "structured_fields" ? "字段整体准确率" : "字段宏平均准确率"} value={primaryAccuracy === undefined ? "—" : percent(primaryAccuracy)} />}
        {!hasImageMetrics && run.category_key !== "knowledge_graph" && <Metric label={metricsView.kind === "structured_fields" ? "字段整体召回率" : "字段宏平均召回率"} value={primaryRecall === undefined ? "—" : percent(primaryRecall)} />}
        <Metric label="人工门禁状态" value={run.status === "running" ? "等待运行完成" : metricsView.failed ? "先处理失败" : "等待人工确认"} />
        <Metric label="下一步" value={availableDeviationIds.length ? `纠偏 ${availableDeviationIds.length} 条` : "查看证据并决定"} />
      </section>
    </Card>
  )
}

function Metric({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="baseline-metric min-w-0 rounded-md border border-line bg-surface-sunken px-5 py-4">
      <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</p>
      <p className="font-data mt-2 text-xl font-bold break-words">{value}</p>
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/regression-page-shared.tsx =====
// 从 baseline-regression-page.tsx 抽出的页内共享件。
// 归属按依赖闭包判定：被两个以上抽取模块用到才放这里；只被一个模块用到的
// 辅助随该模块迁走，避免这里变成杂物间。
import type { BaselineLevel } from "@/lib/types"

export const levels: BaselineLevel[] = ["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"]

export function Metric({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="bg-white px-5 py-4">
      <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</p>
      <p className="font-data mt-2 text-2xl font-bold">{value}</p>
    </div>
  )
}

export function percent(value: number) {
  return `${Math.round(value * 1000) / 10}%`
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/regression-results.tsx =====
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react"
import { Check, CheckSquare, File, PencilSimple, Square, WarningCircle } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { toast } from "sonner"

import { Card } from "@/components/ui/card"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { ImagePreviewButton, type ImagePreview } from "@/components/image-lightbox"
import { api, baselineRegressionApi, jsonBody } from "@/lib/api"
import { can } from "@/lib/access"
import { submitReviewDecision } from "@/lib/review-submit"
import type { BaselineStepProgress } from "@/lib/types"
import type { BaselineFieldMetrics, BaselineRegressionItem, BaselineRegressionRun, ReviewCorrection, User } from "@/lib/types"
import { ReviewCorrectionForm } from "@/pages/review-correction-form"
import { CorrectionTable } from "./correction-table"
import { CorrectionPagination } from "./correction-pagination"
import { CorrectionWorkbench } from "@/features/baseline-regression/correction-workbench"
import {
  correctionDraftFromView,
  correctionDraftHasChanges,
  correctionSubmitBlocker,
  correctionPaidRerunNodeKeys,
  correctionSaveTargetIsCurrent,
  correctionSubmissionPayload,
  mergeCorrectionResponse,
  updateCorrectionDraft,
  type CorrectionSaveTarget,
} from "@/features/correction-contract/correction-view-state"
import type { CorrectionDraft, CorrectionView } from "@/features/correction-contract/types"
import { correctionNodeEditable } from "@/features/correction-contract/contract-renderer"
import { nativeEvaluationPresentation } from "@/features/correction-contract/native-evaluation-presentation"
import { EvaluationDetailPanel } from "@/features/evaluation-detail/evaluation-detail-panel"
import { fieldSpecsFromContractNodes, showsLegacyEvaluationDetail } from "@/features/evaluation-detail/detail-model"
import type { RowCorrectionSubmit } from "@/features/evaluation-detail/row-correction-dialog"
import {
  correctionQueueItems,
  nextPendingCorrectionId,
  previousCorrectionId,
  runStructuredCorrectionApproval,
} from "@/features/baseline-regression/correction-navigation"
import { baselineAcceptanceProgress } from "@/features/baseline-regression/baseline-regression-contract"
import { BaselinePerformanceSummary } from "@/features/baseline-regression/baseline-performance-summary"
import { baselineRunMetricsView } from "@/features/baseline-regression/baseline-run-metrics"
import { modelExecutionSummary } from "@/features/baseline-regression/real-model-execution"
import { CorrectionAnalysisPanel } from "@/features/baseline-regression/correction-analysis-panel"

import { humanNodeCorrectionCount } from "@/features/baseline-regression/correction-level-display"
import { LevelExplanation, levelExplanationSummary } from "@/features/baseline-regression/level-explanation"

  correctionDetailMode,
  structuredCorrectionPrimaryAction,
} from "@/features/baseline-regression/correction-presentation"
import { Metric, percent } from "@/features/baseline-regression/regression-page-shared"
import { structuredFieldLabel, structuredTruthRunSummary, structuredValuesMatch } from "@/features/baseline-regression/structured-truth"

} from "@/features/baseline-regression/truth-revision-panel"


const STEP_DISPLAY_NAMES: Record<string, string> = {
  upstream: "上游预处理",
  prompt_a: "调用 A",
  prompt_b: "调用 B",
  downstream: "结果写回",
}

function stepDisplayName(key: string): string {
  if (STEP_DISPLAY_NAMES[key]) return STEP_DISPLAY_NAMES[key]
  const base = key.replace(/_\d+$/, "")
  const named: Record<string, string> = {
    eligibility_redline: "红线准入",
    track_routing: "赛道路由",
    media_adjustment: "媒介调整",
    quality_cap_exception: "质量封顶豁免",
    dimension_scoring: "维度评分",
    anchor_calibration: "锚点校准",
    score_level_matcher: "分数等级撮合",
  }
  return named[base] ?? key
}

export function correctionIdempotencyKey(runId: number, itemId: number): string {
  const random = typeof globalThis.crypto?.randomUUID === "function"
    ? globalThis.crypto.randomUUID()
    : `${Date.now().toString(36)}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}`
  return `baseline-contract:${runId}:${itemId}:${random}`
}

export function RegressionResults({
  run,
  stepProgress,
  items,
  fullRunItems,
  pagination,
  page,
  onPageChange,
  onPageSizeChange,
  loading,
  fieldMetrics,
  onPreview,
  onOpenMetrics,
  correctionItemId,
  onOpenCorrection,
  onCloseCorrection,
}: {
  run: BaselineRegressionRun
  stepProgress?: BaselineStepProgress | null
  items: BaselineRegressionItem[]
  fullRunItems?: BaselineRegressionItem[]
  pagination: { offset: number; limit: number; total: number }
  page: number
  onPageChange: (page: number) => void
  onPageSizeChange: (size: number) => void
  loading: boolean
  fieldMetrics?: BaselineFieldMetrics
  onPreview: (preview: ImagePreview) => void
  onOpenMetrics: () => void
  correctionItemId: number
  onOpenCorrection: (itemId: number) => void
  onCloseCorrection: () => void
}) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const me = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["me"],
    queryFn: () => api<User>("/api/auth/me"),
  })
  const availableDeviationIds = useMemo(
    () => items
      .filter((item) => (
        item.status === "completed"
        && item.deviation
        && item.optimization_case_id === null
      ))
      .map((item) => item.id),
    [items],
  )
  const canReview = can(me.data, "reviews:write", run.category_key)
  const canDevelop = can(me.data, "drafts:write")
  const [selectedDeviationIds, setSelectedDeviationIds] = useState<Set<number>>(
    new Set(),
  )
  const [activeView, setActiveView] = useState<"results" | "correction" | "table">("results")
  const [, setTableDirty] = useState(false)
  const tableTop = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const selectView = (next: "results" | "correction" | "table") => {
    if (next !== "table") setTableDirty(false)
    setActiveView(next)
  }

  const [reviewNotes, setReviewNotes] = useState<Record<number, string>>({})
  const [reopenSeeds, setReopenSeeds] = useState<Record<number, {
    corrections: ReviewCorrection[]
    note: string
  }>>({})
  const correctionItems = useMemo(
    () => correctionQueueItems(fullRunItems?.length ? fullRunItems : items),
    [fullRunItems, items],
  )
  const correctionProgress = useMemo(
    () => baselineAcceptanceProgress(fullRunItems?.length ? fullRunItems : items, run.status !== "running"),
    [fullRunItems, items, run.status],
  )

  useEffect(() => {
    setSelectedDeviationIds(new Set(availableDeviationIds))
  }, [run.id, availableDeviationIds.join(",")])

  const enqueueDeviations = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => baselineRegressionApi.enqueueDeviations(
      run.id,
      Array.from(selectedDeviationIds),
    ),
    onSuccess: async (result) => {
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({
        queryKey: ["baseline-regression", run.id],
      })
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["optimization-cases"] })
      setSelectedDeviationIds(new Set())
      toast.success(
        result.created
          ? `已将 ${result.created} 张偏差样本加入全局优化池`
          : "所选偏差样本已在全局优化池中",
      )
    },
    onError: (error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })
  const reviewResult = useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({
      item,
      decision,
      note,
      corrections = [],
      nodeCorrectionCount,
    }: {
      item: BaselineRegressionItem
      decision: "approved" | "corrected" | "rejected"
      note: string
      corrections?: ReviewCorrection[]
      nodeCorrectionCount?: number
    }) => {
      if (!item.evaluation) throw new Error("该回归结果没有可审核的评测记录")
      if (!me.data) throw new Error("当前登录账号尚未加载")
      return submitReviewDecision({
        evaluation: item.evaluation,
        reviewer: me.data.username,
        decision,
        note,
        corrections,
      })
    },
    onSuccess: async (_result, variables) => {
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({
        queryKey: ["baseline-regression", run.id],
      })
      void Promise.all([
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-acceptance", run.id] }),
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-acceptance-summary", run.id] }),
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["evaluations"] }),
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["dashboard"] }),
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["optimization-cases"] }),
      ])
      setReviewNotes((current) => ({ ...current, [variables.item.id]: "" }))
      toast.success(
        variables.decision === "corrected"
          ? "人工纠偏与最终等级已保存"
          : variables.decision === "approved"
            ? (variables.nodeCorrectionCount ?? humanNodeCorrectionCount(variables.item)) > 0
              ? "已完成人工验收，节点纠偏事实已保留"
              : "已确认模型结果"
            : "已退回复核",
      )
    },
    onError: (error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })
  const reopenReview = useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ item }: { item: BaselineRegressionItem }) => {
      if (!item.evaluation) throw new Error("该回归结果没有可重开的评测记录")
      const corrections = item.evaluation.human_review?.corrections ?? []
      setReopenSeeds((current) => ({
        ...current,
        [item.id]: {
          corrections,
          note: item.evaluation?.human_review?.note ?? "",
        },
      }))
      return baselineRegressionApi.reopenReview(
        item.evaluation.id,
        item.evaluation.review_revision,
      )
    },
    onSuccess: async (_result, { item }) => {
      await Promise.all([
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-regression", run.id] }),
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-correction-view", run.id, item.id] }),
      ])
      toast.success("已创建新的人工审核轮次，可继续修改")
    },
    onError: (error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })

  const metrics = run.metrics
  const metricsView = baselineRunMetricsView(metrics)
  const pageCount = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(pagination.total / pagination.limit))
  const listCorrectionItem = correctionItems.find((item) => item.id === correctionItemId)
  // 列表是瘦身载荷（item_detail=list），抽屉需要完整评测：按条取全量后合并覆盖
  const fullItemQuery = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["baseline-regression", run.id, "item", correctionItemId],
    queryFn: () => baselineRegressionApi.getRunItem(run.id, correctionItemId as number),
    enabled: Boolean(listCorrectionItem),
  })
  const correctionItem = listCorrectionItem && fullItemQuery.data?.id === listCorrectionItem.id
    ? { ...listCorrectionItem, ...fullItemQuery.data }
    : listCorrectionItem
  const correctionViewQuery = useQuery<CorrectionView>({
    queryKey: ["baseline-correction-view", run.id, correctionItemId],
    queryFn: () => baselineRegressionApi.getCorrectionView(run.id, correctionItemId),
    enabled: correctionItemId > 0 && Boolean(correctionItem),
  })
  const [correctionDraft, setCorrectionDraft] = useState<CorrectionDraft | null>(null)
  const [correctionDraftKey, setCorrectionDraftKey] = useState("")
  const hasUnsavedCorrectionDraft = correctionDraftHasChanges(correctionDraft)
  const correctionBlocker = correctionSubmitBlocker(correctionDraft, correctionViewQuery.data ?? null)
  const correctionViewKey = correctionViewQuery.data
    ? `${correctionViewQuery.data.item_id}:${correctionViewQuery.data.contract?.contract_hash ?? "legacy"}:${correctionViewQuery.data.review_revision}`
    : ""

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!correctionViewQuery.data || !correctionViewKey) return
    if (correctionDraftKey === correctionViewKey) return
    setCorrectionDraft(correctionDraftFromView(correctionViewQuery.data))
    setCorrectionDraftKey(correctionViewKey)
  }, [correctionDraftKey, correctionViewKey, correctionViewQuery.data])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (correctionItemId > 0) return
    setCorrectionDraft(null)
    setCorrectionDraftKey("")
  }, [correctionItemId])

  // 评测细节面板里逐条纠偏：走与增量纠偏同一个节点接口，保证两处口径一致。
  const correctRow = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (payload: RowCorrectionSubmit & { evaluationId: number }) => {
      const suffix = typeof crypto !== "undefined" && "randomUUID" in crypto
        ? crypto.randomUUID()
        : `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(16).slice(2)}`
      return api(`/api/evaluation-results/${payload.evaluationId}/correct-node`, {
        method: "POST",
        ...jsonBody({
          correction_key: `detail-${payload.evaluationId}-${payload.nodePath}-${suffix}`.slice(0, 120),
          node_type: payload.nodeType,
          node_path: payload.nodePath,
          old_value: payload.oldValue,
          new_value: payload.newValue,
          evidence: [],
          reason: payload.reason,
          reason_codes: payload.reasonCodes,
        }),
      })
    },
    onSuccess: async () => {
      toast.success("纠偏已保存")
      await Promise.all([
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-regression", run.id] }),
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-acceptance", run.id] }),
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-acceptance-summary", run.id] }),
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["evaluations"] }),
      ])
    },
    onError: (error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })

  // 回调里读取“当前停留的条目”只能走 ref：闭包里的 correctionItemId 可能已被
  // TanStack Query 同步成下一条，保存目标一律取 mutate 的 variables。
  const currentCorrectionTarget = useRef<CorrectionSaveTarget>({ runId: run.id, itemId: correctionItemId })
  currentCorrectionTarget.current = { runId: run.id, itemId: correctionItemId }
  const applySavedCorrectionView = (target: CorrectionSaveTarget, response: CorrectionView) => {
    queryClient.setQueryData(
      ["baseline-correction-view", target.runId, target.itemId],
      response,
    )
    if (!correctionSaveTargetIsCurrent(target, currentCorrectionTarget.current)) return
    setCorrectionDraft((current) => mergeCorrectionResponse(current ?? {}, response))
    setCorrectionDraftKey(`${response.item_id}:${response.contract?.contract_hash ?? "legacy"}:${response.review_revision}`)
  }

  const submitContractCorrection = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ target, view, draft }: {
      intent: "stay" | "approve"
      target: CorrectionSaveTarget
      view: CorrectionView | undefined
      draft: CorrectionDraft | null
    }) => {
      if (!view || !draft) throw new Error("合同纠偏面板尚未加载")
      const paidNodeKeys = correctionPaidRerunNodeKeys(draft, view)
      if (paidNodeKeys.length > 1) {
        throw new Error("涉及真实模型重跑时请一次只提交一个节点，避免重复计费")
      }
      const realExecutionConfirmed = paidNodeKeys.length > 0
      if (realExecutionConfirmed && !window.confirm(
        "本次纠偏会重新调用下游提示词并产生真实模型费用。确认按冻结模型、提示词和全局硬上限执行吗？",
      )) throw new Error("已取消真实模型付费重跑")
      const payload = correctionSubmissionPayload(
        draft,
        view,
        correctionIdempotencyKey(target.runId, target.itemId),
        { realExecutionConfirmed },
      )
      if (!payload.nodes.length) throw new Error("请先修改至少一个纠偏节点")
      return baselineRegressionApi.submitCorrectionNodes(target.runId, target.itemId, payload)
    },
    onSuccess: async (response, { intent, target }) => {
      applySavedCorrectionView(target, response)
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-regression", target.runId] })
      void Promise.all([
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-acceptance", target.runId] }),
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-acceptance-summary", target.runId] }),
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["evaluations"] }),
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["dashboard"] }),
      ])
      if (intent === "stay") toast.success("合同纠偏已保存，当前素材保持不变")
    },
    onError: (error) => error.message === "已取消真实模型付费重跑"
      ? toast.info(error.message)
      : toast.error(error.message),
  })

  const confirmTruthField = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ confirmation, target, view }: {
      confirmation: TruthFieldConfirmation
      target: CorrectionSaveTarget
      view: CorrectionView | undefined
    }) => {
      if (!view?.contract) throw new Error("当前运行没有可提交的纠偏合同")
      const node = view.nodes.find((item) => item.node_key === confirmation.nodeKey)
      if (!node || !correctionNodeEditable(node)) {
        throw new Error("这个字段当前不能作为人工真值保存")
      }
      const realExecutionConfirmed = node.metadata?.paid_rerun_required === true
      if (realExecutionConfirmed && !window.confirm(
        "确认这个字段会重新调用下游提示词并产生真实模型费用。是否继续？",
      )) throw new Error("已取消真实模型付费重跑")
      return baselineRegressionApi.submitCorrectionNodes(target.runId, target.itemId, {
        contract_hash: view.contract.contract_hash,
        review_revision: view.review_revision,
        idempotency_key: correctionIdempotencyKey(target.runId, target.itemId),
        ...(realExecutionConfirmed ? { real_execution_confirmed: true } : {}),
        nodes: [{
          node_key: confirmation.nodeKey,
          human_value: confirmation.value,
          reason: confirmation.reason,
          evidence: [{ text: confirmation.reason, field_path: confirmation.fieldPath }],
        }],
      })
    },
    onSuccess: async (response, { target }) => {
      applySavedCorrectionView(target, response)
      await Promise.all([
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-regression", target.runId] }),
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-acceptance", target.runId] }),
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-acceptance-summary", target.runId] }),
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["evaluations"] }),
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["dashboard"] }),
      ])
      toast.success(`人工真值 v${response.truth_revision ?? "—"} 已保存`, {
        description: "本轮冻结结果保持不变，新真值会用于下一轮回归。",
      })
    },
    onError: (error) => error.message === "已取消真实模型付费重跑"
      ? toast.info(error.message)
      : toast.error(error.message),
  })

  const reviseKnowledgeGraphRoute = useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ revision, target }: { revision: KnowledgeGraphRouteRevision; target: CorrectionSaveTarget }) => (
      baselineRegressionApi.reviseKnowledgeGraphRoute(target.runId, target.itemId, {
        source: revision.source,
        is_single: revision.isSingle,
        identity_value: revision.identityValue.trim(),
        reason: revision.reason.trim(),
        confirmed: revision.confirmed,
        idempotency_key: `baseline-route:${target.runId}:${target.itemId}:${correctionIdempotencyKey(target.runId, target.itemId)}`,
      })
    ),
    onSuccess: async (response, { target }) => {
      await Promise.all([
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-correction-view", target.runId, target.itemId] }),
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-regression", target.runId] }),
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-set"] }),
      ])
      toast.success(`路线已保存为人工真值 v${response.truth_revision}`, {
        description: "下一轮会自动切换对应提示词分支与字段合同，本轮历史不会改写。",
      })
    },
    onError: (error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })

  if (correctionItemId > 0 && correctionItem) {
    // 提成局部常量，闭包里才能收窄可空性（成员访问在回调中无法保持收窄）。
    const correctionEvaluation = correctionItem.evaluation
    const correctionMode = correctionDetailMode(correctionItem.truth_kind ?? metricsView.kind)
    const nextCorrectionId = nextPendingCorrectionId(correctionItems, correctionItem.id)
    const nodeCorrectionCount = humanNodeCorrectionCount(correctionItem)
    const correctionViewError = correctionViewQuery.isError
      ? correctionViewQuery.error instanceof Error
        ? correctionViewQuery.error.message
        : "人工真值与纠偏合同读取失败，请刷新后重试。"
      : null
    const approveStructuredCorrection = async () => {
      await runStructuredCorrectionApproval({
        hasDirtyDraft: hasUnsavedCorrectionDraft,
        saveDraft: () => submitContractCorrection.mutateAsync({
          intent: "approve",
          target: { runId: run.id, itemId: correctionItemId },
          view: correctionViewQuery.data,
          draft: correctionDraft,
        }),
        submitReview: async (savedView) => {
          if (!correctionItem.evaluation) throw new Error("该回归结果没有可审核的评测记录")
          const itemForReview = savedView
            ? {
                ...correctionItem,
                evaluation: {
                  ...correctionItem.evaluation,
                  review_revision: savedView.review_revision,
                },
              }
            : correctionItem
          await reviewResult.mutateAsync({
            item: itemForReview,
            decision: "approved",
            note: reviewNotes[correctionItem.id]?.trim() ?? "",
            nodeCorrectionCount: savedView
              ? savedView.nodes.filter((node) => Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(node, "human_value")).length
              : nodeCorrectionCount,
          })
        },
        navigateNext: nextCorrectionId ? () => onOpenCorrection(nextCorrectionId) : undefined,
      })
    }
    const correctionActions = correctionItem.evaluation ? (<>
                {correctionItem.evaluation.review_stage === "completed" ? (
                  <div className="space-y-3 rounded-sm border border-alias-edge-059 bg-alias-surface-077 px-4 py-3">
                    <p className="text-sm font-semibold">人工结果已保存</p>
                    <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-022">
                      当前轮次已完成，{nodeCorrectionCount > 0
                        ? `${nodeCorrectionCount} 处节点纠偏记录可回看`
                        : `${correctionItem.evaluation.human_review?.corrections?.length ?? 0} 处字段纠偏记录可回看`}。点击“再次修改”会保留本轮历史并创建新审核轮次。
                    </p>
                    {correctionItem.evaluation.human_review?.note && (
                      <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">说明：{correctionItem.evaluation.human_review.note}</p>
                    )}
                    <Button
                      variant="secondary"
                      disabled={!canReview || reopenReview.isPending}
                      onClick={() => reopenReview.mutate({ item: correctionItem })}
                    >
                      <PencilSimple />{reopenReview.isPending ? "正在创建新轮次" : "再次修改"}
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                    <Button
                      variant="secondary"
                      disabled={!canReview || reviewResult.isPending || submitContractCorrection.isPending}
                      onClick={() => reviewResult.mutate({
                        item: correctionItem,
                        decision: "rejected",
                        note: reviewNotes[correctionItem.id]?.trim() ?? "",
                      })}
                    >
                      退回复核
                    </Button>
                    <Button
                      disabled={
                        !canReview || reviewResult.isPending
                        || submitContractCorrection.isPending
                        || (correctionMode === "structured_fields" && (
                          correctionViewQuery.isLoading
                          || correctionViewQuery.isError
                          || !correctionViewQuery.data
                        ))
                      }
                      onClick={() => {
                        if (correctionMode === "structured_fields") {
                          void approveStructuredCorrection().catch(() => undefined)
                          return
                        }
                        reviewResult.mutate({
                          item: correctionItem,
                          decision: "approved",
                          note: reviewNotes[correctionItem.id]?.trim() ?? "",
                        })
                      }}
                    >
                      <Check weight="bold" />
                      {correctionMode === "structured_fields"
                        ? structuredCorrectionPrimaryAction(
                            Boolean(nextCorrectionId),
                            reviewResult.isPending || submitContractCorrection.isPending,
                          )
                        : reviewResult.isPending
                          ? "正在保存"
                          : nodeCorrectionCount > 0
                            ? "完成本条验收"
                            : "确认结果"}
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                )}
    </>) : null
    return (
      <CorrectionWorkbench
        item={correctionItem}
        decisionActions={correctionActions}
        queueItems={correctionItems}
        onSelectQueueItem={onOpenCorrection}
        runSequence={run.sequence_no}
        reviewedCount={correctionProgress.reviewed}
        reviewTotal={correctionProgress.total}
        hasUnsavedChanges={hasUnsavedCorrectionDraft}
        onBack={onCloseCorrection}
        corrector={me.data?.username ?? ""}
        onPrevious={() => {
          const previousId = previousCorrectionId(correctionItems, correctionItem.id)
          if (previousId) onOpenCorrection(previousId)
        }}
        hasPrevious={Boolean(previousCorrectionId(correctionItems, correctionItem.id))}
        onNext={() => {
          const nextId = nextPendingCorrectionId(correctionItems, correctionItem.id)
          if (nextId) onOpenCorrection(nextId)
        }}
        hasNext={Boolean(nextCorrectionId)}
        correctionView={correctionViewQuery.data ?? null}
        correctionDraft={correctionDraft ?? undefined}
        onCorrectionChange={(nodeKey, patch) => {
          setCorrectionDraft((current) => current ? updateCorrectionDraft(current, nodeKey, patch) : current)
        }}
        onCorrectionSubmit={() => submitContractCorrection.mutate({
          intent: "stay",
          target: { runId: run.id, itemId: correctionItemId },
          view: correctionViewQuery.data,
          draft: correctionDraft,
        })}
        correctionSubmitPending={submitContractCorrection.isPending}
        correctionSubmitDisabled={!canReview || correctionViewQuery.isLoading || correctionViewQuery.isError || Boolean(correctionBlocker)}
        correctionSubmitBlockReason={correctionBlocker}
        correctionDisabled={!canReview || correctionItem.evaluation?.review_stage === "completed"}
        correctionViewLoading={correctionViewQuery.isLoading}
        correctionViewError={correctionViewError}
        onConfirmTruthField={async (confirmation) => {
          await confirmTruthField.mutateAsync({
            confirmation,
            target: { runId: run.id, itemId: correctionItemId },
            view: correctionViewQuery.data,
          })
        }}
        truthFieldPending={confirmTruthField.isPending}
        onReviseKnowledgeGraphRoute={async (revision) => {
          await reviseKnowledgeGraphRoute.mutateAsync({
            revision,
            target: { runId: run.id, itemId: correctionItemId },
          })
        }}
        routeRevisionPending={reviseKnowledgeGraphRoute.isPending}
        onCorrected={async () => {
          await Promise.all([
            queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-regression", run.id] }),
            queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-acceptance", run.id] }),
            queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-acceptance-summary", run.id] }),
            queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["evaluations"] }),
            queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["dashboard"] }),
          ])
        }}
        onPreview={onPreview}
      >
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4">
          {correctionMode === "aesthetic_level" ? <LevelExplanation item={correctionItem} collapseModelFields={Boolean(correctionViewQuery.data?.contract && nativeEvaluationPresentation(correctionViewQuery.data.nodes))} /> : (
            <section className="border-y border-alias-edge-035 bg-alias-surface-044 px-5 py-4" aria-label="字段纠偏说明">
              <p className="text-sm font-bold">按字段确认，不套用美感等级</p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
                在上方逐项确认人工真值；需要排查分支、提示词或写入步骤时，再展开下方的工作流节点与运行证据。
              </p>
            </section>
          )}
          {correctionMode === "aesthetic_level" && correctionEvaluation
            && showsLegacyEvaluationDetail(correctionViewQuery.data?.nodes) && (
            <EvaluationDetailPanel
              precheck={correctionEvaluation.precheck}
              aesthetic={correctionEvaluation.aesthetic}
              scoring={correctionEvaluation.scoring}
              fieldSpecs={fieldSpecsFromContractNodes(correctionViewQuery.data?.nodes)}
              contractNodes={correctionViewQuery.data?.nodes}
              correctionHistory={correctionEvaluation.correction_history}
              correctionPending={correctRow.isPending}
              onCorrect={
                !canReview || correctionEvaluation.review_stage === "completed"
                  ? undefined
                  : async (payload) => {
                      await correctRow.mutateAsync({
                        ...payload,
                        evaluationId: correctionEvaluation.id,
                      })
                    }
              }
              correctedFieldKeys={correctionEvaluation.human_review?.corrections?.map(
                (correction) => correction.field_key,
              )}
            />
          )}
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold">人工决策</p>
            <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
              {correctionMode === "structured_fields" && nextCorrectionId
                ? "确认后自动进入下一条；退回复核时会停留在当前素材。"
                : correctionMode === "structured_fields"
                  ? "这是当前列表最后一条；确认后会停留在本条结果。"
                  : "确认后会停留在本条结果。"}
            </p>
            {correctionItem.evaluation && (
              <div className="space-y-4 border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-4">
                <label>
                  <span className="mb-2 block text-xs font-semibold">人工说明（可选）</span>
                  <Input
                    value={reviewNotes[correctionItem.id] ?? ""}
                    disabled={!canReview || correctionItem.evaluation.review_stage === "completed"}
                    placeholder="补充确认或退回依据"
                    onChange={(event) => setReviewNotes((current) => ({
                      ...current,
                      [correctionItem.id]: event.target.value,
                    }))}
                  />
                </label>
                {!correctionViewQuery.data && fullItemQuery.isSuccess && correctionItem.evaluation.scoring?.dimension_scoring_mode !== "rule_deduction" && (
                  <ReviewCorrectionForm
                    key={`${correctionItem.evaluation.id}-${correctionItem.evaluation.review_revision}`}
                    dimensions={correctionItem.evaluation.aesthetic?.dimensions ?? {}}
                    precheck={correctionItem.evaluation.precheck ?? {}}
                    dimensionSchema={correctionItem.evaluation.dimension_schema}
                    scoring={correctionItem.evaluation.scoring ?? {}}
                    pending={reviewResult.isPending}
                    editable={canReview && correctionItem.evaluation.review_stage !== "completed"}
                    initialCorrections={reopenSeeds[correctionItem.id]?.corrections ?? correctionItem.evaluation.human_review?.corrections ?? []}
                    initialNote={reopenSeeds[correctionItem.id]?.note ?? correctionItem.evaluation.human_review?.note ?? ""}
                    onSubmit={({ note, corrections }) => reviewResult.mutate({
                      item: correctionItem,
                      decision: "corrected",
                      note,
                      corrections,
                    })}
                  />
                )}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </CorrectionWorkbench>
    )
  }
  return (
    <Card data-testid="baseline-correction-list">
      <h3 className="visual-card-title">逐张预测对照</h3>
      <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">每张展示冻结评测理由，并可原位确认、纠偏或退回。</p>
      <div
        className="mt-6 flex gap-0 overflow-x-auto border-b border-[var(--line-strong)]"
        role="tablist"
        aria-label="基准回归工作区"
      >
        <button
          id="baseline-results-tab"
          type="button"
          role="tab"
          aria-controls="baseline-results-panel"
          aria-selected={activeView === "results"}
          className={"visual-native-button rounded-none border-b-0 " + (` shrink-0 border-x border-t px-4 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary ${activeView === "results" ? "border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white" : "border-transparent bg-workspace text-[var(--muted)]"}`)}
          onClick={() => selectView("results")}
        >
          回归结果
        </button>
        <button
          id="baseline-correction-tab"
          type="button"
          role="tab"
          aria-controls="baseline-correction-panel"
          aria-selected={activeView === "correction"}
          className={"visual-native-button rounded-none border-b-0 " + (` shrink-0 border-x border-t px-4 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary ${activeView === "correction" ? "border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white" : "border-transparent bg-workspace text-[var(--muted)]"}`)}
          onClick={() => selectView("correction")}
        >
          纠偏分析与版本推荐 · {availableDeviationIds.length}
        </button>
        <button type="button" role="tab" aria-selected={activeView === "table"}
          className={"visual-native-button rounded-none border-b-0 " + (` shrink-0 border-x border-t px-4 ${activeView === "table" ? "bg-white border-[var(--line-strong)]" : "border-transparent bg-workspace text-[var(--muted)]"}`)}
          onClick={() => selectView("table")}>表格纠偏</button>
      </div>

      {activeView === "table" ? <>
        <div ref={tableTop} className="mt-4 scroll-mt-32 lg:scroll-mt-16">
          <CorrectionPagination page={page} pageSize={pagination.limit} total={pagination.total} loading={loading}
            onPageChange={onPageChange} onPageSizeChange={onPageSizeChange}/>
        </div>
        <CorrectionTable runId={run.id} items={items} disabled={!canReview || loading || run.status === "running"} onPreview={onPreview} onDirtyChange={setTableDirty}/>
        <div className="mt-4">
          <CorrectionPagination page={page} pageSize={pagination.limit} total={pagination.total} loading={loading}
            onPageChange={next => { onPageChange(next); tableTop.current?.scrollIntoView({ block: "start" }) }}
            onPageSizeChange={size => { onPageSizeChange(size); tableTop.current?.scrollIntoView({ block: "start" }) }}/>
        </div>
      </> : activeView === "results" ? (
        <div id="baseline-results-panel" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="baseline-results-tab">



      <section className="mt-7">
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <div className="mb-3 flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-3">
            <div>
              <p className="mt-2 max-w-3xl text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
                全局优化案例池用途（可选）：把偏差样本沉淀到后续自动组批和长期机制优化流程；不影响当前纠偏分析，不修改本轮真值，也不自动启用候选。
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
              <Badge>第 {page + 1}/{pageCount} 页 · 共 {pagination.total} 张</Badge>
              <Button
                variant="ghost"
                size="sm"
                disabled={page <= 0 || loading}
                onClick={() => onPageChange(Math.max(0, page - 1))}
              >
                上一页
              </Button>
              <Button
                variant="ghost"
                size="sm"
                disabled={page + 1 >= pageCount || loading}
                onClick={() => onPageChange(page + 1)}
              >
                下一页
              </Button>
              {availableDeviationIds.length > 0 && (
                <>
                  <Button
                    variant="ghost"
                    size="sm"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setSelectedDeviationIds(
                        selectedDeviationIds.size === availableDeviationIds.length
                          ? new Set()
                          : new Set(availableDeviationIds),
                      )
                    }}
                  >
                    {selectedDeviationIds.size === availableDeviationIds.length
                      ? "取消本页偏差全选"
                      : "选择本页全部偏差"}
                  </Button>
                  <Button
                    size="sm"
                    disabled={!canDevelop || !selectedDeviationIds.size || enqueueDeviations.isPending}
                    onClick={() => enqueueDeviations.mutate()}
                  >
                    {enqueueDeviations.isPending
                      ? "正在加入全局优化池"
                      : `加入全局优化池（可选） · ${selectedDeviationIds.size}`}
                  </Button>
                </>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="max-h-[620px] overflow-auto visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white">
            {loading ? (
              <div className="h-64 animate-pulse bg-white" />
            ) : items.length ? (
              <div className="divide-y divide-[var(--line)]">
                {items.map((item) => {
                  const fallback = gradedByFallback(item.stage_a)
                  const evaluation = item.evaluation
                  const humanStatus = reviewStatus(evaluation)
                  const structuredTruth = item.truth_kind === "structured_fields"
                  return (
                    <div
                      key={item.id}
                      className="bg-white"
                    >
                      <div className="grid gap-3 px-4 py-4 sm:grid-cols-[64px_minmax(0,1fr)_auto] sm:items-center">
                        {isImageMimeType(item.asset.mime_type) ? (
                          <ImagePreviewButton
                            src={item.image_url}
                            alt={item.asset.name}
                            imageClassName="size-14"
                            onPreview={onPreview}
                          />
                        ) : (
                          <div className="flex size-14 items-center justify-center border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-alias-surface-029 text-[var(--muted)] rounded-md" title={item.asset.mime_type}>
                            <File size={24} />
                          </div>
                        )}
                        <div className="min-w-0">
                          <p className="file-name truncate text-sm">{item.asset.name}</p>
                          <p className="font-data mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                            素材 #<span className="visual-code">{item.asset_id}</span> · 评测 #<span className="visual-code">{item.evaluation_id ?? "—"}</span>{structuredTruth ? ` · 字段合同 ${item.truth_contract_key ?? "—"}` : ` · 分数 ${item.authoritative_score ?? "—"}`}
                          </p>
                          <p className="mt-2 line-clamp-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
                            {structuredTruth ? structuredTruthRunSummary(item) : levelExplanationSummary(item)}
                          </p>
                          {item.error_message && (
                            <div className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-041">
                              <p>失败原因：{item.failure?.summary ?? baselineErrorMessage(item.error_message)}</p>
                              {item.failure?.hint && (
                                <p className="text-[var(--muted)]">怎么处理：{item.failure.hint}</p>
                              )}
                              {item.failure?.rejected_model_output && (
                                <p className="font-data text-[var(--muted)]">
                                  模型实际输出：{rejectedOutputSummary(item.failure.rejected_model_output, item.failure.message)}
                                </p>
                              )}
                            </div>
                          )}
                        </div>
                        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 sm:max-w-72 sm:justify-end">
                          {fallback && <Badge tone="warning">fallback 分级</Badge>}
                          {item.optimization_case_id !== null && (
                            <Badge tone="success">已入全局优化池</Badge>
                          )}
                          {humanStatus && (
                            <Badge tone={humanStatus.tone}>{humanStatus.label}</Badge>
                          )}
                          {item.status === "completed"
                            && item.deviation
                            && item.optimization_case_id === null && (
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              className="visual-native-button p-0 flex items-center justify-center focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary"
                              aria-label={`${
                                selectedDeviationIds.has(item.id)
                                  ? "取消加入全局优化池"
                                  : "选择加入全局优化池"
                              }${item.asset.name}`}
                              onClick={() => {
                                setSelectedDeviationIds((current) => {
                                  const next = new Set(current)
                                  if (next.has(item.id)) next.delete(item.id)
                                  else next.add(item.id)
                                  return next
                                })
                              }}
                            >
                              {selectedDeviationIds.has(item.id)
                                ? <CheckSquare size={20} weight="fill" />
                                : <Square size={20} />}
                            </button>
                          )}
                          {item.status === "failed" ? (
                            <Badge tone="danger"><WarningCircle />失败</Badge>
                          ) : item.status === "queued" ? (
                            <Badge tone="active">等待预测</Badge>
                          ) : item.interpretation?.status === "manual_required" ? (
                            <Badge tone="warning">已完成 · 待人工判断</Badge>
                          ) : item.interpretation?.status === "rule_decided" && !structuredTruth ? (
                            <Badge tone={item.deviation ? "danger" : "success"}>
                              {item.deviation ? "规则定级 · 与真值不一致" : "规则定级 · 与真值一致"}
                            </Badge>
                          ) : structuredTruth ? (
                            <Badge tone={item.deviation ? "danger" : "success"}>
                              {item.deviation ? `${item.field_deviations?.length ?? 0} 个字段有偏差` : "结构化字段已对齐"}
                            </Badge>
                          ) : (
                            <Badge tone={item.deviation ? "danger" : "success"}>
                              预测 {item.predicted_level ?? "—"} / 期望 {item.expected_level ?? "—"}
                            </Badge>
                          )}
                          {evaluation && (
                            <Button
                              size="sm"
                              variant="ghost"
                              onClick={() => onOpenCorrection(item.id)}
                            >
                              <PencilSimple />
                              {evaluation.review_stage === "completed"
                                ? "查看人工标记"
                                : "确认或纠偏"}
                            </Button>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <details className="visual-native-details border-t border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-051">
                        <summary className="font-semibold">
                          {structuredTruth ? "展开字段真值与本轮结果" : "展开完整评测理由"}
                        </summary>
                        {structuredTruth ? <StructuredTruthDetails item={item} /> : <LevelExplanation item={item} />}
                      </details>
                    </div>
                  )
                })}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <p className="px-5 py-12 text-center text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">
                当前运行尚无逐张结果。
              </p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <CorrectionAnalysisPanel
          run={run}
          items={items}
          loading={loading}
          onPreview={onPreview}
          canDecide={me.data?.is_admin === true}
          onContinueCorrection={onOpenCorrection}
        />
      )}
    </Card>
  )
}

export function StructuredTruthDetails({ item }: { item: BaselineRegressionItem }) {
  const imageDetail = item.normalized_result?.image_grade_detail
  if (imageDetail) {
    const truthImages = Array.isArray(item.expected_result?.images) ? item.expected_result.images as { cloud_id: string; level: string }[] : []
    const truth = new Map(truthImages.map((image) => [image.cloud_id, image.level]))
    const predicted = new Map(imageDetail.images.map((image) => [image.cloud_id, image]))
    const ids = [...new Set([...truth.keys(), ...predicted.keys(), ...imageDetail.ungraded.map((image) => image.cloud_id)])]
    return <section className="space-y-3 border-t border-[var(--line)] bg-white px-4 py-4" aria-label="逐图预测与人工真值">
      <h4 className="text-sm font-bold">逐图预测与人工真值</h4>
      <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">本轮冻结阈值：{imageDetail.bands.map((band) => `${band.level} ≥ ${band.min_inclusive}`).join("；")}。缺失表示本轮没有可比较的图片档位。</p>
      <div className="overflow-x-auto"><table className="w-full text-left text-xs"><thead><tr>{["图片编号", "人工档位", "模型分数", "模型档位", "对比"].map((label) => <th key={label} className="p-2">{label}</th>)}</tr></thead>
        <tbody>{ids.map((id) => {
          const expected = truth.get(id)
          const actual = predicted.get(id)
          const comparison = !expected ? "额外图片" : !actual ? "缺失" : expected === actual.level ? "一致" : Number(actual.level.slice(1)) < Number(expected.slice(1)) ? "高估" : "低估"
          return <tr key={id} className="border-t border-[var(--line)]"><td className="p-2 font-data"><span className="visual-code">{id}</span></td><td className="p-2">{expected ?? "—"}</td><td className="p-2">{actual?.score ?? "—"}</td><td className="p-2">{actual?.level ?? "—"}</td><td className="p-2">{comparison}</td></tr>
        })}</tbody>
      </table></div>
    </section>
  }
  const expected = flattenResult(item.expected_result ?? {})
  const predicted = flattenResult(item.normalized_result ?? {})
  const fields = [...new Set([...Object.keys(expected), ...Object.keys(predicted)])].sort()
  return (
    <div className="border-t border-[var(--line)] px-4 py-4">
      <div className="overflow-x-auto visual-panel border border-[var(--line)] bg-white">
        <table className="w-full min-w-[620px] text-left text-xs">
          <thead className="bg-alias-surface-029 text-[var(--muted)]"><tr><th className="px-3 py-2">字段</th><th className="px-3 py-2">冻结真值</th><th className="px-3 py-2">本轮结果</th><th className="px-3 py-2">对比</th></tr></thead>
          <tbody className="divide-y divide-[var(--line)]">
            {fields.map((field) => {
              const expectedValue = displayStructuredValue(expected[field])
              const predictedValue = displayStructuredValue(predicted[field])
              const matched = structuredValuesMatch(expected[field], predicted[field])
              return <tr key={field}><td className="px-3 py-2 font-semibold">{structuredFieldLabel(field)}</td><td className="px-3 py-2">{expectedValue}</td><td className="px-3 py-2">{predictedValue}</td><td className="px-3 py-2"><Badge tone={matched ? "success" : "danger"}>{matched ? "一致" : "需纠偏"}</Badge></td></tr>
            })}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function flattenResult(value: Record<string, unknown>, prefix = ""): Record<string, unknown> {
  return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(value).flatMap(([key, child]) => {
    const path = prefix ? `${prefix}.${key}` : key
    return child && typeof child === "object" && !Array.isArray(child)
      ? Object.entries(flattenResult(child as Record<string, unknown>, path))
      : [[path, child]]
  }))
}

function displayStructuredValue(value: unknown) {
  if (value === undefined) return "未返回"
  if (value === null) return "空"
  if (typeof value === "string" || typeof value === "number" || typeof value === "boolean") return String(value)
  return JSON.stringify(value)
}

export function dimensionSelectionName(selection: BaselineRegressionRun["selection"]["dimension"]) {
  if (selection.mode === "none" || selection.prompt_only) return "已关闭 · 仅提示词评级"
  const contract = selection.v3_contract
  if (!contract?.spec_version || !contract.tracks.length) return "未知版本"
  const trackNames: Record<string, string> = {
    class_one: "一类",
    class_two: "二类",
    class_three: "三类",
  }
  const dimensions = contract.tracks
    .map((track) => {
      const fallbackName = track.label.split("（", 1)[0]?.trim() || track.key
      return `${trackNames[track.key] ?? fallbackName}${track.dimension_count}维`
    })
    .join("/")
  // 首要标识用 rev 号——与机制版本选择器（「rev 9 · 现役」）同一套编号，
  // 运营才能把「本轮用的维度」和「机制版本列表」直观对上。内部规格串
  // （inspiration-v3-…）只作括注兜底，历史 run 无 rev 号时单独显示。
  if (typeof contract.revision === "number") {
    return `rev ${contract.revision} · ${dimensions}`
  }
  return `${contract.spec_version} · ${dimensions}`
}

export { baselineErrorMessage } from "@/features/baseline-regression/baseline-error-guidance"

export function SelectionFact({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="baseline-selection-fact min-w-0 rounded-md border border-line bg-surface-sunken px-4 py-3">
      <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</p>
      <p className="font-data mt-2 break-words text-sm font-semibold" title={value}>
        {value}
      </p>
    </div>
  )
}

export function gradedByFallback(stageA: Record<string, unknown>) {
  const nested = [stageA, stageA.classification, stageA.grading]
  return nested.some((value) => (
    value !== null
    && value !== undefined
    && typeof value === "object"
    && !Array.isArray(value)
    && "graded_by" in value
    && (value as { graded_by?: unknown }).graded_by === "fallback"
  ))
}

import type { BaselineRuleDiagnostics } from "@/lib/types"
import { SecondaryDrawer } from "@/components/workspace-page"
import type { ReactNode } from "react"

export function RuleDiagnosticsDrawer({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  children,
}: {
  open: boolean
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void
  children: ReactNode
}) {
  return (
    <SecondaryDrawer
      open={open}
      onOpenChange={onOpenChange}
      title="扣分规则命中诊断"
      description="查看本轮回归中每条规则实际命中多少次；规则不命中时，调整扣分值与红线阈值不会改变结果。"
    >
      {children}
    </SecondaryDrawer>
  )
}

function Stat({ label, value, hint }: { label: string; value: ReactNode; hint?: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="border border-[var(--line)] px-3 py-2 rounded-md">
      <div className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</div>
      <div className="font-data mt-1 text-lg font-semibold">{value}</div>
      {hint ? <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{hint}</div> : null}
    </div>
  )
}

export function RuleDiagnosticsEvidence({
  data,
  loading,
  error,
}: {
  data?: BaselineRuleDiagnostics
  loading: boolean
  error: Error | null
}) {
  if (loading) {
    return <div className="h-52 animate-pulse bg-alias-surface-042" />
  }
  if (error) {
    return (
      <div className="border border-alias-edge-036 bg-alias-surface-067 px-4 py-4 text-sm text-alias-content-041 rounded-md">
        规则命中诊断加载失败：{error.message}
      </div>
    )
  }
  if (!data) {
    return <div className="px-4 py-6 text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">暂无诊断数据。</div>
  }

  const hitRules = data.rules.filter((rule) => rule.hit_count > 0)
  const deadRules = data.rules.filter((rule) => rule.hit_count === 0)
  const noHitShare = data.scored_item_count
    ? Math.round((data.items_without_rule_hits / data.scored_item_count) * 100)
    : 0

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4" data-testid="baseline-rule-diagnostics">
      {/* 规则层几乎不参与打分时，先把结论说清楚：继续调阈值不会有效果。 */}
      {noHitShare >= 50 ? (
        <div
          className="border border-alias-edge-039 bg-alias-surface-062 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-alias-content-030 rounded-md"
          data-testid="baseline-rule-layer-warning"
        >
          <div className="font-semibold">
            {data.rule_layer_inert
              ? "规则层完全未参与打分"
              : `${noHitShare}% 的样本没有命中任何规则`}
          </div>
          <div className="mt-1">
            这些样本的分数就是调用 B 给出的原始分，未经规则层增减
            {data.unpenalised_level
              ? `，落在 ${data.unpenalised_level} 档`
              : ""}
            。此时提高扣分值或下调红线阈值都不会改变判级，应先检查调用 B 是否报出了缺陷。
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : null}

      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 md:grid-cols-4">
        <Stat label="声明规则数" value={data.declared_rule_count} />
        <Stat
          label="从未命中"
          value={data.never_hit_rule_count}
          hint={data.declared_rule_count ? `占 ${Math.round((data.never_hit_rule_count / data.declared_rule_count) * 100)}%` : undefined}
        />
        <Stat
          label="零命中样本"
          value={`${data.items_without_rule_hits}/${data.scored_item_count}`}
          hint={`${noHitShare}%`}
        />
        <Stat label="触发分数上限" value={data.items_with_score_caps} hint="红线等策略生效次数" />
      </div>

      {Object.keys(data.redline_policy).length ? (
        <div className="border border-[var(--line)] px-4 py-3 text-sm rounded-md">
          <div className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">红线策略</div>
          <div className="font-data mt-1 break-all">
            {Object.entries(data.redline_policy)
              .map(([key, value]) => `${key}=${String(value)}`)
              .join("  ")}
            {`  规则数=${data.redline_rule_count}`}
          </div>
          {data.redline_rule_count > 0 && data.items_with_score_caps === 0 ? (
            <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-alias-content-030">
              红线已配置但本轮一次未触发。
            </div>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {data.undeclared_hits.length ? (
        <div className="border border-alias-edge-036 bg-alias-surface-067 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-alias-content-041 rounded-md">
          <div className="font-semibold">模型报出了合同未声明的规则</div>
          <div className="font-data mt-1 break-all">
            {data.undeclared_hits
              .map((hit) => `${hit.dimension_key}/${hit.rule_id} × ${hit.hit_count}`)
              .join("，")}
          </div>
          <div className="mt-1 text-xs">这些命中不会参与扣分，通常说明提示词与合同的规则 id 不一致。</div>
        </div>
      ) : null}

      <section className="border border-[var(--line)] rounded-md">
        <header className="grid grid-cols-[minmax(180px,1fr)_minmax(120px,180px)_80px_70px] gap-3 border-b border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-042 px-4 py-2 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
          <span>规则</span>
          <span>维度</span>
          <span>分值</span>
          <span>命中</span>
        </header>
        {data.rules.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="px-4 py-4 text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">该冻结合同未声明任何扣分或加分规则。</div>
        ) : (
          [...hitRules, ...deadRules].map((rule) => (
            <div
              key={`${rule.dimension_key}:${rule.rule_id}:${rule.kind}`}
              className="grid grid-cols-[minmax(180px,1fr)_minmax(120px,180px)_80px_70px] gap-3 border-b border-[var(--line)] px-4 py-2 text-sm last:border-0"
              data-testid={`baseline-rule-coverage-${rule.dimension_key}-${rule.rule_id}`}
            >
              <span className="font-data break-all">
                <span className="visual-code">{rule.rule_id}</span>
                <span className="ml-2 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                  {rule.kind === "bonus" ? "加分" : "扣分"}
                </span>
                {rule.description ? (
                  <span className="mt-0.5 block text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{rule.description}</span>
                ) : null}
              </span>
              <span className="break-all text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                {rule.dimension_label || rule.dimension_key}
              </span>
              <span className="font-data">{rule.points ?? "—"}</span>
              <span className={rule.hit_count ? "font-data font-semibold" : "font-data text-alias-content-041"}>
                {rule.hit_count}
              </span>
            </div>
          ))
        )}
      </section>

      {Object.keys(data.score_buckets).length ? (
        <div className="border border-[var(--line)] px-4 py-3 text-sm rounded-md">
          <div className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">分数分布</div>
          <div className="font-data mt-1 break-all">
            {Object.entries(data.score_buckets)
              .map(([bucket, count]) => `${bucket}: ${count}`)
              .join("　")}
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  )
}

import type { ReactNode } from "react"
import { SecondaryDrawer } from "@/components/workspace-page"
import { formatMicrosAsCny, microsToYuanPer1k } from "@/features/model-registry/pricing-contract"
import { expectedOutputBasisLabel, expectedOutputSourceLabel } from "./real-model-execution"
import type { RealExecutionPreview } from "./real-model-execution"

export function RunConfigDrawer({ open, onOpenChange, children, footer }: { open: boolean; onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void; children: ReactNode; footer?: ReactNode }) {
  return <SecondaryDrawer open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange} size="wide" title="运行配置" description="选择一个完整评测机制与执行方式；启动后会冻结本轮所有节点和版本。" footer={footer}>{children}</SecondaryDrawer>

export function RunPricingEstimate({ preview }: { preview: RealExecutionPreview }) {
  const reserve = preview.retry_reserve
  const plannedWithRetries = preview.expected.cost_micros + (reserve?.cost_micros ?? 0)
  const tiers = Array.from(new Set((preview.node_pricing ?? []).flatMap((item) =>
    item.expected_tier_buckets?.map((bucket) => bucket.pricing_tier_key)
      ?? (item.expected_pricing_tier_key ? [item.expected_pricing_tier_key] : []),
  )))
  return (
    <section className="border border-alias-edge-049 bg-white px-3 py-3 rounded-md" aria-label="真实调用计价预估">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <div><p className="text-xs font-bold text-alias-content-033">本轮未命中费用上界</p><p className="font-data mt-1 text-lg font-bold text-alias-content-033">{formatMicrosAsCny(preview.expected.cost_micros)}</p></div>
        <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">预计计价档位：{tiers.join("、") || "旧版平价"}</p>
      </div>
      {preview.inference_route && <p className="mt-2 text-sm font-semibold">本轮推理：{preview.inference_route.mode === "batch" ? "批量推理 · 等待时间较长，按批量价格估算" : "在线推理"}</p>}
      {preview.doubao_endpoint && (
        <p className={`mt-2 text-xs leading-5 ${preview.doubao_endpoint.blocker ? "border-l-2 border-danger pl-3 text-alias-content-041" : "text-[var(--muted)]"}`}>
          {preview.doubao_endpoint.blocker
            ? preview.doubao_endpoint.blocker
            : preview.doubao_endpoint.binding
              ? `豆包接入点：${preview.doubao_endpoint.binding.display_name}（${preview.doubao_endpoint.binding.endpoint_id}）· ${preview.doubao_endpoint.binding.verification_status === "verified" ? "已验收" : "未验收"}，费用按该接入点归因`
              : preview.doubao_endpoint.required
                ? "该机制使用豆包 2.0，但尚未绑定推理接入点；当前按模型 ID 直连，费用无法按渠道归因"
                : "该机制不使用豆包 2.0 接入点路由"}
        </p>
      )}
      <dl className="mt-3 grid gap-2 text-xs sm:grid-cols-2">
        <CostFact label="调用次数" value={`${preview.expected.requests.toLocaleString("zh-CN")} 次`} />
        <CostFact label="输入 token" value={preview.expected.input_tokens.toLocaleString("zh-CN")} />
        <CostFact label="输出 token" value={preview.expected.output_tokens.toLocaleString("zh-CN")} />
        <CostFact label="输出 token 上限" value={(preview.expected.output_tokens + (reserve?.output_tokens ?? 0)).toLocaleString("zh-CN")} />
        <CostFact label="图片 token" value={preview.expected.image_tokens == null ? "结算后拆分（已含在输入 token）" : preview.expected.image_tokens.toLocaleString("zh-CN")} />
        <CostFact label="重试预留" value={reserve ? `${reserve.requests.toLocaleString("zh-CN")} 次 · ${formatMicrosAsCny(reserve.cost_micros)}` : "未单独预留"} />
        <CostFact label="含重试未命中上界" value={formatMicrosAsCny(plannedWithRetries)} />
        <CostFact label="服务端费用上限" value={formatMicrosAsCny(preview.caps.max_cost_micros)} />
      </dl>
      {(preview.node_pricing ?? []).length > 0 && (
        <div className="mt-3 divide-y divide-[var(--line)] border-y border-[var(--line)]">
          {preview.node_pricing?.map((node) => (
            <NodePricingFact key={node.node_instance_id} node={node} />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
      <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-4 text-[var(--muted)]">输出 token {expectedOutputSourceLabel(preview.expected_output_source)}。输入 token 已包含图片 token。缓存命中无法在启动前可靠预估；最终费用以供应商返回的 cached_tokens 为准。</p>
    </section>
  )
}

function NodePricingFact({ node }: { node: NonNullable<RealExecutionPreview["node_pricing"]>[number] }) {
  const buckets = node.expected_tier_buckets ?? []
  const retryReserve = node.retry_reserve_request_count
  const expectedTierKeys = new Set(
    buckets.length > 0
      ? buckets.map((bucket) => bucket.pricing_tier_key)
      : [node.expected_pricing_tier_key ?? node.pricing_tier_key],
  )
  const expectedRates = (node.pricing_tiers ?? []).filter((tier) =>
    expectedTierKeys.has(tier.tier_key),
  )
  return (
    <div className="grid gap-1 py-2 text-xs sm:grid-cols-[minmax(10rem,1fr)_minmax(18rem,2fr)]">
      <span className="font-semibold"><span className="visual-code">{node.node_instance_id}</span> · <span className="visual-code">{node.model_id}</span></span>
      <div className="space-y-1 text-[var(--muted)] sm:text-right">
        <p>
          预计 {(node.expected_request_count ?? 0).toLocaleString("zh-CN")} 次 · 输入 {(node.expected_input_tokens ?? 0).toLocaleString("zh-CN")} · 输出 {(node.expected_output_tokens ?? 0).toLocaleString("zh-CN")} · 未命中上界 {node.expected_cost_micros == null ? "待按输入结算" : formatMicrosAsCny(node.expected_cost_micros)}
        </p>
        {expectedRates.map((tier) => (
          <p key={tier.tier_key}>
            {tier.tier_key}：普通输入价 {formatRate(tier.input_micros_per_1k)} · 缓存命中价 {tier.cached_input_micros_per_1k == null ? "合同未提供" : formatRate(tier.cached_input_micros_per_1k)} · 输出价 {formatRate(tier.output_micros_per_1k)}
          </p>
        ))}
        <p>
          逐档：{buckets.length > 0
            ? buckets.map((bucket) => `${bucket.pricing_tier_key} × ${bucket.request_count}（${formatMicrosAsCny(bucket.cost_micros)}）`).join("；")
            : (node.expected_pricing_tier_key ?? "旧版平价")}
        </p>
        <p>{expectedOutputBasisLabel(node.expected_output_basis)}</p>
        <p>
          重试预留：{retryReserve == null ? "旧工作流按全局预算" : `${retryReserve.toLocaleString("zh-CN")} 次`}；最大单次预留 {node.pricing_tier_key} · {formatMicrosAsCny(node.estimated_cost_micros_per_request)}
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function formatRate(microsPer1k: number): string {
  return `${microsToYuanPer1k(microsPer1k)} 元 / 1k tokens`
}

function CostFact({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return <div className="flex justify-between gap-3 border-b border-[var(--line)] py-1"><dt className="text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</dt><dd className="font-data text-right font-semibold">{value}</dd></div>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/run-history-drawer.tsx =====
import type { ReactNode } from "react"
import { SecondaryDrawer } from "@/components/workspace-page"
import { formatMicrosAsCny } from "@/features/model-registry/pricing-contract"
import type { BaselineModelCost } from "@/lib/types"

export function RunHistoryDrawer({ open, onOpenChange, children }: { open: boolean; onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void; children: ReactNode }) {
  return <SecondaryDrawer open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange} title="运行历史" description="查看本基准集的历史回归轮次与冻结版本。">{children}</SecondaryDrawer>
}

export function RunCostEvidence({ cost }: { cost?: BaselineModelCost | null }) {
  if (!cost) return null
  const variance = cost.variance_micros
  const actualCost = cost.actual_cost_known
    ? formatMicrosAsCny(cost.actual_cost_micros)
    : cost.unknown_invocation_count > 0
      ? `账单未知（${cost.unknown_invocation_count} 次调用）`
      : "结算中"
  const cache = cost.cache_observation
  const cachedRatio = cache?.cached_token_ratio == null
    ? "—"
    : `${(cache.cached_token_ratio * 100).toLocaleString("zh-CN", { maximumFractionDigits: 2 })}%`
  const cacheSavings = cache?.cache_adjusted_cost_exact
    ? formatMicrosAsCny(cache.cache_savings_micros ?? 0)
    : "保守结算（含未上报、无效或旧合同）"
  return (
    <dl className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-3 text-xs">
      <CostFact label="规划预估" value={formatMicrosAsCny(cost.estimated_cost_micros)} />
      <CostFact label="最大预留" value={formatMicrosAsCny(cost.reserved_cost_micros)} />
      <CostFact label="实际费用" value={actualCost} />
      <CostFact label="实际档位" value={cost.tiers.map((tier) => `${tier.tier_key}×${tier.count}`).join("、") || "—"} />
      <CostFact label="费用偏差" value={variance == null ? "—" : `${variance >= 0 ? "+" : "−"}${formatMicrosAsCny(Math.abs(variance))}`} />
      <CostFact label="缓存 token" value={cache ? `${cache.cached_input_tokens.toLocaleString("zh-CN")} · ${cachedRatio}` : "旧记录无缓存观测"} />
      <CostFact label="命中 / 未命中" value={cache ? `${cache.hit_count} / ${cache.miss_count}` : "—"} />
      <CostFact label="未上报 / 无效" value={cache ? `${cache.unreported_count} / ${cache.invalid_count}` : "—"} />
      <CostFact label="缓存节省" value={cache ? cacheSavings : "—"} />
      <CostFact label="结算精度" value={cache?.cache_adjusted_cost_exact ? "缓存调整后精确" : "保守结算"} />
    </dl>
  )
}

function CostFact({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return <div><dt className="text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</dt><dd className="font-data mt-1 font-semibold">{value}</dd></div>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/run-page-placeholder.ts =====
/** Keep the editor mounted while paging within a run, never across runs. */
export function sameRunPage<T extends { summary: { id: number } }>(
  selectedRunId: number,
  previous: T | undefined,
): T | undefined {
  return selectedRunId > 0 && previous?.summary.id === selectedRunId ? previous : undefined
}

import type { BaselineRegressionRun } from "../../lib/types"

export function workflowRuntimePending(run: BaselineRegressionRun | null | undefined) {
  return Boolean(run && !["failed", "partial_failed"].includes(run.status)
    && run.workflow_runtime.selected && run.workflow_runtime.pending > 0)
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/structured-baseline-truth.ts =====
import type {
  BaselineTruthContract,
  FilenameKnowledgeGraphTruthSuggestion,
} from "@/lib/types"

export type StructuredTruthDraft = {
  contractKey: string
  values: Record<string, string | number>
  workflowInput?: Record<string, unknown>
  source?: "filename" | "manual"
}

const ROUTE_FIELDS = new Set(["ll_id", "res_id", "is_single"])

export function createStructuredTruthDraft(
  contract?: BaselineTruthContract,
): StructuredTruthDraft {
  if (!contract) return { contractKey: "", values: {} }
  const values: Record<string, string | number> = {}
  for (const field of contract.fields) {
    values[field.key] = field.fixed_value ?? ""
  }
  return { contractKey: contract.contract_key, values }
}

export function switchStructuredTruthContract(
  current: StructuredTruthDraft,
  contract: BaselineTruthContract,
): StructuredTruthDraft {
  const next = createStructuredTruthDraft(contract)
  for (const field of contract.fields) {
    if (ROUTE_FIELDS.has(field.key) || field.fixed_value !== null) continue
    const previous = current.values[field.key]
    if (typeof previous === "string") next.values[field.key] = previous
  }
  return next
}

export function structuredTruthContract(
  draft: StructuredTruthDraft | undefined,
  contracts: readonly BaselineTruthContract[],
) {
  return contracts.find((item) => item.contract_key === draft?.contractKey)
}

export function structuredTruthReady(
  draft: StructuredTruthDraft | undefined,
  contracts: readonly BaselineTruthContract[],
) {
  const contract = structuredTruthContract(draft, contracts)
  if (!draft || !contract) return false
  const identity = draft.values[contract.identity_field]
  if (typeof identity !== "string" || !identity.trim()) return false
  if (contract.contract_kind === "design_case") {
    if (!contract.fields.every((field) => (
      !field.required
      || field.fixed_value !== null
      || String(draft.values[field.key] ?? "").trim().length > 0
    ))) return false
    const workflowInput = structuredTruthWorkflowInput(draft)
    const caseInput = workflowInput?.case
    if (!caseInput || typeof caseInput !== "object" || Array.isArray(caseInput)) return false
    const frozenCase = caseInput as Record<string, unknown>
    if (frozenCase.cloud_id !== identity) return false
    for (const field of ["project_id", "case_name", "case_text"] as const) {
      if (typeof frozenCase[field] !== "string" || !frozenCase[field].trim()) return false
    }
    for (const field of ["quality_score", "time_score", "filter_threshold"] as const) {
      if (typeof frozenCase[field] !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(frozenCase[field])) return false
    }
    const images = frozenCase.images
    if (!Array.isArray(images) || images.length < 1) return false
    if (images.some((image) => {
      if (!image || typeof image !== "object" || Array.isArray(image)) return true
      const frozen = image as Record<string, unknown>
      return typeof frozen.cloud_id !== "string" || !frozen.cloud_id
        || typeof frozen.oss_url !== "string" || !frozen.oss_url
        || typeof frozen.asset_id !== "number" || !Number.isInteger(frozen.asset_id)
        || typeof frozen.asset_sha256 !== "string"
        || !/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(frozen.asset_sha256)
    })) return false
  }
  return contract.fields.every((field) => (
    field.fixed_value === null
    || draft.values[field.key] === field.fixed_value
  ))
}

export function structuredTruthExpectedResult(
  draft: StructuredTruthDraft,
  contract: BaselineTruthContract,
) {
  const result: Record<string, unknown> = {}
  for (const field of contract.fields) {
    const raw = field.fixed_value ?? draft.values[field.key] ?? ""
    // 案例类合同的非必填字段留空就不提交：空串会被合同当成「填了一个空值」，
    // 素材维度更严重——空的文字描述会把已有统计判成冲突。
    if (
      (contract.contract_kind === "design_case"
        || contract.contract_kind === "design_case_aggregate")
      && !field.required
      && String(raw).trim() === ""
    ) continue
    let value: unknown = raw
    if (field.type === "array") {
      value = String(raw).split(",").map((item) => item.trim()).filter(Boolean)
    } else if (field.type === "number" || field.type === "integer") {
      const parsed = Number(raw)
