                    className="mb-3 border border-alias-edge-042 bg-alias-surface-082 px-3 py-2 text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-044 rounded-md"
                  >
                    {copy.goldenSetWarning}
                  </p>
                ) : null}

                <dl className="space-y-2 text-xs leading-5">
                  <div className="flex gap-2">
                    <dt className="w-20 shrink-0 text-[var(--muted)]">最终等级</dt>
                    <dd className="font-medium">{pending.corrected_level ?? LEVEL_HINT}</dd>
                  </div>
                  {pending.decision === "corrected" ? (
                    <div className="flex gap-2">
                      <dt className="w-20 shrink-0 text-[var(--muted)]">维度纠错</dt>
                      <dd className="font-medium">{pending.corrections?.length ?? 0} 项</dd>
                    </div>
                  ) : null}
                  <div className="flex gap-2">
                    <dt className="w-20 shrink-0 text-[var(--muted)]">复核备注</dt>
                    <dd className={pending.reviewNote.trim() ? "font-medium" : "text-[var(--muted)]"}>
                      {pending.reviewNote.trim() || "未填写"}
                    </dd>
                  </div>
                </dl>

                <p className="mt-3 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
                  定案后会自动跳到下一条。逐条纠偏在保存时已分别入库，这一步只决定这条样本的复核结论。
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 border-t border-[var(--line-strong)] px-5 py-3">
                <Button variant="secondary" onClick={onCancel} disabled={submitting}>
                  返回继续看
                </Button>
                {onSkip ? (
                  <Button variant="secondary" onClick={onSkip} disabled={submitting}>
                    {skipLabel ?? "只看下一条，不定案"}
                  </Button>
                ) : null}
                <Button onClick={onConfirm} disabled={submitting}>
                  {submitting ? "提交中…" : confirmLabel ?? copy.confirmLabel}
                </Button>
              </div>
            </>
          ) : null}
        </Dialog.Content>
      </Dialog.Portal>
    </Dialog.Root>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/evaluation-detail/row-correction-dialog.tsx =====
import * as Dialog from "@radix-ui/react-dialog"
import { X } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react"

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea"
import type { RowCorrectionTarget } from "./detail-model"

/**
 * 单条信息的纠偏弹窗。
 *
 * 运营在这里选/填正确结果并写明理由。理由分两部分：结构化归因码用于纠偏分析聚合，
 * 自由文本补充具体判断依据——只有自由文本的话，一万条纠偏也统计不出「哪一层最常
 * 犯哪类错」。
 */

/** 归因码与 review-correction-form 保持同一套，保证纠偏分析口径一致 */
const REASON_CODES: ReadonlyArray<readonly [string, string]> = [
  ["overrated", "评分偏高"],
  ["underrated", "评分偏低"],
  ["ignored_defect", "忽略明显缺陷"],
  ["false_defect", "把正常现象当缺陷"],
  ["photography_as_design", "把摄影质量当设计质量"],
  ["rendering_as_design", "把渲染精美当设计优秀"],
  ["ignored_furnishing", "忽略家具与软装问题"],
  ["boundary_unclear", "等级边界理解错误"],
  ["invalid_evidence", "使用了不可靠证据"],
  ["wrong_visible_attribute", "可见属性判断错误"],
  ["enum_mismatch", "枚举选择错误"],
  ["unsupported_inference", "存在无依据推断"],
  ["missing_evidence", "缺少可见证据"],
]

export type RowCorrectionSubmit = {
  nodeType: RowCorrectionTarget["nodeType"]
  nodePath: string
  oldValue: unknown
  newValue: unknown
  reason: string
  reasonCodes: string[]
}

/** rule_hit 的命中状态选项：未命中，或命中并给出人工置信度。
 * 注意不能写成 tuple 数组——契约脚本按 tuple 字面量识别归因码做同口径校验。 */
const RULE_HIT_CHOICES: ReadonlyArray<{ value: string; label: string }> = [
  { value: "miss", label: "未命中" },
  { value: "high", label: "命中 · 置信度高" },
  { value: "medium", label: "命中 · 置信度中" },
  { value: "low", label: "命中 · 置信度低" },
]

function isRuleHitObject(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value)
}

/** 把服务端存的原始值转成输入框里的初始文本 */
function initialText(target: RowCorrectionTarget): string {
  const raw = target.currentValue
  if (target.valueKind === "rule_hit") {
    return isRuleHitObject(raw) && typeof raw.evidence === "string"
      ? raw.evidence
      : ""
  }
  if (raw === null || raw === undefined) return ""
  if (Array.isArray(raw)) {
    return raw.map((item) => String(item ?? "")).filter(Boolean).join("、")
  }
  if (typeof raw === "object") return ""
  return String(raw)
}

function initialSelection(target: RowCorrectionTarget): string[] {
  const raw = target.currentValue
  if (target.valueKind === "rule_hit") {
    if (isRuleHitObject(raw)) {
      const confidence = typeof raw.confidence === "string" ? raw.confidence : ""
      const known = RULE_HIT_CHOICES.some(
        (choice) => choice.value === confidence && choice.value !== "miss",
      )
      return known ? [confidence] : ["high"]
    }
    return ["miss"]
  }
  if (Array.isArray(raw)) return raw.map((item) => String(item ?? "")).filter(Boolean)
  if (typeof raw === "string" && raw.trim()) return [raw.trim()]
  return []
}

/** 把界面输入折算回服务端期望的值形态；返回 null 表示输入不合法 */
function parseValue(
  target: RowCorrectionTarget,
  text: string,
  selection: string[],
): { ok: true; value: unknown } | { ok: false; message: string } {
  switch (target.valueKind) {
    case "integer": {
      const trimmed = text.trim()
      if (!trimmed) return { ok: false, message: "请填写一个整数" }
      if (!/^-?\d+$/.test(trimmed)) {
        return { ok: false, message: "必须是整数，不能带小数点或其它字符" }
      }
      const parsed = Number(trimmed)
      const min = target.minimum ?? Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY
      const max = target.maximum ?? Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY
      if (parsed < min || parsed > max) {
        return {
          ok: false,
          message: `必须在 ${target.minimum ?? "-∞"} 到 ${target.maximum ?? "∞"} 之间`,
        }
      }
      return { ok: true, value: parsed }
    }
    case "enum": {
      if (!selection.length) return { ok: false, message: "请选择一项" }
      return { ok: true, value: selection[0] }
    }
    case "multi_enum":
      // 允许空数组：清空「过滤原因」正是把误判的红线撤掉。
      return { ok: true, value: selection }
    case "string_list": {
      const items = text
        .split(/[，,、\n]/)
        .map((item) => item.trim())
        .filter(Boolean)
      return { ok: true, value: items }
    }
    case "rule_hit": {
      const choice = selection[0]
      if (!choice) return { ok: false, message: "请选择命中或未命中" }
      // 未命中即把这条命中从结果里撤掉，服务端按 null 删除并重算
      if (choice === "miss") return { ok: true, value: null }
      if (!target.ruleId) {
        return { ok: false, message: "该规则节点缺少 rule_id，无法提交，请刷新后重试" }
      }
      const evidence = text.trim()
      if (!evidence) {
        return { ok: false, message: "判定为命中时必须写明画面上的可见证据" }
      }
      return {
        ok: true,
        value: { rule_id: target.ruleId, confidence: choice, evidence },
      }
    }
    default: {
      const trimmed = text.trim()
      if (!trimmed) return { ok: false, message: "请填写正确结果" }
      return { ok: true, value: trimmed }
    }
  }
}

export function RowCorrectionDialog({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  rowLabel,
  modelValue,
  target,
  submitting,
  errorMessage,
  onSubmit,
}: {
  open: boolean
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void
  rowLabel: string
  /** 模型的原判断，展示用；提交时用 target.currentValue 做并发校验 */
  modelValue: string
  target: RowCorrectionTarget | null
  submitting?: boolean
  errorMessage?: string | null
  onSubmit: (payload: RowCorrectionSubmit) => void
}) {
  const returnFocusRef = useRef<HTMLElement | null>(null)
  const wasOpenRef = useRef(false)
  const [text, setText] = useState("")
  const [selection, setSelection] = useState<string[]>([])
  const [reasonCodes, setReasonCodes] = useState<string[]>([])
  const [note, setNote] = useState("")
  const [localError, setLocalError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  if (open && !wasOpenRef.current && typeof document !== "undefined") {
    returnFocusRef.current = document.activeElement instanceof HTMLElement
      ? document.activeElement
      : null
  }
  wasOpenRef.current = open

  // 每次打开都从模型原值重置，避免把上一条素材的输入带到下一条。
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open || !target) return
    setText(initialText(target))
    setSelection(initialSelection(target))
    setReasonCodes([])
    setNote("")
    setLocalError(null)
  }, [open, target])

  const options = useMemo(() => target?.options ?? [], [target])

  function toggle(list: string[], value: string): string[] {
    return list.includes(value)
      ? list.filter((item) => item !== value)
      : [...list, value]
  }

  function handleSubmit() {
    if (!target) return
    const parsed = parseValue(target, text, selection)
    if (!parsed.ok) {
      setLocalError(parsed.message)
      return
    }
    const trimmedNote = note.trim()
    if (!reasonCodes.length && !trimmedNote) {
      setLocalError("请至少选一个纠偏理由，或写明具体判断依据")
      return
    }
    setLocalError(null)
    onSubmit({
      nodeType: target.nodeType,
      nodePath: target.nodePath,
      oldValue: target.currentValue,
      newValue: parsed.value,
      // 理由文本是纠偏依据，也会作为新值的证据存入结果。
      reason: trimmedNote
        || reasonCodes
          .map((code) => REASON_CODES.find(([key]) => key === code)?.[1] ?? code)
          .join("；"),
      reasonCodes,
    })
  }

  const message = errorMessage ?? localError

  return (
    <Dialog.Root open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
      <Dialog.Portal>
        <Dialog.Overlay className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/20" />
        <Dialog.Content
          className="fixed left-1/2 top-1/2 z-50 flex max-h-[calc(100vh-2rem)] w-[min(560px,calc(100vw-2rem))] -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 flex-col border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white rounded-lg shadow-float focus:outline-none"
          onCloseAutoFocus={(event) => {
            event.preventDefault()
            returnFocusRef.current?.focus()
          }}
          data-testid="row-correction-dialog"
        >
          <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4 border-b border-[var(--line)] px-5 py-4">
            <div>
              <Dialog.Title className="visual-card-title">
                纠偏：{rowLabel}
              </Dialog.Title>
              <Dialog.Description className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
                {target?.recomputes
                  ? "保存后服务端会按冻结规则重算分数与等级。"
                  : "保存后只更新该字段，不触发分数重算。"}
              </Dialog.Description>
            </div>
            <Dialog.Close asChild>
              <Button variant="secondary" size="icon" aria-label="关闭纠偏弹窗">
                <X size={18} />
              </Button>
            </Dialog.Close>
          </div>

          <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 space-y-4 overflow-y-auto px-5 py-4">
            <div className="rounded-md border border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-051 px-3 py-2">
              <p className="font-data text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">模型判断</p>
              <p className="mt-0.5 text-sm">{modelValue}</p>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-bold" htmlFor="row-correction-value">
                正确结果
              </label>
              {target?.hint && (
                <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{target.hint}</p>
              )}
              <div className="mt-2">
                {target?.valueKind === "rule_hit" ? (
                  <div className="space-y-3">
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                      {RULE_HIT_CHOICES.map(({ value, label }) => {
                        const active = selection[0] === value
                        return (
                          <button
                            key={value}
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => setSelection([value])}
                            aria-pressed={active}
                            className={"visual-native-button " + (` border px-3 ${
                              active
                                ? "border-alias-edge-009 bg-active-surface text-active-text"
                                : "border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white hover:bg-alias-surface-051"
                            }`)}
                          >
                            {label}
                          </button>
                        )
                      })}
                    </div>
                    {selection[0] !== "miss" && (
                      <div>
                        <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                          命中证据：写明画面上可定位的内容，会作为这条命中的证据存入结果。
                        </p>
                        <Textarea
                          id="row-correction-value"
                          rows={3}
                          className="mt-1.5"
                          value={text}
                          onChange={(event) => setText(event.target.value)}
                        />
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                ) : target?.valueKind === "enum" || target?.valueKind === "multi_enum" ? (
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                    {options.length === 0 && (
                      <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                        合同未下发候选项，请联系机制侧补充。
                      </p>
                    )}
                    {options.map((option) => {
                      const active = selection.includes(option.value)
                      return (
                        <button
                          key={option.value}
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() =>
                            setSelection(
                              target.valueKind === "enum"
                                ? active ? [] : [option.value]
                                : toggle(selection, option.value),
                            )
                          }
                          aria-pressed={active}
                          className={"visual-native-button " + (` border px-3 ${
                            active
                              ? "border-alias-edge-009 bg-active-surface text-active-text"
                              : "border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white hover:bg-alias-surface-051"
                          }`)}
                        >
                          {option.label}
                        </button>
                      )
                    })}
                  </div>
                ) : target?.valueKind === "multiline" ? (
                  <Textarea
                    id="row-correction-value"
                    rows={3}
                    value={text}
                    onChange={(event) => setText(event.target.value)}
                  />
                ) : (
                  <Input
                    id="row-correction-value"
                    value={text}
                    inputMode={target?.valueKind === "integer" ? "numeric" : undefined}
                    onChange={(event) => setText(event.target.value)}
                  />
                )}
              </div>
              {target?.valueKind === "string_list" && (
                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                  多个值用中文逗号、英文逗号或换行分隔。
                </p>
              )}
            </div>

            <div>
              <p className="text-xs font-bold">纠偏理由</p>
              <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                归因码用于纠偏分析统计，可多选。
              </p>
              <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                {REASON_CODES.map(([code, label]) => {
                  const active = reasonCodes.includes(code)
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={code}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setReasonCodes(toggle(reasonCodes, code))}
                      aria-pressed={active}
                      className={"visual-native-button " + (` border px-2.5 ${
                        active
                          ? "border-alias-edge-009 bg-active-surface text-active-text"
                          : "border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white hover:bg-alias-surface-051"
                      }`)}
                    >
                      {label}
                    </button>
                  )
                })}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-bold" htmlFor="row-correction-note">
                判断依据
              </label>
              <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                写明画面上的可见证据；这段会作为人工结论的证据留存。
              </p>
              <Textarea
                id="row-correction-note"
                rows={3}
                className="mt-2"
                value={note}
                onChange={(event) => setNote(event.target.value)}
              />
            </div>

            {message && (
              <p
                role="alert"
                className="border border-alias-edge-041 bg-alias-surface-059 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-alias-content-036 rounded-md"
              >
                {message}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2 border-t border-[var(--line)] px-5 py-3">
            <Dialog.Close asChild>
              <Button variant="secondary">取消</Button>
            </Dialog.Close>
            <Button onClick={handleSubmit} disabled={submitting || !target}>
              {submitting ? "保存中…" : "保存纠偏"}
            </Button>
          </div>
        </Dialog.Content>
      </Dialog.Portal>
    </Dialog.Root>
  )
}

export { REASON_CODES, parseValue }

===== FILE frontend/src/features/failure-console/api.ts =====
import { api, jsonBody } from "@/lib/api"
import type { FailureResolutionFields, FailureResolutionForm } from "@/lib/types"

export type FailureResolution = {
  id: number
  level: string
  score: number
  fields: Record<string, unknown>
  reason: string
  actor: string
  outbox_event_id: number | null
  created_at: string | null
}

export type FailureRow = {
  kind: string
  production_run_id: number
  run_key: string
  status: string
  attempt_count: number
  category_key: string
  asset_id: number | null
  asset_name: string
  upstream: {
    task_id: number | null
    img_id: number | null
    ll_id: number | null
    class_id: number | null
    class_label: string
  }
  failed_step: string
  failed_step_status: string
  error_code: string
  error_message: string
  created_at: string | null
  updated_at: string | null
  /** 调用 A 跑成过就能预填字段；没跑成运营要自己填全。 */
  stage_a_available: boolean
  downstream_published: boolean
  submittable_levels: string[]
  reassessment?: { production_run_id: number; status: string; delivered: boolean } | null
  /** 同一素材后续有增量运行成功：失败已被补上 */
  superseded_by?: { production_run_id: number; status: string } | null
  resolution: FailureResolution | null
}

export type RecoveryPreview = {
  production_run_id: number; quote_hash: string; requests: number; max_additional_cost_micros: number
  classification: { kind: string; label: string; advice: string }; failed_step: string | null
  blockers: string[]; scheduler_attempts: number; model_calls: number; charged_calls: number
  calls: { id: number; step: string; status: string; error_code: string; request_id: string | null; actual_cost_micros: number; estimated_cost_micros: number }[]
}

export type ResolutionForm = FailureResolutionForm
export type ResolutionFields = FailureResolutionFields

export const failureApi = {
  list: (filters: { resolved?: boolean | null; search?: string | null; category_key?: string; limit?: number; offset?: number } = {}) => {
    const params = new URLSearchParams()
    if (filters.resolved !== undefined && filters.resolved !== null) {
      params.set("resolved", String(filters.resolved))
    }
    if (filters.search?.trim()) params.set("search", filters.search.trim())
    if (filters.category_key) params.set("category_key", filters.category_key)
    params.set("limit", String(filters.limit ?? 50))
    if (filters.offset) params.set("offset", String(filters.offset))
    return api<{ items: FailureRow[]; total: number; limit: number; offset: number; has_more: boolean }>(
      "/api/failures?" + params.toString(),
    )
  },
  recoveryPreview: (runId: number, requests: number) => api<RecoveryPreview>(`/api/failures/${runId}/recovery-preview?requests=${requests}`),
  recover: (runId: number, body: { quote_hash: string; requests: number; reason: string; confirmed: boolean }) =>
    api<{ production_run_id: number; status: string }>(`/api/failures/${runId}/recover`, { method: "POST", ...jsonBody(body) }),
  form: (runId: number) => api<ResolutionForm>(`/api/failures/${runId}/resolution-form`),
  retry: (runId: number, reason: string) => api<{ production_run_id: number; status: string }>(
    `/api/failures/${runId}/retry`,
    { method: "POST", ...jsonBody({ reason }) },
  ),
  resolve: (runId: number, body: {
    level: string
    score: number
    fields: ResolutionFields
    reason: string
    idempotency_key: string
  }) => api<FailureResolution>(
    `/api/failures/${runId}/resolution`,
    { method: "POST", ...jsonBody(body) },
  ),
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/failure-console/dead-letter-panel.tsx =====
import { useState } from "react"
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { ArrowClockwise } from "@phosphor-icons/react"

import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { deadLetterApi } from "@/lib/runtime-api"
import type { DeadLetterOverview, Job } from "@/lib/types"
import { pickedInList } from "./picked-selection"

const queueLabels: Record<Job["queue_class"], string> = {
  validation: "验证",
  interactive: "交互",
  production_batch: "生产批量",
  canary: "金丝雀",
  recovery: "恢复",
}


/** 失败待处理（不会自动重跑）。
 *
 *  两类分开摆，因为处置方式本质不同：运行时调度条目背后可能挂着已经计过费的模型调用，
 *  重排等于再花一次钱，所以只给「放弃」；下游投递按业务幂等键 + 修订幂等，重放不会
 *  产生第二条下游记录，所以「重放」才是它的主路径。两者都写审计。 */
export function DeadLetterPanel() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const deadLetters = useQuery({ queryKey: ["dead-letters"], queryFn: deadLetterApi.list, refetchInterval: 30_000 })
  const [runtimePicked, setRuntimePicked] = useState<number[]>([])
  const [outboxPicked, setOutboxPicked] = useState<number[]>([])
  const [reason, setReason] = useState("")
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState<string | null>(null)

  const refresh = () => {
    void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["dead-letters"] })
    void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["runtime-capacity"] })
  }
  const discardRuntime = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (ids: number[]) => deadLetterApi.discardRuntime({ ids, note: reason.trim().slice(0, 200) }),
    onSuccess: (result) => { setNotice(`已放弃 ${result.discarded} 条调度条目`); setRuntimePicked([]); refresh() },
    onError: (error: Error) => setNotice(`放弃失败：${error.message}`),
  })
  // 运营看完信息只有一个动作：重试。后端按 run 去重做 checkpoint 重试（成功节点不重放），
  // 并重开传输窗口——否则冻结策略按历史立刻再判 exhausted（2026-09-18 实证 37/40）。
  const retryRuntime = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (ids: number[]) => deadLetterApi.retryRuntime({ ids, note: reason.trim().slice(0, 200) }),
    onSuccess: (result) => {
      const failed = result.results.filter((item) => !item.ok)
      setNotice(`已重试 ${result.ok_count} 条运行${failed.length ? `，${failed.length} 条未重试：` + failed.map((item) => `run ${item.run_id ?? "?"} ${item.error ?? item.code ?? ""}`).join("；") : ""}`)
      setRuntimePicked([]); refresh()
    },
    onError: (error: Error) => setNotice(`重试失败：${error.message}`),
  })
  const outboxAction = useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ kind, eventIds }: { kind: "discard" | "replay"; eventIds: number[] }) => (kind === "replay" ? deadLetterApi.replayOutbox : deadLetterApi.discardOutbox)(
      { event_ids: eventIds, reason: reason.trim() },
    ),
    onSuccess: (result) => {
      const failed = result.results.filter((item) => !item.ok)
      setNotice(`成功 ${result.ok_count} 条，失败 ${result.failed_count} 条${failed.length ? "：" + failed.map((item) => `#${item.id} ${item.error ?? ""}`).join("；") : ""}`)
      setOutboxPicked([]); refresh()
    },
    onError: (error: Error) => setNotice(`处理失败：${error.message}`),
  })

  const data: DeadLetterOverview | undefined = deadLetters.data
  const runtimeItems = (data?.queues ?? []).flatMap((group) => group.items.map((item) => ({ ...item, queue_class: group.queue_class })))
  const outboxItems = data?.outbox.items ?? []
  // 自动刷新后已消失的条目不计数、不提交
  const runtimeSelected = pickedInList(runtimePicked, runtimeItems.map((item) => item.id))
  const outboxSelected = pickedInList(outboxPicked, outboxItems.map((item) => item.id))
  const nothing = !runtimeItems.length && !outboxItems.length
  const toggle = (list: number[], id: number) => (list.includes(id) ? list.filter((value) => value !== id) : [...list, id])
  const reasonMissing = !reason.trim()

  return (
    <section id="dead-letters" data-testid="dead-letters" className="rounded-lg shadow-card border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-4 px-5 py-5 md:px-6">
        <div>
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">运行维护</p>
          <h2 className="visual-card-title mt-1">调度与下游投递异常</h2>
          <p className="mt-2 max-w-3xl text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
            这些任务已经用完自动重试、停在这里等人确认。<b>运行时调度</b>可以按运行重试：只重跑失败的那一步、已成功的不重放，并重新给它一轮批量机会（可能再产生一次模型费用），也可以放弃；
            <b>下游投递</b>按业务幂等键 + 修订幂等，重放不会产生第二条下游记录，可以直接重放。所有操作都会写审计，重试和重放都要填原因。
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Badge tone={nothing ? "success" : "warning"}>{nothing ? "当前没有待处理" : `共 ${runtimeItems.length + outboxItems.length} 条`}</Badge>
          <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={() => refresh()}><ArrowClockwise />刷新</Button>
        </div>
      </div>
      {deadLetters.isError && <p role="alert" className="px-5 pb-4 text-sm text-danger md:px-6">读取失败：{deadLetters.error.message}</p>}
      {notice && <p role="status" className="px-5 pb-4 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)] md:px-6">{notice}</p>}
      <div className="border-t border-[var(--line)] px-5 py-4 md:px-6">
        <label className="text-xs font-bold" htmlFor="dead-letter-reason">处理原因（重试与下游投递必填，会写进审计）</label>
        <input
          id="dead-letter-reason"
          value={reason}
          onChange={(event) => setReason(event.target.value)}
          maxLength={200}
          placeholder="例如：上游 404 已修复，重放这批投递"
          className="visual-native-input mt-2 w-full"
        />
      </div>
      <div className="grid gap-px border-t border-[var(--line)] bg-[var(--line)] lg:grid-cols-2">
        <div className="bg-white px-5 py-5 md:px-6">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <p className="text-xs font-bold">运行时调度（{runtimeItems.length} 条）</p>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <Button size="sm" disabled={!runtimeSelected.length || reasonMissing || retryRuntime.isPending || discardRuntime.isPending}
                onClick={() => retryRuntime.mutate(runtimeSelected)}>重试所选（{runtimeSelected.length}）</Button>
              <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled={!runtimeSelected.length || retryRuntime.isPending || discardRuntime.isPending}
                onClick={() => discardRuntime.mutate(runtimeSelected)}>放弃所选（{runtimeSelected.length}）</Button>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="mt-3 max-h-72 overflow-y-auto">
            {runtimeItems.map((item) => (
              <label key={item.id} className="visual-native-choice min-w-0 flex items-start gap-2 border-b border-[var(--line)] py-2 text-xs">
                <input type="checkbox" className="visual-native-checkbox" checked={runtimePicked.includes(item.id)}
                  onChange={() => setRuntimePicked((list) => toggle(list, item.id))} />
                <span className="min-w-0">
                  <span className="visual-code">#{item.id}</span>{item.run_id != null ? <> · run {item.run_id}</> : null} · {queueLabels[item.queue_class as Job["queue_class"]] ?? item.queue_class} · 已试 {item.attempt_count} 次
                  <span className="block break-all text-[var(--muted)]">{item.last_error_code || "未记录错误码"} · {item.dead_lettered_at ?? "时间未知"}</span>
                </span>
              </label>
            ))}
            {!runtimeItems.length && <p className="py-3 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">没有运行时调度条目。</p>}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-white px-5 py-5 md:px-6">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <p className="text-xs font-bold">下游投递（{outboxItems.length} 条，可重放）</p>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <Button size="sm" disabled={!outboxSelected.length || reasonMissing || outboxAction.isPending}
                onClick={() => outboxAction.mutate({ kind: "replay", eventIds: outboxSelected })}>重放（{outboxSelected.length}）</Button>
              <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled={!outboxSelected.length || reasonMissing || outboxAction.isPending}
                onClick={() => outboxAction.mutate({ kind: "discard", eventIds: outboxSelected })}>放弃</Button>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="mt-3 max-h-72 overflow-y-auto">
            {outboxItems.map((item) => (
              <label key={item.id} className="visual-native-choice min-w-0 flex items-start gap-2 border-b border-[var(--line)] py-2 text-xs">
                <input type="checkbox" className="visual-native-checkbox" checked={outboxPicked.includes(item.id)}
                  onChange={() => setOutboxPicked((list) => toggle(list, item.id))} />
                <span className="min-w-0">
                  <span className="visual-code">#{item.id}</span> · {item.target_contract_key} · 修订 {item.revision ?? "—"} · 已试 {item.attempt_count} 次
                  <span className="block break-all text-[var(--muted)]">{item.business_idempotency_key ?? "无幂等键"}</span>
                  <span className="block break-all text-[var(--muted)]">{item.last_error_code || "未记录错误码"} · {item.dead_lettered_at ?? "时间未知"}</span>
                </span>
              </label>
            ))}
            {!outboxItems.length && <p className="py-3 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">没有下游投递条目。</p>}
          </div>
          {reasonMissing && !!outboxSelected.length && <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-warning">下游投递的放弃与重放都要填原因。</p>}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/failure-console/invocation-review-panel.tsx =====
import { useState } from "react"
import { useMutation, useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { toast } from "sonner"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea"
import { api, jsonBody } from "@/lib/api"
import type { InvocationReviewRow, InvocationReviewRequest } from "@/lib/types"

type Page = { items: InvocationReviewRow[]; total: number; offset: number; has_more: boolean; review_allowed: boolean }
const categories: Record<string, string> = { inspiration_image: "灵感图", model_3d_su: "3D & SU", knowledge_graph: "知识图谱", space_image: "空间图片", material_image: "材质图", design_case: "设计案例" }
const statuses: Record<string, string> = { succeeded: "已完成", failed: "失败", canceled: "已取消", running: "执行中", queued: "等待执行", paused: "已暂停", retryable: "等待重试" }
const steps: Record<string, string> = { prompt_a: "提示词 A", prompt_b: "提示词 B" }

export function InvocationReviewPanel({ categoryKey }: { categoryKey: string }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const [reviewed, setReviewed] = useState(false)
  const [offset, setOffset] = useState(0)
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<number | null>(null)
  const list = useQuery({ queryKey: ["invocation-reviews", categoryKey, reviewed, offset], enabled: open,
    queryFn: () => api<Page>(`/api/failures/invocations?${new URLSearchParams({ category_key: categoryKey, reviewed: String(reviewed), offset: String(offset), limit: "25" })}`) })
  return <section className="rounded-lg shadow-card border border-[var(--line)] bg-white p-card sm:p-card">
    <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setOpen(!open)}>历史未知调用核验{open ? " · 收起" : ""}</Button>
    {open && <div className="mt-4 space-y-4">
      <p className="max-w-3xl text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">运行已结束，供应商调用仍可能留下未核实记录。管理员须先核实请求已结束，再登记证据解除历史运行阻塞；原结果和费用责任保留，费用仍待对账，不触发重试。</p>
      <label className="visual-native-choice min-w-0 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm"><input className="visual-native-checkbox" type="checkbox" checked={reviewed} onChange={e => { setReviewed(e.target.checked); setOffset(0); setSelected(null) }} />查看已登记核验</label>
      {list.isPending && <p>读取调用记录…</p>}
      {list.isError && <p role="alert">读取失败：{list.error.message}</p>}
      {list.data && <>
        <p className="text-sm">共 {list.data.total} 条 · 当前 {offset + (list.data.items.length ? 1 : 0)}–{offset + list.data.items.length}</p>
        <div className="divide-y divide-[var(--line)]">{list.data.items.map(row => <div key={row.id} className="space-y-2 py-4">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1 break-words text-sm">
              <p className="font-bold">调用 #{row.id} · 运行 {row.production_run_id} · {steps[row.step] ?? row.step}</p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-[var(--muted)]">{categories[row.category_key] ?? row.category_key} · {row.provider === "local_qwen" ? "本地 Qwen" : row.provider} · {statuses[row.run_status] ?? row.run_status}</p>
              <p className="mt-1 break-all">请求号：{row.request_id || "供应商未提供，请使用时间和服务日志核验"}</p>
              <p className="mt-1">调用预留 ¥{(row.estimated_cost_micros / 1_000_000).toFixed(4)} · 实际费用待对账</p>
            </div>
            {!row.review && row.can_review && list.data.review_allowed && <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => setSelected(selected === row.id ? null : row.id)}>登记核验</Button>}
          </div>
          {!row.can_review && !row.review && <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">运行或调用尚未终止，暂不可核验关闭。</p>}
          {row.review && <p className="break-words text-sm">已由 {row.review.actor} 登记：{row.review.reason}<br />依据：{row.review.evidence_reference}<br />费用仍待核对。</p>}
          {selected === row.id && <ReviewForm key={row.id} row={row} onDone={() => { setSelected(null); void list.refetch() }} />}
        </div>)}</div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2"><Button variant="secondary" size="sm" disabled={offset === 0 || list.isFetching} onClick={() => { setOffset(Math.max(0, offset - 25)); setSelected(null) }}>上一页</Button><Button variant="secondary" size="sm" disabled={!list.data.has_more || list.isFetching} onClick={() => { setOffset(offset + 25); setSelected(null) }}>下一页</Button></div>
      </>}
    </div>}
  </section>
}

function ReviewForm({ row, onDone }: { row: InvocationReviewRow; onDone: () => void }) {
  const [reference, setReference] = useState("")
  const [reason, setReason] = useState("")
  const [ended, setEnded] = useState(false)
  const [liability, setLiability] = useState(false)
  const save = useMutation({ mutationFn: () => {
    const body: InvocationReviewRequest = { quote_hash: row.quote_hash, evidence_reference: reference,
      reason, execution_ended_confirmed: ended, liability_retained_confirmed: liability }
    return api(`/api/failures/invocations/${row.id}/review`, { method: "POST", ...jsonBody(body) })
  }, onSuccess: () => { toast.success("核验已登记，原费用责任保留"); onDone() }, onError: error => toast.error(error.message) })
  return <div className="max-w-3xl space-y-3 border border-[var(--line)] p-4 text-sm rounded-md">
    <label className="block">核验依据（完整文件路径、工单号或供应商回执）<Input className="mt-1" value={reference} onChange={e => setReference(e.target.value)} maxLength={1000} /></label>
    <label className="block">核验结论<Textarea className="mt-1" value={reason} onChange={e => setReason(e.target.value)} maxLength={1000} /></label>
    <label className="visual-native-choice min-w-0 flex items-start gap-2"><input className="visual-native-checkbox" type="checkbox" checked={ended} onChange={e => setEnded(e.target.checked)} />我已核实该供应商请求执行结束，没有在途请求。</label>
    <label className="visual-native-choice min-w-0 flex items-start gap-2"><input className="visual-native-checkbox" type="checkbox" checked={liability} onChange={e => setLiability(e.target.checked)} />保留原调用和可能发生的费用责任，后续仍需对账。</label>
    <Button size="sm" disabled={!reference.trim() || !reason.trim() || !ended || !liability || save.isPending} onClick={() => save.mutate()}>核验并登记</Button>
  </div>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/failure-console/picked-selection.ts =====
/**
 * 勾选与当前列表求交集：列表 30 秒自动刷新后，已被别人处理掉、不在列表里的条目
 * 不能再计入“所选”数量，也不能被提交。保持勾选顺序。
 */
export function pickedInList(picked: readonly number[], listIds: Iterable<number>): number[] {
  const present = new Set(listIds)
  return picked.filter((id) => present.has(id))
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/incremental-automation/automation-panel.tsx =====
import { useState } from "react"
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { Link } from "react-router-dom"
import { ArrowRight, ArrowClockwise } from "@phosphor-icons/react"

import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { api, jsonBody } from "@/lib/api"
import type { DoubaoEndpointBinding, IncrementalAutomationCard, IncrementalAutomationCardsResponse, IncrementalCostSummary } from "@/lib/types"
import { RoutingControlPanel } from "./routing-control-panel"
import { IncrementalObservabilityPanel } from "./observability-panel"
import { IncrementalOperationsPanel } from "./operations-panel"
import { ServiceClassControl } from "./service-class-control"
import { formatPlatformTime } from "../../lib/platform-time"

const root = "/api/incremental-mechanisms/automation"
const queryKey = ["incremental-automation-cards"]
const costsKey = ["incremental-automation-costs"]
const stateLabels = { paused: "已暂停运行", blocked: "待就绪", active: "有在途任务", waiting: "等待增量", attention: "有素材待处理" }
const runStatusLabels: Record<string, string> = { planned: "已计划", queued: "排队中", running: "运行中", waiting_external: "等待外部结果", paused: "已暂停", succeeded: "成功", failed: "失败", retryable: "等待重试", blocked: "已阻塞", canceled: "已取消" }
const timestamp = (value: string | null) => value ? formatPlatformTime(value.endsWith("Z") || /[+-]\d\d:\d\d$/.test(value) ? value : `${value}Z`) : "暂无运行"

export function IncrementalAutomationPanel() {
  const [category, setCategory] = useState("")
  const client = useQueryClient()
  const cards = useQuery({ queryKey, queryFn: () => api<IncrementalAutomationCardsResponse>(`${root}/cards?include_costs=false`), refetchInterval: 5000 })
  const items = cards.data?.items ?? []
  const categories = [...new Map(items.map(item => [item.category_key, item.category_name])).entries()]
  const visible = items.filter(item => !category || item.category_key === category)
  return <section aria-label="增量自动化链路管理" className="automation-workspace workspace-list-panel space-y-5">
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-4 border-b border-[var(--line-strong)] pb-4">
      <div><h2 className="visual-card-title">自动化链路</h2><p className="mt-2 text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">按类目和工作流独立查看、启停与追踪增量运行。</p></div>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
        <label className="text-xs font-bold">类目筛选 <select className="visual-native-select w-auto max-w-full ml-2" value={category} onChange={event => setCategory(event.target.value)}><option value="">全部类目</option>{categories.map(([key, name]) => <option key={key} value={key}>{name}</option>)}</select></label>
        <Button variant="secondary" disabled={cards.isFetching} onClick={() => { void cards.refetch(); void client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: costsKey }) }}><ArrowClockwise />刷新</Button>
      </div>
    </div>
    {cards.isError && <p role="alert" className="border border-red-200 p-4 text-sm text-danger rounded-md">自动化状态读取失败：{cards.error.message}。已显示数据可能过期，刷新成功后才能操作。</p>}
    {cards.isPending && <p role="status" className="py-8 text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">正在读取自动化链路…</p>}
    {cards.data && <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">运行数、成功及失败按最近 {cards.data.window_hours} 小时统计；在途与待派发为当前全部积压。更新于 {timestamp(cards.data.updated_at)}。自动运行开关同时控制受理与派发，在途任务继续执行。</p>}
    {!cards.isPending && !cards.isError && !visible.length && <div className="border border-[var(--line)] p-8 rounded-md"><p className="text-sm">{category ? "该类目暂无增量自动化工作流。" : "暂无声明增量用途的工作流。"}</p><Button className="mt-4" variant="secondary" asChild><Link to="/workflow/governance/workflow-manager">前往工作流管理<ArrowRight /></Link></Button></div>}
    <div className="automation-card-grid grid items-stretch gap-5 xl:grid-cols-2">{visible.map(card => <AutomationCard key={`${card.category_key}:${card.workflow_definition_id}`} card={card} canControl={Boolean(cards.data?.can_control) && !cards.isError} />)}</div>
  </section>
}

function AutomationCard({ card: state, canControl }: { card: IncrementalAutomationCard; canControl: boolean }) {
  const costs = useQuery({
    queryKey: [...costsKey, state.category_key, state.workflow_definition_id],
    queryFn: () => api<{ costs: IncrementalCostSummary; updated_at: string }>(
      `${root}/${encodeURIComponent(state.category_key)}/${state.workflow_definition_id}/costs`),
    refetchInterval: 30_000,
    staleTime: 30_000,
  })
  const card = { ...state, costs: costs.data?.costs ?? state.costs }
  const [intent, setIntent] = useState<{ enabled: boolean; expected_revision: number; expected_routing_revision: number } | null>(null)
  const [reason, setReason] = useState("")
  const client = useQueryClient()
  const mutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => api(`${root}/${encodeURIComponent(card.category_key)}/${card.workflow_definition_id}/operation`, { method: "PUT", ...jsonBody({ ...intent, reason }) }),
    onSuccess: async () => { setIntent(null); setReason(""); await client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey }) },
    onError: async () => { await client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey }) },
  })
  const link = `/workflow/governance/workflow-manager?${new URLSearchParams({ category_key: card.category_key, workflow_key: card.workflow_key })}`
  const revisionChanged = intent !== null && (intent.expected_revision !== card.revision || intent.expected_routing_revision !== (card.routing.policy?.revision ?? 0))
  return <article aria-label={`${card.category_name} · ${card.workflow_name}`} className="automation-card rounded-lg min-w-0 border border-[var(--line)] bg-white">
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3 border-b border-[var(--line)] p-5">
      <div className="min-w-0"><p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">{card.category_name}</p><h3 className="visual-item-title mt-2 break-words">{card.workflow_name}</h3><p className="mt-2 break-words text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{card.bound ? "当前接入版本" : "最近生成版本"} {card.workflow_version} · {card.execution_mode === "real" ? "真实模型执行" : "模拟执行"}</p></div>
      <Badge tone={card.state === "blocked" || card.state === "attention" ? "warning" : card.state === "active" ? "success" : "neutral"}>{stateLabels[card.state]}</Badge>
    </div>
    <div className="automation-card-body space-y-4 p-5">
      <ServiceClassControl
        categoryKey={card.category_key}
        categoryName={card.category_name}
        serviceClass={card.service_class}
        source={card.service_class_source}
        lockVersion={card.mechanism_lock_version}
        canControl={canControl}
        queryKey={queryKey}
      />
      <dl className="grid gap-3 text-xs sm:grid-cols-2"><div><dt className="text-[var(--muted)]">上游步骤</dt><dd className="mt-1 break-words leading-5">{card.upstream.join("、") || "未配置"}</dd></div><div><dt className="text-[var(--muted)]">下游步骤</dt><dd className="mt-1 break-words leading-5">{card.downstream.join("、") || "未配置"}</dd></div></dl>
      <div className="automation-cost-region">
      <EndpointCostStrip card={card} />
      <div className="automation-cost-status">
      {costs.isPending && <p role="status" className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">正在读取费用，运行状态已加载。</p>}
      {costs.data && <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">费用更新于 {timestamp(costs.data.updated_at)} · 每 30 秒刷新{costs.isFetching ? " · 刷新中…" : ""}</p>}
      {costs.isError && <p role="alert" className="text-xs text-danger">费用读取失败{costs.data ? "，显示上次成功读取的费用" : ""}。<button type="button" className="visual-native-button ml-2 font-bold underline" disabled={costs.isFetching} onClick={() => void costs.refetch()}>重试费用</button></p>}
      </div>
      </div>
      <div className="automation-card-details space-y-4">
      <RoutingControlPanel card={card} canControl={canControl} />
      {card.metrics.admission_backlog > 0 && <p className="text-xs leading-5">暂停期间登记的待受理事件：{card.metrics.admission_backlog} 条。自动运行开启后由后台分批补处理，旧源版本已被更新的事件不再执行。</p>}
      <DispatchOnlyControl card={card} canControl={canControl} />
      {card.metrics.waiting_material > 0 && <p className="border-l-2 border-warning pl-3 text-xs leading-5">当前类目有 {card.metrics.waiting_material} 个业务实体等待素材或案例齐备；尚未派发模型调用。素材齐备后的新源版本可继续接入，也可按事件 ID 补受理。</p>}
      <IncrementalObservabilityPanel card={card} canControl={canControl} />
      <IncrementalOperationsPanel card={card} canControl={canControl} />
      {card.enable_blocker && <p className="border-l-2 border-[var(--line-strong)] pl-3 text-xs leading-5">{card.enable_blocker}</p>}
      {card.control_source === "default_open" && <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">尚未设置独立暂停，是否执行仍取决于各运行的冻结策略。关闭可同时暂停本工作流所有旧版本的待派发任务。</p>}
      <dl className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-px bg-[var(--line)]">
        {[["运行数", card.metrics.runs], ["成功", card.metrics.succeeded], ["失败/阻塞", card.metrics.failed], ["在途", card.metrics.in_flight], ["待派发", card.metrics.pending], ["最老等待", card.metrics.oldest_wait_seconds === null ? "—" : `${Math.ceil(card.metrics.oldest_wait_seconds / 60)} 分钟`]].map(([label, value]) => <div key={label} className="bg-white py-3 pl-2"><dt className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</dt><dd className="mt-2 font-data text-lg font-bold">{value}</dd></div>)}
      </dl>
      <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">最近 24 小时已组包接入 {card.metrics.received} 条 · 最近运行：{timestamp(card.last_run_at)}</p>
      {card.metrics.blocked_inputs > 0 && <details className="visual-native-details border-l-2 border-warning pl-3 text-xs"><summary className="cursor-pointer font-bold">当前 {card.metrics.blocked_inputs} 条素材在运行前被隔离</summary><p className="mt-2 leading-5">这些素材尚未创建运行，未计入上方运行失败数。启停开关不会自动修复隔离原因。</p>{card.blocked_examples.map(item => <p key={item.package_id} className="mt-2 break-words">素材包 #{item.package_id} · 冻结版本 ID {item.workflow_version_id} · {item.error_code === "incremental_snapshot_invalid" ? "增量快照损坏" : item.error_code}</p>)}<p className="mt-2 text-[var(--muted)]">展示最近 5 条；可按素材包 ID 在接入记录中追溯。</p></details>}
      <details className="visual-native-details border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-3"><summary className="cursor-pointer text-xs font-bold">运行记录与冻结版本</summary><div className="mt-3 space-y-3">
        {!card.recent_runs.length && <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">暂无自动运行记录。</p>}
        {card.recent_runs.map(run => <div key={run.run_key} className="border-b border-[var(--line)] pb-3 text-xs"><p className="break-all visual-code">{run.run_key}</p><p className="mt-1 text-[var(--muted)]">{runStatusLabels[run.status] ?? "未知状态"} · 冻结工作流版本 ID {run.workflow_version_id} · {timestamp(run.created_at)}</p>{run.error_message && <p className="mt-1 break-words text-danger">{run.error_message}</p>}</div>)}
        <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">仅展示最近 5 次自动运行；费用及下游读回明细请在运行中心核对。</p>
        <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" asChild><Link to="/workflow/operations">运行中心<ArrowRight /></Link></Button>
      </div></details>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3"><span className="text-xs font-bold">自动运行：{card.operation_enabled ? "开启" : "关闭"}</span><Button size="sm" variant={card.operation_enabled ? "secondary" : "primary"} disabled={!canControl || mutation.isPending || (!card.operation_enabled && Boolean(card.enable_blocker))} aria-label={`${card.operation_enabled ? "关闭" : "开启"}${card.workflow_name}自动运行`} onClick={() => { mutation.reset(); setIntent(intent ? null : { enabled: !card.operation_enabled, expected_revision: card.revision, expected_routing_revision: card.routing.policy?.revision ?? 0 }) }}>{card.operation_enabled ? "关闭" : "开启"}</Button></div>
        <div className="flex gap-2"><Button variant="secondary" size="sm" asChild><Link to={`/workflow/incremental/dashboard?${new URLSearchParams({ category_key: card.category_key, workflow_definition_id: String(card.workflow_definition_id) })}`}>数据看板<ArrowRight /></Link></Button>
        <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" asChild><Link to={link}>工作流管理<ArrowRight /></Link></Button>
        </div>
      </div>
      {!canControl && <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">当前仅可查看；控制操作需要管理员权限及有效状态数据。</p>}
      <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">派发{card.enabled ? "已开启" : "已关闭"} · {card.routing.policy?.admission_paused ? "受理已暂停" : "受理已开启"}。上游通过事件推送接入；配置自动拉取间隔的来源由后台持续轮询。</p>
      {intent && <form className="space-y-3 border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-4" onSubmit={event => { event.preventDefault(); if (!revisionChanged) mutation.mutate() }}>
        <label className="block text-xs font-bold">操作原因<Input className="mt-2" value={reason} onChange={event => setReason(event.target.value)} maxLength={2000} required placeholder="记录本次启停的原因" /></label>
        <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">{!intent.enabled ? "关闭后停止创建新运行，接入事件与积压保留，在途任务继续；已配置路由策略的链路同时暂停新受理，不会自动切换到旧平台。" : "开启后持续受理与派发，并自动补处理暂停期间登记的事件；继续受冻结工作流及执行额度约束。"}</p>
        {revisionChanged && <p role="alert" className="text-xs text-danger">开关或受理策略已更新，请取消后重新选择操作。</p>}
        <div className="flex gap-2"><Button size="sm" disabled={!reason.trim() || !canControl || mutation.isPending || revisionChanged}>{mutation.isPending ? "提交中…" : "确认操作"}</Button><Button size="sm" variant="secondary" type="button" disabled={mutation.isPending} onClick={() => setIntent(null)}>取消</Button></div>
      </form>}
      {mutation.isError && <p role="alert" className="text-xs text-danger">操作未完成：{mutation.error.message}</p>}
      </div>
    </div>
  </article>
}

const money = (micros: number) => `¥${(micros / 1_000_000).toFixed(4)}`

const verificationTone = (status: DoubaoEndpointBinding["verification_status"]) =>
  status === "verified" ? "success" as const : "warning" as const
const verificationText = (status: DoubaoEndpointBinding["verification_status"]) =>
  status === "verified" ? "已验收" : status === "failed" ? "验收失败" : "未验收"

/** 接入点、近 24 小时实时调用量、缓存命中与已结算费用：渠道成本归因的第一眼信息。
 *
 *  显示的是解析出来的实际路由，不是绑定表上的在线位——钉死批量的工作流在这里要看到
 *  批量接入点，在线那条只作为兜底位单列。 */
function EndpointCostStrip({ card }: { card: IncrementalAutomationCard }) {
  const costs = card.costs
  const totals = costs ? (["shadow", "formal", "legacy"] as const).map(key => costs.by_destination[key]) : []
  const calls = totals.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.calls, 0)
  const settled = totals.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.settled_micros, 0)
  const unresolved = totals.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.unresolved_calls, 0)
  const hit = costs?.cache?.hit_ratio
  const route = card.doubao_route
  const local = Boolean(card.local_models?.length)
  return <div className="automation-cost-strip border-y border-[var(--line)] py-3 text-xs">
    <div className="automation-cost-route">
    <p className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
      <span className="font-bold">{local ? "本地模型" : "豆包接入点"}</span>
      {local ? <><Badge tone="neutral">本地推理</Badge><span className="break-all">{card.local_models!.join("、")}</span></> : route?.error
        ? <span role="alert" className="text-warning">路由未就绪：{route.error}</span>
        : route?.primary
          ? <><Badge tone="neutral">{route.mode === "batch" ? "日常批量" : "在线"}</Badge><span className="visual-code break-all">{route.primary.endpoint_id}</span><span className="text-[var(--muted)]">{route.primary.display_name}</span><Badge tone={verificationTone(route.primary.verification_status)}>{verificationText(route.primary.verification_status)}</Badge></>
          : <span className="text-[var(--muted)]">未绑定，费用按模型 ID 直连归因</span>}
    </p>
    {route?.fallback && <p className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-[var(--muted)]">
      <span>在线兜底</span><span className="visual-code break-all">{route.fallback.endpoint_id}</span><span>{route.fallback.display_name}</span>
      <span>批量重试打满或素材入队满 24 小时后转到这里，按在线费率计费。</span>
    </p>}
    </div>
    <dl className="mt-2 grid grid-cols-3 gap-px bg-[var(--line)]">
      {[["近 24h 调用", costs ? `${calls} 次${unresolved ? `（未决 ${unresolved}）` : ""}` : "—"], ["缓存命中", !costs ? "—" : hit === null || hit === undefined ? "无输入 token" : `${(hit * 100).toFixed(1)}%`]].map(([label, value]) => <div key={label} className="min-w-0 bg-white py-2 pl-2"><dt className="text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</dt><dd className="mt-1 break-words font-data font-bold">{value}</dd></div>)}
      <div className="min-w-0 bg-white py-2 pl-2"><dt className="text-[var(--muted)]">{local ? "本地运行成本" : "已结算费用"}</dt><dd className="mt-1 break-words font-data font-bold">{local ? <Link className="underline underline-offset-4" to="/workflow/governance/model-registry#hardware-cost">查看整机估算</Link> : costs ? money(settled) : "—"}</dd></div>
    </dl>
  </div>
}

function DispatchOnlyControl({ card, canControl }: { card: IncrementalAutomationCard; canControl: boolean }) {
  const [reason, setReason] = useState("")
  const client = useQueryClient()
  const mutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => api(`${root}/${encodeURIComponent(card.category_key)}/${card.workflow_definition_id}`, { method: "PUT", ...jsonBody({ enabled: !card.enabled, expected_revision: card.revision, reason }) }),
    onSuccess: async () => { setReason(""); await client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey }) },
    onError: async () => { await client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey }) },
  })
  return <details className="visual-native-details border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-3"><summary className="cursor-pointer text-xs font-bold">高级控制：仅调整派发</summary><form className="mt-3 space-y-3" onSubmit={event => { event.preventDefault(); mutation.mutate() }}>
    <p className="text-xs leading-5">受理状态保持当前设置。派发{card.enabled ? "已开启" : "已关闭"}；在途继续执行。</p>
    <Input aria-label="独立派发操作原因" placeholder="填写独立派发操作原因" value={reason} onChange={event => setReason(event.target.value)} required maxLength={2000} />
    <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled={!canControl || !reason.trim() || mutation.isPending || (!card.enabled && Boolean(card.enable_blocker))}>{card.enabled ? "仅关闭派发" : "仅开启派发"}</Button>
    {mutation.isError && <p role="alert" className="text-xs text-danger">{mutation.error.message}</p>}
  </form></details>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/incremental-automation/observability-panel.tsx =====
import { useState } from "react"
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { api, jsonBody } from "@/lib/api"
import type { IncrementalAutomationCard, IncrementalEntityTrace } from "@/lib/types"

const root = "/api/incremental-mechanisms/automation"
const cardsKey = ["incremental-automation-cards"]
const money = (micros: number | null) => micros === null ? "未结算" : `¥${(micros / 1_000_000).toFixed(4)}`
const destinationLabels = { shadow: "影子运行", formal: "正式运行", legacy: "旧链路" }

export function IncrementalObservabilityPanel({ card, canControl }: { card: IncrementalAutomationCard; canControl: boolean }) {
  const costs = card.costs
  const base = `${root}/${encodeURIComponent(card.category_key)}/${card.workflow_definition_id}`
  const [sourceSystem, setSourceSystem] = useState("")
  const [entityId, setEntityId] = useState("")
  const [request, setRequest] = useState<{ sourceSystem: string; entityId: string; beforeId?: number } | null>(null)
  const [recovery, setRecovery] = useState<{ revision: number } | null>(null)
  const [eventIds, setEventIds] = useState("")
  const [reason, setReason] = useState("")
  const client = useQueryClient()
  const trace = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["incremental-entity-trace", card.category_key, card.workflow_definition_id, request],
    enabled: Boolean(request),
    queryFn: () => {
      const params = new URLSearchParams({ source_system: request!.sourceSystem, entity_id: request!.entityId })
      if (request!.beforeId) params.set("before_id", String(request!.beforeId))
      return api<IncrementalEntityTrace>(`${base}/trace?${params}`)
    },
  })
  const ids = eventIds.split(/[\s,，]+/).filter(Boolean)
  const idsValid = ids.length >= 1 && ids.length <= 100 && ids.every(value => /^\d+$/.test(value) && Number(value) > 0)
  const stale = Boolean(recovery && card.routing.policy?.revision !== recovery.revision)
  const recover = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => api(`${base}/recover`, { method: "POST", ...jsonBody({ expected_revision: recovery!.revision, event_ids: ids.map(Number), reason: reason.trim() }) }),
    onSuccess: async () => { setRecovery(null); setEventIds(""); setReason(""); await Promise.all([client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: cardsKey }), trace.refetch()]) },
    onError: async () => { await client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: cardsKey }) },
  })

  return <details className="visual-native-details border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-3">
    <summary className="cursor-pointer text-xs font-bold">费用与业务链路追踪</summary>
    <div className="mt-4 space-y-4">
      {costs && <div><h4 className="visual-item-title">近 {costs.window_hours} 小时调用费用</h4><dl className="mt-2 grid grid-cols-3 gap-px bg-[var(--line)]">{(["shadow", "formal", "legacy"] as const).map(key => { const item = costs.by_destination[key]; return <div className="bg-white p-3" key={key}><dt className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{destinationLabels[key]}</dt><dd className="mt-1 font-data text-sm font-bold">已结算 {money(item.settled_micros)}</dd><dd className="mt-1 text-xs">{item.calls} 次（未决 {item.unresolved_calls} 次）· 预留估算 {money(item.reserved_estimate_micros)}</dd></div> })}</dl><p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">{costs.precision}。来源：模型调用账本；与火山用量的按日对账见系统管理「成本对账」，此处金额不能作为发票实际金额。</p></div>}
      {!costs && <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">费用尚未读到，读取状态见卡片上方。</p>}
      <form className="grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-[1fr_1fr_auto]" onSubmit={event => { event.preventDefault(); setRequest({ sourceSystem: sourceSystem.trim(), entityId: entityId.trim() }) }}>
        <label className="text-xs font-bold">来源系统<Input className="mt-1" value={sourceSystem} onChange={event => setSourceSystem(event.target.value)} required /></label>
        <label className="text-xs font-bold">业务实体 ID<Input className="mt-1" value={entityId} onChange={event => setEntityId(event.target.value)} required /></label>
        <Button className="self-end" size="sm" disabled={!sourceSystem.trim() || !entityId.trim() || trace.isFetching}>查询业务链路</Button>
      </form>
      {trace.isFetching && <p role="status" className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">正在读取追踪证据…</p>}
      {trace.isError && <p role="alert" className="text-xs text-danger">追踪读取失败：{trace.error.message}</p>}
      {trace.data && <div className="space-y-3 text-xs">
        <p>{trace.data.assignment ? `归属 #${trace.data.assignment.assignment_id} · ${trace.data.assignment.destination} · epoch ${trace.data.assignment.ownership_epoch}` : "该实体尚无路由归属记录"}</p>
        {!trace.data.events.length && <p className="text-[var(--muted)]">暂无接入事件。</p>}
        {trace.data.events.map(item => <details key={item.id} className="visual-native-details border rounded-md border-[var(--line)] p-3"><summary className="cursor-pointer font-bold">事件 #{item.id} · {item.event_id} · {item.status}</summary><div className="mt-3 space-y-3">{item.runs.map(run => <div className="border-l-2 border-[var(--line)] pl-3" key={run.run_key}><p className="break-all visual-code font-bold">{run.run_key}</p><p className="mt-1">{run.status} · 版本 ID {run.workflow_version_id} · {run.model_execution_mode}{run.error_code ? ` · ${run.error_code}` : ""}</p><p className="mt-1 text-[var(--muted)]">{run.cost.calls} 次调用 · 已结算 {money(run.cost.settled_micros)} · 估算 {money(run.cost.estimated_micros)} · 未完成发票对账</p>{run.route && <details className="visual-native-details mt-2"><summary className="cursor-pointer">路由快照</summary><pre className="mt-2 max-h-48 overflow-auto whitespace-pre-wrap bg-[var(--surface-sunken)] p-2">{JSON.stringify(run.route, null, 2)}</pre></details>}{run.shadow_results.map(result => <details className="visual-native-details mt-2" key={result.id}><summary className="cursor-pointer">结果 JSON · {result.step_key} · 旧平台对比缺失</summary><p className="mt-1 break-all text-[var(--muted)]">哈希 {result.result_hash}</p><pre className="mt-2 max-h-64 overflow-auto whitespace-pre-wrap bg-[var(--surface-sunken)] p-2">{JSON.stringify(result.result, null, 2)}</pre></details>)}{run.publications.length > 0 && <p className="mt-2">正式发布：{run.publications.map(row => `#${row.id} r${row.revision} ${row.delivery_status}`).join("；")}</p>}</div>)}</div></details>)}
        {trace.data.next_before_id && <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => setRequest(current => current ? { ...current, beforeId: trace.data!.next_before_id! } : current)}>查看更早 50 条</Button>}
      </div>}
      <div className="border-l-2 border-[var(--line-strong)] pl-4">
        {!recovery && <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled={!canControl || !card.routing.policy} onClick={() => setRecovery({ revision: card.routing.policy!.revision })}>恢复接入事件</Button>}
        {recovery && <form className="space-y-3" onSubmit={event => { event.preventDefault(); if (!stale && idsValid && reason.trim()) recover.mutate() }}><p className="leading-5">只恢复接入或组包重试；已有运行与重复证据保留，不重放付费结果未知的模型调用。</p><label className="block font-bold">事件数据库 ID（1–100 个）<Input className="mt-1" value={eventIds} onChange={event => setEventIds(event.target.value)} placeholder="例如 1201, 1202" /></label><label className="block font-bold">恢复原因<Input className="mt-1" value={reason} maxLength={2000} onChange={event => setReason(event.target.value)} /></label>{!idsValid && eventIds && <p role="alert" className="text-danger">请输入 1–100 个正整数事件 ID。</p>}{stale && <p role="alert" className="text-danger">路由策略已更新，请取消后重新操作。</p>}<div className="flex gap-2"><Button size="sm" disabled={recover.isPending || stale || !idsValid || !reason.trim()}>{recover.isPending ? "提交中…" : "确认恢复"}</Button><Button size="sm" variant="secondary" type="button" disabled={recover.isPending} onClick={() => setRecovery(null)}>取消</Button></div></form>}
        {recover.isError && <p role="alert" className="mt-2 text-danger">恢复未完成：{recover.error.message}</p>}
      </div>
    </div>
  </details>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/incremental-automation/operations-panel.tsx =====
import { useState } from "react"
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { api, jsonBody } from "@/lib/api"
import type { IncrementalAutomationCard, IncrementalSourceRecoveryRow as SourceRow, IncrementalMaterialJob as MaterialJob, IncrementalCapacitySampling as Sampling, IncrementalReferenceComparison as Comparison, IncrementalAcceptanceReport as Report } from "@/lib/types"
import { platformLocalInputValue } from "../../lib/platform-time"

const comparisonLabels: Record<string, string> = { equal: "一致", different: "存在差异", missing_reference: "缺少旧平台参考", incompatible: "版本或合同不可比" }
const jobLabels: Record<string, string> = { queued: "排队中", processing: "处理素材中", retry_wait: "等待自动重试", failed: "需处理", completed: "素材已接入", superseded: "已被新版本替代" }
const sourceLabels: Record<string, string> = { quarantined: "已隔离", replaying: "补偿中", replayed: "已补偿" }

export function IncrementalOperationsPanel({ card, canControl }: { card: IncrementalAutomationCard; canControl: boolean }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  return <details className="visual-native-details border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-3" onToggle={event => setOpen(event.currentTarget.open)}>
    <summary className="cursor-pointer text-xs font-bold">异常处理、影子对比与验收证据</summary>
    {open && <OperationsContent card={card} canControl={canControl} />}
  </details>
}

function OperationsContent({ card, canControl }: { card: IncrementalAutomationCard; canControl: boolean }) {
  const client = useQueryClient()
  const scope = `${card.category_key}:${card.workflow_definition_id}`
  const params = new URLSearchParams({ category_key: card.category_key, workflow_definition_id: String(card.workflow_definition_id) })
  const [reason, setReason] = useState("")
  const [sourceAfter, setSourceAfter] = useState(0)
  const [materialBefore, setMaterialBefore] = useState<number | null>(null)
  const [comparisonBefore, setComparisonBefore] = useState<number | null>(null)
  const [interval, setInterval] = useState(300)
  const [since, setSince] = useState(platformLocalInputValue(Date.now() - 86400000))
  const [until, setUntil] = useState(platformLocalInputValue(Date.now()))
  const [reportWindow, setReportWindow] = useState<{ since: string; until: string } | null>(null)
  const [fileData, setFileData] = useState<unknown>(null)
  const [fileError, setFileError] = useState("")
  const [fileKind, setFileKind] = useState<"references" | "materials">("references")
  const key = ["incremental-operations", scope]
  const sources = useQuery({ queryKey: [...key, "sources", sourceAfter], queryFn: () => api<{ items: SourceRow[]; next_after_id: number }>(`/api/source-recovery/rows?category_key=${encodeURIComponent(card.category_key)}&after_id=${sourceAfter}&limit=50`), refetchInterval: 15000 })
  const materials = useQuery({ queryKey: [...key, "materials", materialBefore], queryFn: () => api<{ items: MaterialJob[]; next_before_id: number | null }>(`/api/incremental-materials/jobs?category_key=${encodeURIComponent(card.category_key)}&limit=20${materialBefore ? `&before_id=${materialBefore}` : ""}`), refetchInterval: 15000 })
  const comparisons = useQuery({ queryKey: [...key, "comparisons", comparisonBefore], queryFn: () => api<{ items: Comparison[]; next_before_id: number | null }>(`/api/incremental-evidence/comparisons?${params}&limit=20${comparisonBefore ? `&before_id=${comparisonBefore}` : ""}`) })
  const samplingPath = `/api/incremental-evidence/sampling/${encodeURIComponent(card.category_key)}/${card.workflow_definition_id}`
  const sampling = useQuery({ queryKey: [...key, "sampling"], queryFn: () => api<Sampling>(samplingPath) })
  const report = useQuery({ queryKey: [...key, "report", reportWindow], enabled: Boolean(reportWindow), queryFn: () => api<Report>(`/api/incremental-evidence/report?${params}&${new URLSearchParams(reportWindow!)}&limit=200`) })
  const action = useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ path, body, method = "POST" }: { path: string; body: unknown; method?: string }) => api<unknown>(path, { method, ...jsonBody(body) }),
    onSuccess: async () => { setReason(""); await client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: key }) },
    onError: async () => { await client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: key }) },
  })
  const ready = canControl && Boolean(reason.trim()) && !action.isPending
  const download = () => {
    if (!report.data) return
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([JSON.stringify(report.data, null, 2)], { type: "application/json" }))
    const link = document.createElement("a"); link.href = url; link.download = `验收证据-${card.category_key}-${card.workflow_definition_id}.json`; link.click(); URL.revokeObjectURL(url)
  }
  const upload = async (file?: File) => {
    setFileError(""); setFileData(null)
    if (!file) return
    if (file.size > 1024 * 1024) { setFileError("文件不能超过 1 MB。"); return }
    try { setFileData(JSON.parse(await file.text())) } catch { setFileError("文件不是有效 JSON，请使用研发导出的接入清单。") }
  }
  const importFile = () => {
    try {
      if (fileKind === "references") {
        if (!Array.isArray(fileData) || !fileData.length || fileData.length > 100) throw new Error("参考结果须为 1–100 条记录的数组。")
        const items = fileData.map(item => {
          if (!item || typeof item !== "object" || item.category_key !== card.category_key || item.workflow_definition_id !== card.workflow_definition_id) throw new Error("文件含其他类目或工作流，请核对所选卡片。")
          return item
        })
        action.mutate({ path: "/api/incremental-evidence/references", body: { items, reason: reason.trim() } })
      } else {
        if (!fileData || typeof fileData !== "object" || !("payload" in fileData) || !fileData.payload || typeof fileData.payload !== "object" || !("category_key" in fileData.payload) || fileData.payload.category_key !== card.category_key) throw new Error("素材清单类目与当前卡片不一致。")
        action.mutate({ path: "/api/incremental-materials/jobs", body: fileData })
      }
    } catch (error) { setFileError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "文件校验失败") }
  }
  return <div className="mt-4 space-y-5 text-xs">
    <p className="leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">知识图谱、旧豆包链路和灵感图新机制均须先完成运营存量验证，再进入增量路由与正式运行。这里展示运行证据，不自动判定业务验收通过。</p>
    <label className="block font-bold">本次操作原因<Input className="mt-1" value={reason} onChange={event => setReason(event.target.value)} maxLength={500} placeholder="补偿、导入或调整采集时填写" disabled={!canControl} /></label>
    {!canControl && <p>当前账号可查看；恢复、导入和采集配置由管理员操作。</p>}
    {action.isError && <p role="alert" className="text-danger">操作未完成：{action.error.message}</p>}
    {action.isSuccess && <p role="status">操作已受理，请以下方最新处理状态为准。</p>}
    <section aria-label="来源异常"><h4 className="visual-item-title">本类目来源隔离记录</h4><p className="mt-1 text-[var(--muted)]">仅重放原始事件。错误业务字段须由上游发送修正版本；补偿不会跳过付费结果保护。</p>
      {sources.isError && <p role="alert">读取失败：{sources.error.message}</p>}
      {sources.isLoading && <p role="status">正在读取…</p>}
      {sources.data?.items.length === 0 && <p className="mt-2">本页暂无隔离记录。</p>}
      {sources.data?.items.map(row => <div key={row.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-[var(--line)] py-2"><span>#{row.id} · {sourceLabels[row.status] ?? row.status} · {row.error_code || "无错误"} · 已尝试 {row.attempt_count} 次</span><Button size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled={!ready || row.status !== "quarantined"} onClick={() => action.mutate({ path: `/api/source-recovery/rows/${row.id}/replay`, body: { source_contract_id: row.source_contract_id, category_key: row.category_key, expected_lock_version: row.lock_version, reason: reason.trim() } })}>原样补偿</Button></div>)}
      <div className="mt-2 flex gap-2"><Button size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled={!sourceAfter} onClick={() => setSourceAfter(0)}>回到首批</Button><Button size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled={sources.data?.items.length !== 50} onClick={() => setSourceAfter(sources.data!.next_after_id)}>后续记录</Button></div>
    </section>
    <section aria-label="素材接入"><h4 className="visual-item-title">本类目素材接入与齐备</h4>
      {materials.isError && <p role="alert">读取失败：{materials.error.message}</p>}
      {materials.isLoading && <p role="status">正在读取…</p>}
      {materials.data?.items.length === 0 && <p className="mt-2">暂无素材接入任务。</p>}
      {materials.data?.items.map(row => <div key={row.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-[var(--line)] py-2"><div><p>#{row.id} · {row.source_content_id} · 源版本 {row.source_version} · {jobLabels[row.status] ?? row.status}</p><p className="mt-1 text-[var(--muted)]">素材 {row.resolved_count}/{row.expected_count}{row.error_code ? ` · ${row.error_code}` : ""}{row.routing_status ? ` · 接入结果 ${row.routing_status}` : ""}</p></div><Button size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled={!ready || !["failed", "retry_wait"].includes(row.status)} onClick={() => action.mutate({ path: `/api/incremental-materials/jobs/${row.id}/retry`, body: { reason: reason.trim() } })}>恢复素材处理</Button></div>)}
      <p className="mt-2 text-[var(--muted)]">“素材已接入”不等于工作流已完成；受理暂停、预算和工作流配置仍按主卡片状态处理。</p>
      <div className="mt-2 flex gap-2"><Button size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled={!materialBefore} onClick={() => setMaterialBefore(null)}>最新任务</Button><Button size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled={!materials.data?.next_before_id} onClick={() => setMaterialBefore(materials.data!.next_before_id)}>更早任务</Button></div>
    </section>
    <section aria-label="影子结果对比"><h4 className="visual-item-title">旧平台参考结果与影子结果对比</h4><p className="mt-1 text-[var(--muted)]">按实体与源版本配对。与旧平台一致不等于准确率达标，准确率仍以运营黄金真值为准。</p>
      {comparisons.isError && <p role="alert">读取失败：{comparisons.error.message}</p>}
      {comparisons.isLoading && <p role="status">正在读取…</p>}
      {comparisons.data?.items.length === 0 && <p className="mt-2">暂无影子结果可比较。</p>}
      {comparisons.data?.items.map(row => <details key={row.shadow_id} className="visual-native-details border-b border-[var(--line)] py-2"><summary className="cursor-pointer">{row.entity_id} · {row.source_version} · {row.step_key} · {comparisonLabels[row.comparison_status] ?? row.comparison_status}</summary><pre className="mt-2 max-h-64 overflow-auto whitespace-pre-wrap break-all">{JSON.stringify(row.differences, null, 2)}</pre>{row.differences_truncated && <p>差异超过显示上限，请缩小比较范围。</p>}</details>)}
      <div className="mt-2 flex gap-2"><Button size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled={!comparisonBefore} onClick={() => setComparisonBefore(null)}>最新结果</Button><Button size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled={!comparisons.data?.next_before_id} onClick={() => setComparisonBefore(comparisons.data!.next_before_id)}>更早结果</Button><Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => comparisons.refetch()}>刷新对比</Button></div>
    </section>
    <details className="visual-native-details border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-3"><summary className="cursor-pointer font-bold">导入研发交付的参考结果或素材清单</summary><div className="mt-3 space-y-2"><label className="block">清单类型<select className="visual-native-select w-auto max-w-full ml-2" value={fileKind} onChange={event => { setFileKind(event.target.value as "references" | "materials"); setFileData(null) }}><option value="references">旧平台参考结果</option><option value="materials">素材接入清单</option></select></label><input aria-label="选择接入清单文件" key={fileKind} type="file" accept=".json,application/json" disabled={!canControl} onChange={event => upload(event.target.files?.[0])} /><p>JSON 文件，最多 1 MB。自动接入仍通过研发接口持续提交；这里用于首轮联调和有范围的补偿。</p>{fileError && <p role="alert" className="text-danger">{fileError}</p>}<Button size="sm" disabled={!ready || !fileData} onClick={importFile}>提交所选清单</Button></div></details>
    <section aria-label="连续观测与验收"><h4 className="visual-item-title">连续观测与验收证据</h4>
      {sampling.isError && <p role="alert">采集配置读取失败：{sampling.error.message}</p>}
      <p className="mt-2">连续采集：{sampling.data ? sampling.data.enabled ? `已开启，每 ${sampling.data.interval_seconds} 秒记录` : "未开启" : "读取中"}</p>
      <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-end gap-2"><label>采集间隔（秒）<Input type="number" min={60} max={86400} value={interval} onChange={event => setInterval(Number(event.target.value))} /></label><Button size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled={!ready || !sampling.data || !Number.isInteger(interval) || interval < 60 || interval > 86400} onClick={() => action.mutate({ path: samplingPath, method: "PUT", body: { enabled: !sampling.data!.enabled, expected_revision: sampling.data!.revision, interval_seconds: interval, reason: reason.trim() } })}>{sampling.data?.enabled ? "关闭连续采集" : "开启连续采集"}</Button></div>
      <form className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap items-end gap-2" onSubmit={event => { event.preventDefault(); setReportWindow({ since: `${since}:00Z`, until: `${until}:00Z` }) }}><label>起始时间（UTC）<Input className="focus-within:outline-2 focus-within:outline-solid focus-within:outline-ink focus-within:outline-offset-2 focus-within:ring-4 focus-within:ring-brand" type="datetime-local" required value={since} onChange={event => setSince(event.target.value)} /></label><label>结束时间（UTC）<Input className="focus-within:outline-2 focus-within:outline-solid focus-within:outline-ink focus-within:outline-offset-2 focus-within:ring-4 focus-within:ring-brand" type="datetime-local" required value={until} onChange={event => setUntil(event.target.value)} /></label><Button size="sm" disabled={!since || !until || Date.parse(`${until}Z`) <= Date.parse(`${since}Z`) || report.isFetching}>生成验收证据</Button></form>
      {report.isFetching && <p role="status" className="mt-2">正在汇集证据…</p>}{report.isError && <p role="alert" className="mt-2 text-danger">报告未生成：{report.error.message}</p>}
      {report.data && <div className="mt-3 space-y-3"><p className="font-bold">证据报告已生成，业务通过结论仍需运营与下游签认。</p><p>观测 {report.data.capacity.samples.length} 次 · 影子一致 {report.data.quality.comparison_counts.equal ?? 0} 条 · 差异 {report.data.quality.comparison_counts.different ?? 0} 条 · 缺参考 {report.data.quality.comparison_counts.missing_reference ?? 0} 条</p>{(report.data.capacity.truncated || report.data.quality.truncated) && <p role="alert">本次结果达到查询上限，请缩小时间窗后再验收。</p>}<div className="overflow-x-auto"><table className="w-full text-left"><thead><tr><th>目的地</th><th>执行证据</th><th>运行状态</th><th>数量</th></tr></thead><tbody>{report.data.runs.map((row, index) => <tr className="border-t border-[var(--line)]" key={index}><td className="py-2">{row.destination === "formal" ? "正式" : row.destination === "shadow" ? "影子" : "未分类"}</td><td>{row.evidence_kind === "actual" ? "真实执行记录" : row.evidence_kind === "synthetic" ? "模拟" : "未分类"}</td><td>{row.status}</td><td>{row.count}</td></tr>)}</tbody></table></div><p>冻结基准命中率：{report.data.quality.baseline_truth_accuracy == null ? "证据不足或存在多个基准，请查看分次结果" : `${(report.data.quality.baseline_truth_accuracy * 100).toFixed(2)}%`} · 存量回归 {report.data.quality.baseline_runs.length} 次</p><div className="overflow-x-auto"><table className="w-full text-left"><thead><tr><th>容量分组</th><th>观测次数</th><th>积压变化/秒</th><th>观察到的排空秒数</th></tr></thead><tbody>{report.data.capacity.groups.map((row, index) => <tr key={index} className="border-t border-[var(--line)]"><td className="py-2">{row.destination === "formal" ? "正式" : row.destination === "shadow" ? "影子" : "未分类"} · {row.evidence_kind === "actual" ? "真实" : row.evidence_kind === "synthetic" ? "模拟" : "未分类"}</td><td>{row.sample_count}</td><td>{row.backlog_slope_per_second?.toFixed(3) ?? "证据不足"}</td><td>{row.observed_drain_seconds?.toFixed(0) ?? "证据不足"}</td></tr>)}</tbody></table></div><details className="visual-native-details"><summary className="cursor-pointer">查看完整证据与缺失项</summary><pre className="mt-2 max-h-80 overflow-auto whitespace-pre-wrap break-all">{JSON.stringify(report.data, null, 2)}</pre></details><Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={download}>下载验收证据</Button></div>}
    </section>
  </div>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/incremental-automation/routing-control-panel.tsx =====
import { useState } from "react"
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { api, jsonBody } from "@/lib/api"
