import type { IncrementalAutomationCard } from "@/lib/types"

const root = "/api/incremental-mechanisms/automation"
const queryKey = ["incremental-automation-cards"]
type FormKind = "policy" | "admission" | "handover"

const percent = (value: number | null) => value === null ? "暂无数据" : `${value.toFixed(1)}%`
const destinationLabels = { shadow: "影子隔离结果", excluded: "排除，不执行", formal: "本平台正式结果", old: "旧平台正式结果" }

export function RoutingControlPanel({ card, canControl }: { card: IncrementalAutomationCard; canControl: boolean }) {
  const policy = card.routing.policy
  const [form, setForm] = useState<{ kind: FormKind; revision: number } | null>(null)
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<"shadow" | "split">(policy?.mode ?? "shadow")
  const [ratio, setRatio] = useState(String(policy?.ratio_percent ?? 0))
  const [reason, setReason] = useState("")
  const [ownership, setOwnership] = useState(policy?.ownership_contract ?? "")
  const [watermark, setWatermark] = useState("")
  const [assignments, setAssignments] = useState("")
  const [destination, setDestination] = useState<keyof typeof destinationLabels>("shadow")
  const client = useQueryClient()
  const stale = Boolean(form && (policy?.revision ?? 0) !== form.revision)
  const base = `${root}/${encodeURIComponent(card.category_key)}/${card.workflow_definition_id}`
  const close = () => { setForm(null); setReason(""); mutation.reset() }
  const open = (kind: FormKind) => {
    if (!policy && kind !== "policy") return
    setMode(policy?.mode ?? "shadow"); setRatio(String(policy?.ratio_percent ?? 0)); setOwnership(policy?.ownership_contract ?? "")
    setReason(""); setWatermark(""); setAssignments(""); setForm({ kind, revision: policy?.revision ?? 0 }); mutation.reset()
  }
  const mutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async () => {
      if (!form) return
      const common = { expected_revision: form.revision, reason: reason.trim() }
      if (form.kind === "admission") return api(`${base}/admission`, { method: "PUT", ...jsonBody({ ...common, paused: !policy?.admission_paused }) })
      const policyFields = { ...common, mode, ratio_percent: Number(ratio), ownership_contract: ownership.trim() }
      if (form.kind === "policy") return api(`${base}/policy`, { method: "PUT", ...jsonBody(policyFields) })
      const assignment_ids = assignments.split(/[\s,，]+/).filter(Boolean).map(Number)
      return api(`${base}/handover`, { method: "POST", ...jsonBody({ ...policyFields, watermark: watermark.trim(), assignment_ids, destination }) })
    },
    onSuccess: async () => { close(); await client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey }) },
    onError: async () => { await client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey }) },
  })
  const ratioValid = Boolean(ratio.trim()) && Number.isInteger(Number(ratio)) && Number(ratio) >= 0 && Number(ratio) <= 100
  const assignmentIds = assignments.split(/[\s,，]+/).filter(Boolean)
  const assignmentsValid = assignmentIds.length <= 1000 && assignmentIds.every(value => /^\d+$/.test(value) && Number(value) > 0)
  const valid = Boolean(reason.trim()) && ratioValid && (!form || form.kind !== "handover" || Boolean(watermark.trim()) && assignmentsValid)

  return <section aria-label="路由与归属控制" className="space-y-4 border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-4">
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
      <div><h4 className="visual-item-title">路由与归属</h4><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">策略配置、增量受理和正式写入权分别控制。</p></div>
      <span className="text-xs font-bold">{policy ? policy.mode === "shadow" ? "影子验证" : "比例分流" : "尚未配置"}</span>
    </div>
    <dl className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-px bg-[var(--line)] sm:grid-cols-4">
      {[["目标比例", policy ? `${policy.ratio_percent}%` : "—"], ["实际接入", percent(card.routing.actual_sample_percent)], ["正式接管", percent(card.routing.formal_takeover_percent)], ["统计分母", `${card.routing.denominator} 个实体`]].map(([label, value]) => <div className="bg-white p-3" key={label}><dt className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</dt><dd className="mt-1 font-data text-base font-bold">{value}</dd></div>)}
    </dl>
    <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">窗口：全部已归属业务实体。影子结果只写入隔离表，不接管正式流量；有限样本的实际比例可能偏离目标。</p>
    {policy && <div className="text-xs leading-5">
      <p>策略 v{policy.revision} · {policy.admission_paused ? "新受理已暂停" : "正在受理"} · {policy.actor} · {policy.reason}</p>
      <p className="text-[var(--muted)]">归属合同：{policy.ownership_contract || "未填写"}</p>
    </div>}
    <dl className="grid gap-2 text-xs sm:grid-cols-2"><div><dt className="text-[var(--muted)]">排空状态</dt><dd className="mt-1">{card.routing.drain.drained ? "已排空" : "尚未排空"} · 待处理 {card.routing.drain.pending} · 在途 {card.routing.drain.in_flight}</dd></div><div><dt className="text-[var(--muted)]">未决外部结果</dt><dd className="mt-1">Outbox {card.routing.drain.outbox_unresolved} · 供应商 {card.routing.drain.provider_unresolved}</dd><dd className="mt-1">历史已核验、费用待对账 {card.routing.drain.provider_reviewed_billing_pending ?? 0}</dd></div></dl>
    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
      <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled={!canControl || mutation.isPending} onClick={() => open("policy")}>{policy ? "修改路由策略" : "配置路由策略"}</Button>
      <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled={!canControl || !policy || mutation.isPending} onClick={() => open("admission")}>{policy?.admission_paused ? "恢复新受理" : "暂停新受理"}</Button>
      <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled={!canControl || !policy || mutation.isPending} onClick={() => open("handover")}>显式交接</Button>
    </div>
    {form && <form className="space-y-3 border-l-2 border-[var(--line-strong)] pl-4" onSubmit={event => { event.preventDefault(); if (!stale && valid) mutation.mutate() }}>
      {form.kind !== "admission" && <><label className="block text-xs font-bold">运行模式<select className="visual-native-select mt-2 block w-full" value={mode} onChange={event => setMode(event.target.value as "shadow" | "split")}><option value="shadow">影子验证</option><option value="split">比例分流</option></select></label><label className="block text-xs font-bold">目标比例（0–100 的整数）<Input className="mt-2" type="number" min={0} max={100} step={1} value={ratio} onChange={event => setRatio(event.target.value)} /></label><label className="block text-xs font-bold">归属合同<Input className="mt-2" value={ownership} maxLength={500} onChange={event => setOwnership(event.target.value)} placeholder="记录旧平台与本平台的归属边界" /></label></>}
      {form.kind === "handover" && <><p className="text-xs leading-5">正式切换必须单独完成写入权交接；将比例调到 100% 不代表全量接管。</p><label className="block text-xs font-bold">事件水位<Input className="mt-2" value={watermark} maxLength={500} onChange={event => setWatermark(event.target.value)} required /></label><label className="block text-xs font-bold">实体归属 ID（逗号或空格分隔，最多 1000 个）<Input className="mt-2" value={assignments} onChange={event => setAssignments(event.target.value)} /></label><label className="block text-xs font-bold">交接目的地<select className="visual-native-select mt-2 block w-full" value={destination} onChange={event => setDestination(event.target.value as keyof typeof destinationLabels)}>{Object.entries(destinationLabels).map(([key, label]) => <option key={key} value={key}>{label}</option>)}</select></label></>}
      <label className="block text-xs font-bold">操作原因<Input className="mt-2" value={reason} maxLength={2000} onChange={event => setReason(event.target.value)} required /></label>
      {form.kind === "admission" && <p className="text-xs leading-5">{policy?.admission_paused ? "恢复后接收新增量，并由后台补处理暂停期间登记的事件；仍受独立自动派发门禁约束。" : "暂停只停止新受理；在途、Outbox 和供应商未决结果继续按排空状态处理。"}</p>}
      {!ratioValid && form.kind !== "admission" && <p role="alert" className="text-xs text-danger">目标比例必须是 0–100 的整数。</p>}
      {!assignmentsValid && form.kind === "handover" && <p role="alert" className="text-xs text-danger">实体归属 ID 必须是最多 1000 个正整数。</p>}
      {stale && <p role="alert" className="text-xs text-danger">状态已在后台更新，请关闭表单后重新操作。</p>}
      <div className="flex gap-2"><Button size="sm" disabled={!canControl || mutation.isPending || stale || !valid}>{mutation.isPending ? "提交中…" : "确认提交"}</Button><Button size="sm" variant="secondary" type="button" disabled={mutation.isPending} onClick={close}>取消</Button></div>
    </form>}
    {mutation.isError && <p role="alert" className="text-xs text-danger">操作未完成：{mutation.error.message}</p>}
    {!canControl && <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">路由与交接控制仅限管理员；当前可查看服务端实际状态。</p>}
  </section>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/incremental-automation/service-class-control.tsx =====
import { useState } from "react"
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { Lightning, Minus } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { toast } from "sonner"

import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { api, jsonBody } from "@/lib/api"

type ServiceClass = "high" | "normal"

/** Queue priority for a whole category.
 *
 *  High wins a submission slot ahead of normal, and is the only class that
 *  escalates to the online endpoint at the 24 hour deadline. Materials already
 *  running keep the class frozen into their own snapshot, so changing this only
 *  affects work enqueued from now on - which is why it needs no drain. */
export function ServiceClassControl({
  categoryKey,
  categoryName,
  serviceClass,
  source,
  lockVersion,
  canControl,
  queryKey,
}: {
  categoryKey: string
  categoryName: string
  serviceClass: ServiceClass | null
  source: "category" | "deployment_default" | "sla_disabled" | "deployment_unset"
  lockVersion: number | null
  canControl: boolean
  queryKey: unknown[]
}) {
  const client = useQueryClient()
  const [target, setTarget] = useState<ServiceClass | null>(null)
  const [reason, setReason] = useState("")
  const effective = serviceClass

  const save = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => api(
      `/api/incremental-mechanisms/categories/${encodeURIComponent(categoryKey)}/service-class`,
      { method: "PUT", ...jsonBody({ service_class: target, expected_lock_version: lockVersion, reason }) },
    ),
    onSuccess: async () => {
      setTarget(null); setReason("")
      await client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey })
      toast.success("已更新类目优先级，对新入队的素材生效")
    },
    onError: async (error) => {
      await client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey })
      toast.error(error.message)
    },
  })

  return (
    <div data-testid="service-class-control" className="space-y-2 border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-3">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <span className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">类目优先级</span>
          <Badge tone={effective === "high" ? "success" : "neutral"}>
            {effective === "high" ? <><Lightning />高优先</> : effective === "normal" ? <><Minus />普通</> : <><Minus />未分级</>}
          </Badge>
          {source === "deployment_default" && (
            <span className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">（来自部署清单，尚未在此设置）</span>
          )}
          {source === "sla_disabled" && (
            <span className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">（优先级功能未开启，所有素材一视同仁）</span>
          )}
          {source === "deployment_unset" && (
            <span className="text-xs text-warning">（优先级已开启但部署侧没有给出高优先类目，请研发补齐）</span>
          )}
        </div>
        {canControl && lockVersion !== null && target === null && (
          <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => { setTarget(effective === "high" ? "normal" : "high"); setReason("") }}>
            改为{effective === "high" ? "普通" : "高优先"}
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>
      <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
        高优先的素材先拿到提交名额。在线推理与本地模型直接执行，不等待 24 小时批量兜底。
        已经在跑的素材沿用它入队时的优先级，不受这次改动影响。
      </p>
      {target !== null && (
        <div className="grid gap-2 border border-[var(--line)] bg-surface-sunken p-3 md:grid-cols-[1fr_auto] rounded-md">
          <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">
            <span className="mb-2 block">
              把「{categoryName}」改为{target === "high" ? "高优先" : "普通"}的原因
            </span>
            <Input
              value={reason}
              onChange={(event) => setReason(event.target.value)}
              placeholder={target === "high" ? "例如：3dsu 增量要当天跑完" : "例如：让灵感图先让位给 3dsu"}
            />
          </label>
          <div className="flex items-end gap-2">
            <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setTarget(null)}>取消</Button>
            <Button disabled={!reason.trim() || save.isPending} onClick={() => save.mutate()}>确认</Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/incremental-correction/api.ts =====
import { api, jsonBody } from "@/lib/api"
import type { Paged } from "@/lib/paging"
import type {
  BatchLevelOutcome,
  CorrectionDashboard,
  IncrementalCorrectionDetail,
  IncrementalCorrectionFilters,
  IncrementalCorrectionRow,
} from "./types"

export type QueueFilters = {
  categoryKey?: string
  workflowVersionId?: number | null
  state?: "uncorrected" | "corrected" | null
  level?: string | null
  upstreamClassId?: number | null
  correctedBy?: string | null
  publishedFrom?: string | null
  publishedTo?: string | null
  search?: string | null
  limit?: number
  offset?: number
}

export const incrementalCorrectionApi = {
  list: (filters: QueueFilters = {}) => {
    const params = new URLSearchParams()
    if (filters.categoryKey) params.set("category_key", filters.categoryKey)
    if (filters.workflowVersionId) params.set("workflow_version_id", String(filters.workflowVersionId))
    if (filters.state) params.set("state", filters.state)
    if (filters.level) params.set("level", filters.level)
    if (filters.upstreamClassId) params.set("upstream_class_id", String(filters.upstreamClassId))
    if (filters.correctedBy) params.set("corrected_by", filters.correctedBy)
    if (filters.publishedFrom) params.set("published_from", filters.publishedFrom)
    if (filters.publishedTo) params.set("published_to", filters.publishedTo)
    if (filters.search?.trim()) params.set("search", filters.search.trim())
    params.set("limit", String(filters.limit ?? 50))
    if (filters.offset) params.set("offset", String(filters.offset))
    return api<Paged<IncrementalCorrectionRow>>("/api/incremental-corrections?" + params.toString())
  },
  filters: () => api<IncrementalCorrectionFilters>("/api/incremental-corrections/filters"),
  dashboard: (filters: { categoryKey?: string; workflowVersionId?: number | null; window?: number } = {}) => {
    const params = new URLSearchParams()
    if (filters.categoryKey) params.set("category_key", filters.categoryKey)
    if (filters.workflowVersionId) params.set("workflow_version_id", String(filters.workflowVersionId))
    params.set("window", String(filters.window ?? 1000))
    return api<CorrectionDashboard>("/api/incremental-corrections/dashboard?" + params.toString())
  },
  detail: (evaluationId: number) => api<IncrementalCorrectionDetail>(
    `/api/incremental-corrections/${evaluationId}/detail`,
  ),
  correctLevel: (evaluationId: number, body: {
    level: string
    reason: string
    idempotency_key: string
    expected_level: string | null
  }) => api<IncrementalCorrectionRow>(
    `/api/incremental-corrections/${evaluationId}/level`,
    { method: "PUT", ...jsonBody(body) },
  ),
  batchCorrectLevel: (body: {
    items: Array<{ evaluation_id: number; expected_level: string | null }>
    level: string
    reason: string
    idempotency_key: string
  }) => api<BatchLevelOutcome>(
    "/api/incremental-corrections/batch/level",
    { method: "PUT", ...jsonBody(body) },
  ),
  correctField: (evaluationId: number, body: {
    node_instance_id: string
    field_path: string
    value: unknown
    reason: string
    idempotency_key: string
    real_execution_confirmed: boolean
  }) => api<IncrementalCorrectionRow>(
    `/api/incremental-corrections/${evaluationId}/field`,
    { method: "PUT", ...jsonBody(body) },
  ),
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/incremental-correction/batch-dock.tsx =====
import { useLayoutEffect, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from "react"

/** Presentation only. Reserve the measured height, including on narrow/zoomed
 * viewports, so the fixed batch controls cannot cover the last card or pager. */
export function BatchDock({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLElement>(null)
  const [height, setHeight] = useState(0)
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const node = ref.current
    if (!node) return
    const measure = () => setHeight(node.getBoundingClientRect().height)
    measure()
    const observer = new ResizeObserver(measure)
    observer.observe(node)
    return () => observer.disconnect()
  }, [])
  return <>
    <div aria-hidden="true" style={{ height: height + 48 }} />
    <aside className="pointer-events-none fixed inset-x-0 bottom-4 z-30 px-4 lg:left-[252px] lg:px-8">
      <section ref={ref} data-testid="correction-batch-bar"
        className="pointer-events-auto mx-auto flex max-h-[45dvh] max-w-[1188px] flex-wrap items-center gap-3 overflow-y-auto rounded-lg bg-canvas p-5 shadow-float">
        {children}
      </section>
    </aside>
  </>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/incremental-correction/batch-outcome.ts =====
import type { BatchLevelOutcome } from "./types"

/** 批量改等级的结果提示：等级取实际提交的那次 variables，不取提示时页面上的选择。 */
export function batchLevelOutcomeMessage(
  outcome: Pick<BatchLevelOutcome, "corrected" | "unchanged" | "skipped">,
  submittedLevel: string,
): string {
  const parts = [`${outcome.corrected.length} 条已保存为 ${submittedLevel}，投递结果见各素材`]
  if (outcome.unchanged.length) parts.push(`${outcome.unchanged.length} 条本来就是 ${submittedLevel}`)
  if (outcome.skipped.length) parts.push(`${outcome.skipped.length} 条跳过（被人先改过或没发过下游）`)
  return parts.join("；")
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/incremental-correction/correction-cache.ts =====
import type { QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import type { Paged } from "../../lib/paging"
import type { IncrementalCorrectionRow } from "./types"

/** The write response is authoritative; aggregate reads must not hold the editor open. */
export async function acceptIncrementalCorrections(client: QueryClient, rows: IncrementalCorrectionRow[]) {
  await client.cancelQueries({ queryKey: ["incremental-corrections"] })
  const saved = new Map(rows.map(row => [row.evaluation_id, row]))
  for (const [key] of client.getQueriesData<Paged<IncrementalCorrectionRow>>({ queryKey: ["incremental-corrections"] })) {
    client.setQueryData<Paged<IncrementalCorrectionRow>>(key, old => {
      if (!old) return old
      let removed = 0
      const items = old.items.flatMap(previous => {
        const row = saved.get(previous.evaluation_id)
        if (!row || row.review_revision < previous.review_revision) return [previous]
        // State and level are the only mutable queue filters. Refill pagination
        // and recompute totals in the background using the server's full filters.
        if ((key[3] && key[3] !== row.correction_state) || (key[4] && key[4] !== row.level)) {
          removed += 1
          return []
        }
        return [row]
      })
      return { ...old, items, total: Math.max(0, old.total - removed) }
    })
  }
  // These requests can be slow or unavailable after a successful write. They
  // update the page independently; they are not part of saving the correction.
  void client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["incremental-corrections"] })
  void client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["incremental-correction-filters"] })
  void client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["incremental-correction-dashboard"] })
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/incremental-correction/dashboard-panel.tsx =====
import { useState } from "react"
import { CaretDown } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Card } from "@/components/ui/card"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import type { CorrectionDashboard } from "./types"

const percent = (value: number | null) =>
  value === null ? "—" : `${(value * 100).toFixed(1)}%`

/** 增量纠偏看板。
 *
 *  口径和存量回归那块看板不一样，页面上必须写明白：存量比的是「AI 判的」对
 *  「基准集真值」，每一条都有真值；这里没有真值，只有「运营改过的」和「运营没动的」。
 *  没动不等于人工确认正确，只等于没人提出异议。所以纠偏率按全窗口算，而等级流向
 *  矩阵只统计真的被改过的那些——一条没改时矩阵是空的，不会把 1000 条堆在对角线上
 *  冒充准确率。 */
export function CorrectionDashboardPanel({
  data,
  loading,
}: {
  data: CorrectionDashboard | undefined
  loading: boolean
}) {
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(false)
  if (loading && !data) {
    return (
      <Card narrow className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
        正在统计最近的纠偏情况…
      </Card>
    )
  }
  if (!data || data.window_actual === 0) {
    return (
      <Card narrow className="">
        <h2 className="visual-card-title visual-card-title-narrow">纠偏数据看板</h2>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">
          当前筛选条件下还没有增量素材，算不出比例。
        </p>
      </Card>
    )
  }

  const levels = data.levels
  const byLevel = new Map(data.by_level.map((item) => [item.level, item]))
  const hottest = [...data.by_level]
    .filter((item) => item.changed_rate !== null && item.ai_count >= 5)
    .sort((a, b) => (b.changed_rate ?? 0) - (a.changed_rate ?? 0))[0]

  return (
    <Card narrow data-testid="correction-dashboard" className="space-y-4">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">纠偏数据看板</p>
          <h2 className="visual-card-title visual-card-title-narrow mt-1">最近 {data.window_actual} 条素材，人工改了什么</h2>
          <p className="mt-2 max-w-3xl text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
            这里没有基准集真值，只有「运营改过的」和「运营没动的」。
            <strong className="text-ink">没动不等于人工确认正确</strong>
            ，只等于没人提出异议——所以下面不给「准确率」，只给纠偏率和等级流向。
          </p>
        </div>
        {/* 徽章和按钮放同一行并垂直居中（老大 10-04：右上角徽章没对齐）。 */}
        <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-3">
          {hottest && (
            <Badge tone="warning">
              {hottest.level} 被改走最多 · {percent(hottest.changed_rate)}
            </Badge>
          )}
          <Button variant="secondary" aria-expanded={expanded} aria-controls="incremental-dashboard-detail" onClick={() => setExpanded((open) => !open)}>
            {expanded ? "收起明细" : "看明细"}<CaretDown />
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
        <Metric label="已纠偏" value={`${data.corrected} 条`} note={`占窗口 ${percent(data.correction_rate)}`} />
        <Metric label="未纠偏" value={`${data.uncorrected} 条`} note="保持 AI 结果，未提出异议" />
        <Metric
          label="等级被改动"
          value={`${data.level_changed} 条`}
          note={`占窗口 ${percent(data.level_changed_rate)}；其余纠偏只改了内容`}
        />
        <Metric
          label="参与纠偏"
          value={data.correctors.length ? `${data.correctors.length} 人` : "—"}
          note={data.correctors.map((item) => `${item.actor} ${item.count}`).join(" · ") || "暂无人工改动"}
        />
      </div>

      <div id="incremental-dashboard-detail" hidden={!expanded}>
      {data.level_changed === 0 ? (
        <p className="border-t border-[var(--line)] px-5 py-5 text-sm text-[var(--muted)] md:px-6">
          这一窗口里还没有人改过等级，所以没有流向可看。
          {data.unlevelled > 0 && `（其中 ${data.unlevelled} 条属于没有等级的类目。）`}
        </p>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid gap-6 border-t border-line pt-4 xl:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_minmax(0,0.9fr)]">
          <div className="min-w-0 overflow-x-auto">
            <p className="text-xs font-bold">等级流向：AI 判的（行）→ 人工改成的（列）</p>
            <table className="mt-3 min-w-[420px] text-left text-xs">
              <thead className="text-[var(--muted)]">
                <tr>
                  <th className="px-2 py-2 font-semibold">AI ＼ 人工</th>
                  {levels.map((level) => (
                    <th key={level} className="px-2 py-2 text-center font-semibold">{level}</th>
                  ))}
                  <th className="px-2 py-2 text-center font-semibold">被改走</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {levels.map((row) => (
                  <tr key={row} className="border-t border-[var(--line)]">
                    <th className="px-2 py-2 text-left font-semibold">{row}</th>
                    {levels.map((column) => {
                      const count = data.confusion_matrix[row]?.[column] ?? 0
                      return (
                        <td
                          key={column}
                          className={`px-2 py-2 text-center font-data tabular-nums ${
                            count ? "bg-active-surface font-bold" : "text-muted-foreground"
                          }`}
                        >
                          {count || "·"}
                        </td>
                      )
                    })}
                    <td className="px-2 py-2 text-center font-data tabular-nums">
                      {byLevel.get(row)?.corrected_away ?? 0}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
            <p className="mt-3 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
              对角线是空的：等级没变的纠偏不进这张表，只统计真的被改动的方向。
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="min-w-0 overflow-x-auto">
            <p className="text-xs font-bold">哪一级判得最不准</p>
            <table className="mt-3 w-full text-left text-xs">
              <thead className="text-[var(--muted)]">
                <tr>
                  <th className="py-2 font-semibold">等级</th>
                  <th className="py-2 text-right font-semibold">AI 判出</th>
                  <th className="py-2 text-right font-semibold">被改走</th>
                  <th className="py-2 text-right font-semibold">被改率</th>
                  <th className="py-2 text-right font-semibold">净流入</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {data.by_level.map((item) => (
                  <tr key={item.level} className="border-t border-[var(--line)]">
                    <td className="py-2 font-semibold">{item.level}</td>
                    <td className="py-2 text-right font-data tabular-nums">{item.ai_count}</td>
                    <td className="py-2 text-right font-data tabular-nums">{item.corrected_away}</td>
                    <td className="py-2 text-right font-data tabular-nums">
                      {item.changed_rate === null
                        ? <span className="text-muted-foreground" title="这个等级 AI 从没判出过">未出现</span>
                        : percent(item.changed_rate)}
                    </td>
                    <td className={`py-2 text-right font-data tabular-nums ${
                      item.net > 0 ? "text-success" : item.net < 0 ? "text-danger" : "text-muted-foreground"
                    }`}>
                      {item.net > 0 ? `+${item.net}` : item.net || "·"}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
            <p className="mt-3 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
              「被改率」＝ 这个等级被人工改走的条数 ÷ AI 判出这个等级的条数。
              从没判出过的等级显示「未出现」而不是 0%——两者不是一回事。
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
      </div>
    </Card>
  )
}

function Metric({ label, value, note }: { label: string; value: string; note: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="min-w-0 rounded-md bg-surface-sunken px-4 py-3">
      <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</p>
      <p className="mt-1 font-data text-2xl font-bold tabular-nums">{value}</p>
      <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">{note}</p>
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/incremental-correction/delivery-status.ts =====
import type { DownstreamState } from "./types"

/** A saved human revision is not evidence that the downstream accepted it. */
export function correctionDeliveryLabel(state: DownstreamState): string {
  const saved = state.review_state === "reviewed" ? "，人工修改已保存" : ""
  if (!state.published) return "尚未投递下游"
  switch (state.delivery_status) {
    case "delivered":
      return state.review_state === "reviewed" ? "下游已覆盖" : "下游已接收"
    case "rejected":
      return `下游拒绝${saved}`
    case "dead_lettered":
      return `下游投递失败${saved}`
    case "pending":
      return `等待下游接收${saved}`
    case "retrying":
      return `下游投递重试中${saved}`
    default:
      return `下游状态待确认${saved}`
  }
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/incremental-correction/detail-drawer.tsx =====
import { useRef, useState } from "react"
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { useAccess } from "@/lib/access"
import { PencilSimple, Warning } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { toast } from "sonner"

import { AssetThumbnail } from "@/components/asset-thumbnail"
import { SecondaryDrawer } from "@/components/workspace-page"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { incrementalCorrectionApi } from "./api"
import { initialFieldDraft, parseFieldDraft } from "./field-draft"
import { correctionDeliveryLabel } from "./delivery-status"
import type { CorrectionField, CorrectionNode, IncrementalCorrectionRow } from "./types"

function valueText(value: unknown) {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return "（空）"
  if (typeof value === "string") return value || "（空字符串）"
  if (typeof value === "boolean") return value ? "是" : "否"
  if (typeof value === "number") return String(value)
  return JSON.stringify(value, null, 2)
}

/** 详情纠偏：改具体内容字段。
 *
 *  和列表改等级的区别只在改什么：这里能改分类、标题这类内容字段。落到下游的动作是同一个
 *  ——覆盖 AI 结果，按 revision+1 重发同一行。 */
export function CorrectionDetailDrawer({
  evaluationId,
  onOpenChange,
  onCorrected,
}: {
  evaluationId: number | null
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void
  onCorrected: (row: IncrementalCorrectionRow) => void
}) {
  const access = useAccess()
  const client = useQueryClient()
  const currentEvaluationId = useRef(evaluationId)
  currentEvaluationId.current = evaluationId
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<{ node: string; field: string } | null>(null)
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState("")
  const [reason, setReason] = useState("")
  const [confirmPaid, setConfirmPaid] = useState(false)
  // 换素材时清空编辑态：否则 A 素材未提交的草稿/理由/付费确认会带到 B 素材上提交。
  const [stateEvaluationId, setStateEvaluationId] = useState(evaluationId)
  if (stateEvaluationId !== evaluationId) {
    setStateEvaluationId(evaluationId)
    setEditing(null)
    setDraft("")
    setReason("")
    setConfirmPaid(false)
  }

  const detail = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["incremental-correction-detail", evaluationId],
    queryFn: () => incrementalCorrectionApi.detail(evaluationId as number),
    enabled: evaluationId !== null,
  })

  const submit = useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ targetEvaluationId, node, field, value, reason: submitReason, realExecutionConfirmed }: {
      targetEvaluationId: number
      node: string
      field: string
      value: unknown
      reason: string
      realExecutionConfirmed: boolean
    }) =>
      incrementalCorrectionApi.correctField(targetEvaluationId, {
        node_instance_id: node,
        field_path: field,
        value,
        reason: submitReason,
        idempotency_key: `${targetEvaluationId}:${node}:${field}:${Date.now()}`,
        real_execution_confirmed: realExecutionConfirmed,
      }),
    onSuccess: (row, { targetEvaluationId }) => {
      if (targetEvaluationId === currentEvaluationId.current) {
        setEditing(null); setDraft(""); setReason(""); setConfirmPaid(false)
      }
      void client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["incremental-correction-detail", targetEvaluationId] })
      onCorrected(row)
      const notify = ["rejected", "dead_lettered"].includes(row.downstream.delivery_status ?? "")
        ? toast.warning : toast.success
      notify(`已保存纠偏；${correctionDeliveryLabel(row.downstream)}`)
    },
    onError: (error: Error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })

  const data = detail.data
  const paidRerun = data?.model_execution_mode === "real"
  const savingCurrent = submit.isPending && submit.variables?.targetEvaluationId === evaluationId

  function startEditing(node: CorrectionNode, field: CorrectionField) {
    setEditing({ node: node.node_instance_id, field: field.field_path })
    setDraft(initialFieldDraft(field))
    setReason("")
    setConfirmPaid(false)
  }

  function parsedDraft(field: CorrectionField): { value: unknown; error: string; unchanged: boolean } {
    return parseFieldDraft(field, draft)
  }

  return (
    <SecondaryDrawer
      open={evaluationId !== null}
      onOpenChange={onOpenChange}
      size="wide"
      title={access.can("reviews:write", data?.category_key) ? "逐字段纠偏" : "逐字段查看"}
      description={access.can("reviews:write", data?.category_key) ? "改完会重跑受影响的后续步骤，并按新的结果覆盖下游同一行。没有改过的字段保持 AI 结果不变。" : access.readOnlyReason}
    >
      {detail.isLoading && <p className="text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">正在读取本轮结果…</p>}
      {detail.isError && <p className="text-sm text-danger">{(detail.error as Error).message}</p>}
      {data && (
        <div className="space-y-5">
          {savingCurrent && (
            <p role="status" aria-live="polite" className="rounded-md bg-surface-sunken p-3 text-sm">
              正在保存修改并重跑受影响的后续步骤，完成后会提交下游。请勿重复提交。
            </p>
          )}
          <section className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4 border-y border-[var(--line)] py-4">
            <AssetThumbnail assetId={data.asset_id} alt={data.run_key} size="size-20" />
            <div className="min-w-0 max-w-full [overflow-wrap:anywhere]">
              <p className="visual-code text-sm font-bold">{data.run_key}</p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{data.category_key}</p>
              <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                <Badge>{data.level ?? "本类目无等级"}</Badge>
                {data.score !== null && <span className="font-data text-xs">{data.score} 分</span>}
                {paidRerun && (
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-warning">
                    <Warning />真实模型运行：改上游内容会重跑提示词并产生费用
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </section>

          {data.nodes.map((node) => (
            <section key={node.node_instance_id} className="min-w-0 rounded-md border border-line">
              <header className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-[var(--line)] bg-surface-sunken px-4 py-3">
                <div className="min-w-0 max-w-full [overflow-wrap:anywhere]">
                  <p className="text-sm font-bold">{node.display_name}</p>
                  <p className="mt-1 visual-code text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{node.node_instance_id}</p>
                </div>
                {!node.correctable && <Badge>{node.read_only_reason || "只读"}</Badge>}
              </header>
              {node.correctable ? (
                <ul className="divide-y divide-[var(--line)]">
                  {node.fields.map((field) => {
                    const active = editing?.node === node.node_instance_id
                      && editing.field === field.field_path
                    const parsed = active ? parsedDraft(field) : { value: null, error: "", unchanged: true }
                    return (
                      <li key={field.field_path} className="px-4 py-3">
                        <div className="grid min-w-0 items-start gap-2 md:grid-cols-[minmax(0,200px)_minmax(0,1fr)_auto]">
                          <p className="visual-code min-w-0 whitespace-normal [overflow-wrap:anywhere] text-xs font-semibold">{field.field_path}</p>
                          <pre className="min-w-0 max-w-full max-h-40 overflow-auto whitespace-pre-wrap [overflow-wrap:anywhere] text-xs leading-5">
                            {valueText(field.value)}
                          </pre>
                          <Button
                            size="sm"
                            variant="ghost"
                            className="justify-self-start md:justify-self-end"
                            aria-label={`纠偏 ${field.field_path}`}
                            disabled={!access.can("reviews:write", data.category_key) || savingCurrent}
                            onClick={() => (active ? setEditing(null) : startEditing(node, field))}
                          >
                            <PencilSimple />{active ? "收起" : "纠偏"}
                          </Button>
                        </div>
                        {active && (
                          <div className="mt-3 space-y-2 border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-surface-sunken p-3 rounded-md">
                            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">
                              <span className="mb-1 block">改成</span>
                              <textarea
                                className="visual-native-textarea min-h-16 w-full font-data text-xs"
                                value={draft}
                                disabled={savingCurrent}
                                onChange={(event) => setDraft(event.target.value)}
                                spellCheck={false}
                              />
                            </label>
                            {parsed.error && <p className="text-xs text-danger">{parsed.error}</p>}
                            {parsed.unchanged && <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">尚未修改，改动后才能保存</p>}
                            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">
                              <span className="mb-1 block">理由</span>
                              <Input value={reason} disabled={savingCurrent} onChange={(event) => setReason(event.target.value)} />
                            </label>
                            {paidRerun && (
                              <label className="visual-native-choice flex min-h-hit items-start gap-2 text-xs text-warning">
                                <input
                                  type="checkbox"
                                  className="visual-native-checkbox"
                                  checked={confirmPaid}
                                  disabled={savingCurrent}
                                  onChange={(event) => setConfirmPaid(event.target.checked)}
                                />
                                我确认这次修改可能重跑真实模型并产生费用
                              </label>
                            )}
                            <Button
                              size="sm"
                              disabled={!access.can("reviews:write", data.category_key) || Boolean(parsed.error) || parsed.unchanged || !reason.trim() || submit.isPending || evaluationId === null}
                              onClick={() => evaluationId !== null && submit.mutate({
                                targetEvaluationId: evaluationId,
                                reason: reason.trim(),
                                realExecutionConfirmed: confirmPaid,
                                node: node.node_instance_id,
                                field: field.field_path,
                                value: parsed.value,
                              })}
                            >
                              {savingCurrent ? "正在保存并重跑…" : "保存并覆盖下游"}
                            </Button>
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </li>
                    )
                  })}
                  {!node.fields.length && (
                    <li className="px-4 py-3 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">这一步没有可改的字段。</li>
                  )}
                </ul>
              ) : (
                <p className="px-4 py-3 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{node.read_only_reason}</p>
              )}
            </section>
          ))}

          {data.correction_history.length > 0 && (
            <section className="border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-4">
              <h3 className="text-xs font-bold">纠偏记录</h3>
              <ul className="mt-2 space-y-2">
                {data.correction_history.slice().reverse().map((entry, index) => (
                  <li key={index} className="border-b border-[var(--line)] pb-2 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                    <span className="visual-code">{String(entry.node_path ?? entry.node_instance_id ?? "")}</span>
                    {" · "}{String(entry.corrector ?? "")}{" · "}{String(entry.reason ?? "")}
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </section>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </SecondaryDrawer>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/incremental-correction/field-draft.ts =====
import type { CorrectionField } from "./types"

/** 编辑框初值：原值为空（null/undefined）时给空串，不能预填字面量 "null"。 */
export function initialFieldDraft(field: Pick<CorrectionField, "value">): string {
  if (field.value === null || field.value === undefined) return ""
  if (typeof field.value === "string") return field.value
  return JSON.stringify(field.value)
}

/**
 * 把编辑框内容解析成要提交的值。
 * - 与初值相同视为未修改，不允许提交；
 * - 数字字段空白视为未填（Number("") 会得到 0，不能静默保存成 0）；
 * - 非文本字段空白同样视为未填。
 */
export function parseFieldDraft(
  field: Pick<CorrectionField, "value" | "value_type">,
  draft: string,
): { value: unknown; error: string; unchanged: boolean } {
  if (draft === initialFieldDraft(field)) return { value: null, error: "", unchanged: true }
  if (field.value_type === "text") return { value: draft, error: "", unchanged: false }
  if (!draft.trim()) return { value: null, error: "请先填写新值", unchanged: false }
  if (field.value_type === "number") {
    const parsed = Number(draft)
    return Number.isFinite(parsed)
      ? { value: parsed, error: "", unchanged: false }
      : { value: null, error: "这个字段要填数字", unchanged: false }
  }
  try {
    return { value: JSON.parse(draft), error: "", unchanged: false }
  } catch {
    return { value: null, error: "这个字段要填合法 JSON", unchanged: false }
  }
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/incremental-correction/level-cell.tsx =====
import { useState } from "react"
import { Check, Lock, PencilSimple, X } from "@phosphor-icons/react"

import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { LevelBadge } from "@/components/ui/level-badge"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import type { IncrementalCorrectionRow } from "./types"

/** 列表里直接改等级。
 *
 *  改完立刻按 revision+1 覆盖下游那一行，所以必须填理由——事后要说得清是谁、为什么改的。
 *  提交时带上运营当时看到的等级，别人刚改过就让这次先刷新，而不是把人家的结论盖掉。 */
export function LevelCell({
  row,
  onCorrect,
  pending,
}: {
  row: IncrementalCorrectionRow
  onCorrect: (input: { level: string; reason: string }) => Promise<boolean>
  pending: boolean
}) {
  const [target, setTarget] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [reason, setReason] = useState("")
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false)
  const lockedLevels = row.locked_levels ?? []
  const editable = row.selectable_levels.some((level) => !lockedLevels.includes(level))

  async function saveWithReason() {
    if (target === null) return
    setSaving(true)
    try {
      if (await onCorrect({ level: target, reason: reason.trim() })) {
        setTarget(null)
        setReason("")
      }
    } finally {
      setSaving(false)
    }
  }

  if (target !== null) {
    return (
      <div className="min-w-56 space-y-2" data-testid="level-cell-editing">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1">
          {row.selectable_levels.map((level) => {
            const locked = lockedLevels.includes(level) && level !== row.level
            return (
              <button
                key={level}
                type="button"
                aria-pressed={level === target}
                disabled={locked || pending || saving}
                title={locked ? row.level_lock_reason ?? "暂不开放" : undefined}
                className={`visual-control min-h-hit min-w-hit rounded-md border px-3 py-2 font-data text-xs ${
                  level === target
                    ? "border-active-border bg-active-surface text-active-text font-bold"
                    : "border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white"
                } disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40`}
                onClick={() => setTarget(level)}
              >
                {level}
              </button>
            )
          })}
        </div>
        {row.level_lock_reason && <LockHint reason={row.level_lock_reason} />}
        <Input
          value={reason}
          disabled={pending || saving}
          placeholder="改成这个等级的理由"
          onChange={(event) => setReason(event.target.value)}
        />
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <Button
            size="sm"
            disabled={pending || saving || !reason.trim() || target === row.level}
            onClick={saveWithReason}
          >
            <Check />{saving ? "正在保存…" : "覆盖下游"}
          </Button>
          <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled={pending || saving} onClick={() => { setTarget(null); setReason("") }}>
            <X />取消
          </Button>
        </div>
        {!saving && target === row.level && (
          <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">当前等级为 {row.level}，请选择不同的目标等级。</p>
        )}
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
      {row.level === null ? <Badge>本类目无等级</Badge> : <LevelBadge level={row.level} />}
      {row.score !== null && <span className="font-data text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{row.score}</span>}
      {!editable && row.level_lock_reason && (
        <span className="text-[var(--muted)]" title={row.level_lock_reason} aria-label={row.level_lock_reason}><Lock /></span>
      )}
      {editable && (
        <Button
          size="sm"
          variant="ghost"
          disabled={pending}
          aria-label={`纠偏 ${row.asset_name || row.run_key} 的等级`}
          onClick={() => { setTarget(row.level ?? row.selectable_levels[0]); setReason("") }}
        >
          <PencilSimple />纠偏
        </Button>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

export function LockHint({ reason }: { reason: string }) {
  return (
    <p className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
      <Lock className="shrink-0" />{reason}
    </p>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/incremental-correction/material-card.tsx =====
import { useState } from "react"
import { Check, PencilSimple, X } from "@phosphor-icons/react"

import type { ImagePreview } from "@/components/image-lightbox"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { Card } from "@/components/ui/card"
import { LevelBadge } from "@/components/ui/level-badge"
import { SegmentedControl } from "@/components/ui/segmented-control"
import type { IncrementalCorrectionRow } from "./types"
import { correctionDeliveryLabel } from "./delivery-status"
import { LockHint } from "./level-cell"
import { formatPlatformMinute } from "@/lib/platform-time"

const categoryLabels: Record<string, string> = {
  knowledge_graph: "知识图谱",
  inspiration_image: "灵感图",
  model_3d_su: "3D & SU",
  space_image: "空间图片",
  material_image: "材质图",
  design_case: "设计案例",
}

/** 一张素材卡。
 *
 *  做成卡片而不是表格行，是因为纯列表里的缩略图太小——运营要在不展开的情况下
 *  一眼看出画面内容才谈得上遍历纠偏。所以图片占满卡片宽度、按原始宽高比展示，
 *  等级和纠偏入口压在图下方，改等级就地展开，不跳页。 */
/** 列表里点等级就直接覆盖下游时带的理由。运营的诉求是少点两下；理由仍然写进纠偏记录，
 *  想写具体原因的走「写理由」。 */
export const QUICK_CORRECTION_REASON = "列表快捷纠偏"

export function MaterialCard({
  row,
  onCorrect,
  onDetail,
  onPreview,
  pending,
  selected = false,
  onSelect,
  canWrite = false,
}: {
  row: IncrementalCorrectionRow
  onCorrect: (input: { level: string; reason: string }) => Promise<boolean>
  onDetail: () => void
  onPreview: (preview: ImagePreview) => void
  pending: boolean
  selected?: boolean
  onSelect?: (selected: boolean) => void
  canWrite?: boolean
}) {
  // target 只在「写理由」模式下有值：点了等级但还没提交。默认模式下点等级立即提交。
  const [target, setTarget] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [reason, setReason] = useState("")
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false)
  const [broken, setBroken] = useState(false)
  const lockedLevels = row.locked_levels ?? []
  const editable = canWrite && row.selectable_levels.some((level) => !lockedLevels.includes(level))
  const src = row.asset_id ? `/api/assets/${row.asset_id}/file` : null
  const alt = row.asset_name || row.run_key
  const corrected = row.correction_state === "corrected"

  async function saveWithReason() {
    if (target === null) return
    setSaving(true)
    try {
      // 只收起这一次成功提交的编辑区；失败或别人的更新都保留自己的草稿。
      if (await onCorrect({ level: target, reason: reason.trim() })) {
        setTarget(null)
        setReason("")
      }
    } finally {
      setSaving(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <Card asChild className="p-3">
    <article
      data-testid="correction-card"
      className={`mb-4 min-w-0 break-inside-avoid ${selected ? "ring-2 ring-active-border" : ""}`}
    >
      {src && !broken ? (
        <button
          type="button"
          aria-label={`查看原图：${alt}`}
          className="block w-full cursor-zoom-in visual-control rounded-md focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-ink focus-visible:outline-offset-2"
          onClick={() => onPreview({ src, alt })}
        >
          <img
            src={src}
            alt=""
            loading="lazy"
            decoding="async"
            onError={() => setBroken(true)}
            className="block h-auto w-full bg-canvas object-contain"
          />
        </button>
      ) : (
        <div className="flex h-40 items-center justify-center bg-surface-sunken text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
          {row.asset_id ? "原图已不在存储上" : "这条运行没有记录素材"}
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="space-y-3 px-2 pb-2 pt-4">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            {onSelect && (
              <label className="visual-native-choice inline-flex min-h-hit min-w-hit items-center justify-center">
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                aria-label={`选中 ${alt}`}
                className="visual-native-checkbox"
                checked={selected}
                disabled={!canWrite}
                onChange={(event) => onSelect(event.target.checked)}
              />
              </label>
            )}
            {row.level === null ? <Badge>本类目无等级</Badge> : <LevelBadge level={row.level} />}
            {row.score !== null && (
              <span className="font-data text-xs tabular-nums text-[var(--muted)]">{row.score} 分</span>
            )}
          </div>
          <Badge tone={corrected ? "success" : "neutral"}>{corrected ? "已纠偏" : "未纠偏"}</Badge>
        </div>

        <p className="file-name break-words text-xs" title={alt}>{alt}</p>
        <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
          {categoryLabels[row.category_key] ?? row.category_key} · 机制 #<span className="visual-code">{row.workflow_version_id}</span>
          {row.upstream.class_label && ` · ${row.upstream.class_label}`}
          {` · ${correctionDeliveryLabel(row.downstream)}`}
        </p>
        <p className="break-words text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]" title="平台评测编号 · 工厂任务号（task_id）/ img / ll id · 发布时间">
          评测 #<span className="visual-code">{row.evaluation_id}</span>
          {row.upstream.task_id !== null && <> · 工厂任务 <span className="visual-code">{row.upstream.task_id}</span></>}
          {row.upstream.img_id !== null && <> · img <span className="visual-code">{row.upstream.img_id}</span></>}
          {row.upstream.ll_id !== null && <> · ll <span className="visual-code">{row.upstream.ll_id}</span></>}
          {row.published_at && ` · 发布 ${formatPlatformMinute(row.published_at)}`}
        </p>
        {corrected && row.correction_reason && (
          <p className="border-l-2 border-[var(--line-strong)] pl-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
            {row.corrected_by}：{row.correction_reason}
          </p>
        )}

        {target === null ? (
          <div className="space-y-2">
            {row.selectable_levels.length > 0 && (
              /* 等级按钮常驻：点一个不同的等级就直接覆盖下游（理由固定为「列表快捷纠偏」），
                 这是运营要的「点等级即完成」；要写具体理由再点「写理由」。 */
              <SegmentedControl label="改等级" value={row.level}
                disabled={!canWrite || pending} className="w-full [&_[role=radio]]:px-2 [&_[role=radio]:disabled]:opacity-100"
                options={row.selectable_levels.map((level) => {
                  const locked = lockedLevels.includes(level) && level !== row.level
                  return { value: level, disabled: level === row.level || locked,
                    label: <span title={level === row.level ? "当前等级" : locked ? row.level_lock_reason ?? "暂不开放" : `改为 ${level} 并提交下游`}><LevelBadge level={level} /></span> }
                })}
                onValueChange={(level) => onCorrect({ level, reason: QUICK_CORRECTION_REASON })} />
            )}
            {row.level_lock_reason && <LockHint reason={row.level_lock_reason} />}
            {!canWrite && <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">当前角色或类目仅可查看</p>}
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              {editable && (
                <Button
                  size="sm"
                  variant="ghost"
                  disabled={pending}
                  onClick={() => { setTarget(row.level ?? row.selectable_levels[0]); setReason("") }}
                >
                  <PencilSimple />写理由
                </Button>
              )}
              <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={onDetail}>{canWrite ? "纠偏具体内容" : "查看具体内容"}</Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-2 border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-3" aria-busy={saving}>
            <SegmentedControl label="目标等级" value={target} disabled={!canWrite || pending || saving} className="w-full [&_[role=radio]]:px-2 [&_[role=radio]:disabled]:opacity-100"
              options={row.selectable_levels.map((level) => {
                const locked = lockedLevels.includes(level) && level !== row.level
                return { value: level, disabled: locked,
                  label: <span title={locked ? row.level_lock_reason ?? "暂不开放" : undefined}><LevelBadge level={level} /></span> }
              })}
              onValueChange={setTarget} />
            <Input
              value={reason}
              disabled={!canWrite || pending || saving}
              placeholder="改成这个等级的理由"
              onChange={(event) => setReason(event.target.value)}
            />
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <Button
                size="sm"
                disabled={!canWrite || pending || saving || !reason.trim() || target === row.level}
                onClick={saveWithReason}
              >
                <Check />{saving ? "正在保存…" : "保存并提交下游"}
              </Button>
              <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled={pending || saving} onClick={() => { setTarget(null); setReason("") }}>
                <X />取消
              </Button>
            </div>
            {!saving && target === row.level && (
              <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">当前等级为 {row.level}，请选择不同的目标等级。</p>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </article>
    </Card>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/incremental-correction/submission-gate.ts =====
import type { QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"

/** 增量纠偏列表的写入口（快捷改等级、批量改等级）一次只接受一笔，跨页面卸载与重新进入都成立。
 *
 *  入口挂在同一个 QueryClient 上，不随纠偏页卸载：保存还没完成时离开再回来，新页面读到的仍是
 *  “正在保存”，不会再接受一笔不同幂等键的写入。进入是同步的，不等 pending 的下一次渲染。
 *  交还：写入结果和权威缓存都处理完（mutation 的 onSettled）之后——有页面在显示，就等它把完成
 *  状态提交到控件再交还；没有页面在显示，就当场交还，不留永久锁。
 *
 *  页面订阅的是三态（空闲／保存中／已结清），结清本身也算一次变化：保存中离开再回来的页面没有
 *  这笔写入的 mutation 观察者，旧写失败时也没有缓存变化，只看“占没占”就收不到通知，会一直锁到
 *  下一次无关的渲染。票记着自己属于哪个 QueryClient，结清和撤回都回到那一个入口。 */
export type SubmissionTicket = { readonly id: number; readonly client: QueryClient; settled: boolean }

export type SubmissionState = "idle" | "saving" | "settled"

type Gate = { ticket: SubmissionTicket | null; pages: number; next: number; listeners: Set<() => void> }

const gates = new WeakMap<QueryClient, Gate>()

function gateOf(client: QueryClient): Gate {
  let gate = gates.get(client)
  if (!gate) gates.set(client, gate = { ticket: null, pages: 0, next: 1, listeners: new Set() })
  return gate
}

function notify(gate: Gate) {
  for (const listener of [...gate.listeners]) listener()
}

/** 同步占住入口；已有未交还的写入时返回 null，这一下不提交。 */
export function enterSubmission(client: QueryClient): SubmissionTicket | null {
  const gate = gateOf(client)
  if (gate.ticket) return null
  gate.ticket = { id: gate.next++, client, settled: false }
  notify(gate)
  return gate.ticket
}

/** mutate 同步抛错、写入根本没有发出时撤回。 */
export function abandonSubmission(ticket: SubmissionTicket) {
  const gate = gateOf(ticket.client)
  if (gate.ticket !== ticket) return
  gate.ticket = null
  notify(gate)
}

/** 写入结果和权威缓存处理完。没有页面在显示时当场交还。 */
export function settleSubmission(ticket: SubmissionTicket) {
  const gate = gateOf(ticket.client)
  if (gate.ticket !== ticket) return
  ticket.settled = true
  if (gate.pages === 0) gate.ticket = null
  notify(gate)
}

/** 页面把完成状态提交到控件之后调用：已处理完的写入在这里交还。 */
export function releaseSettledSubmission(client: QueryClient) {
  const gate = gateOf(client)
  if (!gate.ticket?.settled) return
  gate.ticket = null
  notify(gate)
}

export function submissionState(client: QueryClient): SubmissionState {
  const ticket = gateOf(client).ticket
  return ticket === null ? "idle" : ticket.settled ? "settled" : "saving"
}

export function subscribeSubmission(client: QueryClient, listener: () => void) {
  const gate = gateOf(client)
  gate.listeners.add(listener)
  return () => { gate.listeners.delete(listener) }
}

/** 页面挂载期间登记；最后一个页面离开时，已处理完的写入当场交还。 */
export function attachSubmissionPage(client: QueryClient) {
  const gate = gateOf(client)
  gate.pages++
  return () => {
    gate.pages--
    if (gate.pages === 0 && gate.ticket?.settled) {
      gate.ticket = null
      notify(gate)
    }
  }
}

}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/incremental-dashboard/coverage.ts =====
/** 处置覆盖率的统一显示：汇总卡与每日明细同口径保留 1 位小数；缺分母显示 —，不补零。 */
export function formatCoverage(coverage: number | null | undefined): string {
  return coverage == null ? "—" : `${(coverage * 100).toFixed(1)}%`
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/mechanism-config/anchor-mechanism-editor.tsx =====
/** 锚点图机制编辑器 —— 只管「哪些图片代表哪个等级」。
 *
 * 架构约束（2026-08-24 裁决）：本面板只写 anchor_mechanism 块里的锚点图片、
 * 等级标注、送图上限与开关。分数阈值归合同顶层 level_scale、维度与权重归
 * Call B 的 dimensions、红线与硬伤封顶归 Call A 与 redline_policy —— 这类旋钮
 * 一个都不准出现在本面板，否则「换一张锚图看分数怎么变」就失去可归因性。
 *
 * 锚点图必须是素材库里已有的素材：合同只存 asset_id + sha256，后端
 * resolve_frozen_anchor_assets 只能从 Asset 表解析。要用新图先走素材库上传。
 */

import { useMemo, useState } from "react"
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { Trash, Warning } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { HoverPreview } from "@/components/hover-preview"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { baselineRegressionApi } from "@/lib/api"
import type { Asset } from "@/lib/types"
import type { Editable } from "./types"
import { FieldCard, inputClass, numberClass } from "./mechanism-form-primitives"
import {
  ANCHOR_LEVELS,
  ANCHOR_MECHANISM_KEY,
  ANCHOR_MIME_TYPES,
  MAX_ANCHOR_IMAGES_CEILING,
  anchorMechanismIntruders,
  readAnchorMechanism,
  removeAnchorMechanismAnchor,
  setAnchorMechanismEnabled,
  setAnchorMechanismMaxImages,
  upsertAnchorMechanismAnchor,
  type AnchorLevel,
} from "./image-rule-contract"

/** 素材能否作为锚点图；不能时给出人能看懂的原因，而不是静默过滤掉。 */
function ineligibleReason(asset: Asset): string | null {
  if (!(ANCHOR_MIME_TYPES as readonly string[]).includes(asset.mime_type)) {
    return `格式 ${asset.mime_type} 不支持，仅限 JPEG / PNG`
  }
  if (!asset.sha256) return "缺少内容哈希，无法冻结图片身份"
  return null
}

export function AnchorMechanismEditor({
  draft,
  onPatch,
}: {
  draft: Editable
  onPatch: (mutator: (next: Editable) => void) => void
}) {
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [pickerOpen, setPickerOpen] = useState(false)
  const [keyword, setKeyword] = useState("")
  const [onlyThisCategory, setOnlyThisCategory] = useState(true)
  const [pendingAsset, setPendingAsset] = useState<Asset | null>(null)

  const view = readAnchorMechanism(draft.contract)
  const intruders = anchorMechanismIntruders(draft.contract[ANCHOR_MECHANISM_KEY])

  // 关键词走服务端检索：素材库数千条，只拉一页再本地过滤会让页外素材永远搜不到
  // （运营反馈「素材库里明明有、就是搜不出来」的根因）。
  const trimmedKeyword = keyword.trim()
  const assets = useQuery({
    queryKey: [
      "anchor-mechanism-assets",
      onlyThisCategory ? draft.category_key : "__all__",
      trimmedKeyword,
    ],
    queryFn: () =>
      baselineRegressionApi.listAssets(
        undefined,
        onlyThisCategory ? draft.category_key : undefined,
        0,
        200,
        trimmedKeyword || undefined,
      ),
    enabled: pickerOpen,
  })

  const alreadyUsed = useMemo(
    () => new Set(view.anchors.map((a) => a.assetId)),
    [view.anchors],
  )

  const candidates = useMemo(() => {
    const items = assets.data?.items ?? []
    // 关键词已由服务端匹配，这里只排除已配为锚点的素材。
    return items.filter((asset) => !alreadyUsed.has(asset.id))
  }, [assets.data?.items, alreadyUsed])

  function addAnchor(asset: Asset, level: AnchorLevel) {
    const reason = ineligibleReason(asset)
    if (reason) {
      setNotice(`素材 ${asset.id} 不可用作锚点图：${reason}`)
      return
    }
    let failure: string | null = null
    onPatch((next) => {
      const result = upsertAnchorMechanismAnchor(next.contract, {
        level,
        assetId: asset.id,
        mimeType: asset.mime_type,
        sha256: asset.sha256!,
      })
      if (!result.ok) failure = result.reason
    })
    setNotice(failure)
    if (!failure) {
      setPendingAsset(null)
      setPickerOpen(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <FieldCard title="锚点图机制">
      <p className="text-xs leading-relaxed text-[var(--muted)]">
        只配「哪些图片代表哪个等级」。分数阈值在<b>等级刻度</b>、维度与权重在
        <b>调用 B 维度</b>、红线与硬伤封顶在<b>红线与人工复核</b>—— 这里不重复设置，
        这样换一张锚图后分数怎么变才归因得清。锚点图取自素材库，需要新图请先上传素材。
      </p>

      {intruders.length > 0 && (
        <div className="flex items-start gap-2 border border-alias-edge-046 bg-alias-surface-060 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-alias-content-041 rounded-md">
          <Warning className="mt-[2px] size-4 shrink-0" />
          <span>
            合同里的锚点机制块混入了不属于它的机制：{intruders.join("、")}。
            保存会被后端隔离守卫拒绝，请把这些设置移回各自的面板。
          </span>
        </div>
      )}

      <label className="visual-native-choice flex items-center gap-2">
        <input className="visual-native-checkbox"
          type="checkbox"
          checked={view.present && view.enabled}
          onChange={(e) =>
            onPatch((next) => {
              setAnchorMechanismEnabled(next.contract, e.target.checked)
            })
          }
        />
        <span className="font-semibold">启用锚点图机制</span>
        <span className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
          关闭即从合同移除整块；重新开启会新建空块，不依赖历史修订
        </span>
      </label>

      {view.present && view.enabled && (
        <>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4 text-[0.78rem]">
            <label className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="font-semibold">送图上限</span>
              <input
                type="number"
                min={1}
                max={MAX_ANCHOR_IMAGES_CEILING}
                className={numberClass}
                value={view.maxAnchorImages}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  const parsed = Number(e.target.value)
                  let failure: string | null = null
                  onPatch((next) => {
                    const result = setAnchorMechanismMaxImages(next.contract, parsed)
                    if (!result.ok) failure = result.reason
                  })
                  setNotice(failure)
                }}
              />
              <span className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                上限 {MAX_ANCHOR_IMAGES_CEILING}
              </span>
            </label>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="font-semibold">等级覆盖</span>
              {ANCHOR_LEVELS.map((level) => {
                const covered = view.levelsCovered.includes(level)
                return (
                  <span
                    key={level}
                    className={`inline-flex h-6 min-w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-md border px-1 text-xs ${
                      covered
                        ? "border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-alias-surface-013 font-semibold"
                        : "border-dashed border-[var(--line)] text-[var(--muted)]"
                    }`}
                    title={covered ? `${level} 已配锚点图` : `${level} 未配锚点图`}
                  >
                    {level}
                  </span>
                )
              })}
              <span className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                不要求五档齐全，可以先配两档开始调
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {view.enabled && view.anchors.length > 0 && (
            // 锚图是随请求一起发给模型的额外图片，调用B正文若不声明这件事，
            // 模型会把锚点当成待评图的一部分来评价——运营写正文时必踩的坑。
            <div className="border border-alias-edge-029 bg-alias-surface-026 px-3 py-2 text-xs leading-5 rounded-md">
              <span className="font-semibold">调用B正文需配套说明</span>
              ：启用后每次请求会发出{" "}
              <span className="font-data">{view.anchors.length + 1}</span> 张图——
              前 <span className="font-data">{view.anchors.length}</span> 张是锚点（按
              {" "}{view.anchors.map((a) => a.level).join("、")} 顺序），
              <span className="font-semibold">最后一张才是待评图</span>。
              调用B正文必须写明这一点并要求与锚点做相对比较，否则模型会把锚点当作待评图内容来评价；
              还应禁止把锚点图的内容写进待评图的证据里。锚点只改变参照系，不改变输出结构。
            </div>
          )}

          {notice && (
            <div className="border border-alias-edge-046 bg-alias-surface-060 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-alias-content-041 rounded-md">
              {notice}
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="space-y-2">
            {view.anchors.length === 0 && (
              <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                还没有锚点图。点下方「挑选锚点图」从素材库添加。
              </p>
            )}
            {view.anchors.map((anchor) => (
              <div
                key={anchor.assetId}
                className="grid gap-2 border border-[var(--line)] px-3 py-2 sm:grid-cols-[64px_90px_1fr_auto] sm:items-center rounded-md"
              >
                <HoverPreview src={`/api/assets/${anchor.assetId}/file`} alt={`锚点图 ${anchor.assetId}`}>
                  <span className="block h-12 w-16 rounded-md overflow-hidden">
                    <img
                      src={`/api/assets/${anchor.assetId}/file`}
                      alt={`锚点图 ${anchor.assetId}`}
                      className="h-12 w-16 border border-[var(--line)] bg-white object-contain"
                      loading="lazy"
                    />
                  </span>
                </HoverPreview>
                <select
                  className="visual-native-select w-full"
                  value={anchor.level}
                  onChange={(e) => {
                    const level = e.target.value as AnchorLevel
                    let failure: string | null = null
                    onPatch((next) => {
                      const result = upsertAnchorMechanismAnchor(next.contract, {
                        ...anchor,
                        level,
                      })
                      if (!result.ok) failure = result.reason
                    })
                    setNotice(failure)
                  }}
                >
                  {ANCHOR_LEVELS.map((level) => (
                    <option key={level} value={level}>
                      {level}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <input
                    className={inputClass}
                    placeholder="备注：这张图为什么代表该等级（可留空）"
                    value={anchor.note ?? ""}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      onPatch((next) => {
                        upsertAnchorMechanismAnchor(next.contract, {
                          ...anchor,
                          note: e.target.value,
                        })
                      })
                    }
                  />
                  <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                    素材 <span className="visual-code">#{anchor.assetId}</span> · {anchor.mimeType} ·{" "}
                    <span className="visual-code">{anchor.sha256.slice(0, 12)}</span>…
                  </p>
                </div>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  title="移除该锚点图"
                  onClick={() => {
                    onPatch((next) => {
                      removeAnchorMechanismAnchor(next.contract, anchor.assetId)
                    })
                    setNotice(null)
                  }}
                  className="visual-native-button size-11 shrink-0 p-0 text-alias-content-041 hover:bg-alias-surface-060 [&_svg]:size-4"
                >
                  <Trash />
                </button>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>

          {!pickerOpen ? (
            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="secondary"
              onClick={() => {
                setPickerOpen(true)
                setNotice(null)
              }}
            >
              挑选锚点图
            </Button>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-2 border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-alias-surface-051 px-3 py-3 rounded-md">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
                <input
                  className={inputClass}
                  placeholder="按素材名或 ID 搜索"
                  value={keyword}
                  onChange={(e) => setKeyword(e.target.value)}
                />
                <label className="visual-native-choice flex items-center gap-2">
                  <input className="visual-native-checkbox"
                    type="checkbox"
                    checked={onlyThisCategory}
                    onChange={(e) => setOnlyThisCategory(e.target.checked)}
                  />
                  只看本类目素材
                </label>
                <Button
                  type="button"
                  variant="ghost"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setPickerOpen(false)
                    setPendingAsset(null)
                  }}
                >
                  收起
                </Button>
              </div>

              {assets.isLoading && (
                <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">正在读素材库…</p>
              )}
              {assets.isError && (
                <p className="text-xs text-alias-content-041">
                  素材库读取失败，请重试或检查权限。
                </p>
              )}
              {!assets.isLoading && !assets.isError && candidates.length === 0 && (
                <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                  {trimmedKeyword
                    ? onlyThisCategory
                      ? `本类目内没有匹配「${trimmedKeyword}」的素材。该图若属于其它类目，请取消勾选「只看本类目素材」再搜。`
                      : `没有匹配「${trimmedKeyword}」的素材。可按素材名称或资产编号搜索。`
                    : "没有可选素材。已配为锚点图的素材不会重复出现。"}
                </p>
              )}

              <div className="grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4">
                {candidates.map((asset) => {
                  const reason = ineligibleReason(asset)
                  const selected = pendingAsset?.id === asset.id
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={asset.id}
                      type="button"
                      disabled={Boolean(reason)}
                      title={reason ?? `选择素材 #${asset.id}`}
                      onClick={() => setPendingAsset(asset)}
                      className={`visual-native-button items-stretch flex flex-col gap-1 px-2 py-2 text-left ${
                        reason
                          ? "cursor-not-allowed border-dashed border-[var(--line)] opacity-60"
                          : selected
                            ? "border-active-border bg-active-surface hover:bg-active-surface"
                            : "border-[var(--line)] bg-white hover:bg-alias-surface-047"
                      }`}
                    >
                      <HoverPreview src={asset.image_url ?? `/api/assets/${asset.id}/file`} alt={asset.name}>
                        <span className="block h-20 w-full rounded-md overflow-hidden">
                          <img
                            src={asset.image_url ?? `/api/assets/${asset.id}/file`}
                            alt={asset.name}
                            className="block h-20 w-full border border-[var(--line)] bg-white object-contain"
                            loading="lazy"
                          />
                        </span>
                      </HoverPreview>
                      <span className="truncate font-semibold" title={asset.name}>
                        {asset.name}
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-[var(--muted)]">
                        #{asset.id}
                        {reason ? ` · ${reason}` : ""}
                      </span>
                    </button>
                  )
                })}
              </div>

              {pendingAsset && (
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-2 text-xs">
                  <span className="font-semibold">
                    把「{pendingAsset.name}」设为哪个等级的锚点图：
                  </span>
                  {ANCHOR_LEVELS.map((level) => (
                    <Button
                      key={level}
                      type="button"
                      variant="secondary"
                      onClick={() => addAnchor(pendingAsset, level)}
                    >
                      {level}
                    </Button>
                  ))}
                  <Button
                    type="button"
                    variant="ghost"
                    onClick={() => setPendingAsset(null)}
                  >
                    取消
                  </Button>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </>
      )}
    </FieldCard>
  )
}
